```
from settings import *
import numpy as np,torch
from physics import thermodynamics,operating_state
from operational import interpolate_nodes

class SharedData:
    def __init__(self,split,spec,rows=None):
        assert split in ['train','val']
        ready=json.loads((DATA/'READY.json').read_text())
        assert ready['passed'] and ready['protocol']=='daily_08_BJT_latency15h'
        assert ready['regional_delay_minutes']==0
        assert spec['preprocess_fingerprint']==ready['preprocess_fingerprint'], 'Stale data/spec combination'
        self.split=split;self.spec=spec;self.root=DATA/split
        self.a={k:np.load(self.root/(k+'.npy'),mmap_mode='r') for k in ['h','y','origin','phase','cycle','offset','nodes','dynamics','near','rings']}
        self.rows=np.arange(len(self.a['h'])) if rows is None else np.asarray(rows)
        self.farms=self.a['h'].shape[1];self.n=len(self.rows)*self.farms
        stats=json.loads((ASSETS/'normalization.json').read_text())['sites']
        self.flat=np.array([stats[str(s)]['flat_threshold'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4')
        self.sm=np.array([stats[str(s)]['sel_mean'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4');self.ss=np.array([stats[str(s)]['sel_std'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4')
        self.rm=np.array([stats[str(s)]['ring_mean'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4');self.rs=np.array([stats[str(s)]['ring_std'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4')
        self.mu=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_mean.npy');self.sd=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_std.npy');self.elevation=np.load(ASSETS/'elevation.npy')
        self.rm_history=np.load(DATA/'regional_history'/f'{split}.npy',mmap_mode='r') if spec.get('rm') else None
        self.condition=spec.get('condition','neutral');self.reference=None
        if self.condition in ['cb','cbh','anchor','wrong_cb','wrong_cbh']:
            seed=spec['seed'];self.reference=np.load(DATA/f'reference_r{seed}/{split}.npy',mmap_mode='r')
            if self.condition.startswith('wrong'):
                self.donor=np.load(self.root/f'donor_r{seed}.npy');self.donor_base=np.load(DATA/f'reference_r{seed}/train.npy',mmap_mode='r');self.donor_h=np.load(DATA/'train/h.npy',mmap_mode='r')
    def batch(self,indices,device='cpu'):
        idx=np.asarray(indices);ti=self.rows[idx//self.farms];si=idx%self.farms
        h=np.asarray(self.a['h'][ti,si]);y=np.asarray(self.a['y'][ti,si]);b=dict(h=h,phase=np.asarray(self.a['phase'][ti]),site=si)
        if self.spec['family']!='B0':
            cy=self.a['cycle'][ti];offset=self.a['offset'][ti]
            weather=interpolate_nodes(self.a['nodes'][cy,si],offset,np.arange(1,25)/4).astype('f4')
            dyn=interpolate_nodes(self.a['dynamics'][cy,si],offset,np.arange(1,25)/4).astype('f4')
            p=weather*self.sd+self.mu
            den,stab=thermodynamics(p,self.elevation[si,None]);state=operating_state(h,self.flat[si,None])
            x=np.concatenate([den,dyn,stab,np.broadcast_to(state[:,None],(len(h),24,7))],-1)
            b.update(weather=weather,physical=p,x=((x-self.sm[si,None])/self.ss[si,None]).astype('f4'),region=((self.a['rings'][ti,si]-self.rm[si])/self.rs[si]).astype('f4'),near=np.asarray(self.a['near'][ti,si]))
        if self.rm_history is not None:b['rm_history']=np.asarray(self.rm_history[ti,si])
        if self.reference is not None:
            base=np.asarray(self.reference[ti,si]);ch=h
            if self.condition.startswith('wrong'):
                donor=self.donor[ti];base=np.asarray(self.donor_base[donor,si]);ch=np.asarray(self.donor_h[donor,si])
            b.update(base=base,base_delta=base-np.concatenate([ch[:,-1:],base[:,:-1]],-1),condition_h=ch)
        inputs={k:torch.as_tensor(np.array(v),dtype=torch.long if k in ['site','phase'] else torch.float32,device=device) for k,v in b.items()}
        return inputs,torch.as_tensor(np.array(y),device=device),si,ti
```

```
"""Compact implementations of the requested forecasting baselines.

Every invocation is a single station and a single random seed.  The classes are
deliberately kept dependency free so that the experiment is reproducible on the
existing environment.  The names follow the published model families: the
Transformer/TFT/TimeXer versions consume known future weather covariates, the
_*_H versions consume only the 24 observed power points, and the *_W versions
consume only the issued weather trajectory.
"""
import math
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

FAMILIES = [
    "TRANSFORMER_FC", "TFT_FC", "TIMEXER_FC",
    "TIDE_FC", "DLINEAR_FC", "NBEATSX_FC", "TCN_FC",
    "LSTM_H", "GRU_H", "PATCHTST_H", "ITRANSFORMER_H", "MODERNTCN_H",
    "CNN_W", "VIT_W", "SWIN_W",
]


class PositionalEncoding(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d, max_len=64):
        super().__init__()
        pos = torch.arange(max_len, dtype=torch.float32)[:, None]
        div = torch.exp(torch.arange(0, d, 2, dtype=torch.float32) * (-math.log(10000.0) / d))
        pe = torch.zeros(max_len, d)
        pe[:, 0::2] = torch.sin(pos * div)
        pe[:, 1::2] = torch.cos(pos * div[:pe[:, 1::2].shape[1]])
        self.register_buffer("pe", pe[None], persistent=False)

    def forward(self, x):
        return x + self.pe[:, :x.shape[1]].to(dtype=x.dtype, device=x.device)


class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, inp, hidden, out):
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(inp, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(hidden, out))

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.net(x)


def _encoder(d, layers=2, heads=4):
    layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d, heads, 2 * d, dropout=0.0,
                                       batch_first=True, norm_first=True, activation="gelu")
    return nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, layers)


def _interpolate_weather(nodes, times):
    """Linear interpolation from seven hourly Pangu nodes to 24 quarter hours."""
    b, n, c = nodes.shape
    q = torch.arange(1, 25, device=nodes.device, dtype=nodes.dtype)[None] / 4.0
    q = q.expand(b, -1)
    # searchsorted accepts a batch of sorted rows on current torch versions.
    right = torch.searchsorted(times.contiguous(), q.contiguous()).clamp(1, n - 1)
    left = right - 1
    gather_l = left[..., None].expand(-1, -1, c)
    gather_r = right[..., None].expand(-1, -1, c)
    t0 = times.gather(1, left)
    t1 = times.gather(1, right)
    alpha = ((q - t0) / (t1 - t0).clamp_min(1e-6))[..., None]
    l = nodes.gather(1, gather_l)
    r = nodes.gather(1, gather_r)
    return l + alpha * (r - l)


class HistoryLSTM(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64, cell="lstm"):
        super().__init__(); self.cell = cell
        self.inp = nn.Linear(2, d)
        R = nn.LSTM if cell == "lstm" else nn.GRU
        self.rnn = R(d, d, num_layers=2, batch_first=True, dropout=0.0)
        self.head = MLP(d + 1, d, 24)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        clock = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None, :, None].expand(h.shape[0], -1, -1)
        z, _ = self.rnn(self.inp(torch.cat([h[..., None], clock], -1)))
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(torch.cat([z[:, -1], h[:, -1, None]], -1))


class PatchTSTHistory(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64, patch=4):
        super().__init__(); self.patch = patch
        self.embed = nn.Linear(patch + 2, d); self.pe = PositionalEncoding(d, 16)
        self.enc = _encoder(d, 3); self.head = MLP(d, d, 24)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; x = h.reshape(b, 6, 4)
        phasef = (phase.float() / 3.0)[:, None, None].expand(-1, 6, 1)
        p = torch.linspace(0, 1, 6, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None, :, None].expand(b, -1, -1)
        z = self.pe(self.embed(torch.cat([x, phasef, p], -1)))
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(self.enc(z).mean(1))


class ITransformerHistory(nn.Module):
    """Inverted Transformer: the one observed power variate is a token."""
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.embed = nn.Linear(24 + 1, d); self.enc = _encoder(d, 2)
        self.head = MLP(d, d, 24)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        z = self.embed(torch.cat([h, phase.float()[:, None] / 3.0], -1))[:, None]
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(self.enc(z)[:, 0])


class ModernTCNHistory(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.inp = nn.Conv1d(2, d, 1)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Conv1d(d, d, 5, padding=2, groups=d), nn.Conv1d(d, d, 1), nn.GELU(),
                                                   nn.Conv1d(d, d, 3, padding=2, dilation=2, groups=d), nn.Conv1d(d, d, 1), nn.GELU()) for _ in range(3)])
        self.norm = nn.BatchNorm1d(d); self.head = MLP(d + 1, d, 24)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        clock = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(h.shape[0], -1)
        z = self.inp(torch.stack([h, clock], 1))
        for block in self.blocks: z = z + block(z)
        z = self.norm(z).mean(-1)
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(torch.cat([z, h[:, -1, None]], -1))


class TransformerFutureCov(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.h_in = nn.Linear(2, d); self.w_in = nn.Linear(14 + 4, d)
        self.pe = PositionalEncoding(d, 64); self.h_enc = _encoder(d, 2)
        dec_layer = nn.TransformerDecoderLayer(d, 4, 2 * d, dropout=0.0, batch_first=True, norm_first=True, activation="gelu")
        self.dec = nn.TransformerDecoder(dec_layer, 2); self.head = MLP(d + 1, d, 1)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; hist_clock = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None, :, None].expand(b, -1, -1)
        mem = self.h_enc(self.pe(self.h_in(torch.cat([h[..., None], hist_clock], -1))))
        lead = torch.arange(1, 25, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1) / 24
        feat = torch.stack([lead, torch.sin(2 * math.pi * lead), torch.cos(2 * math.pi * lead), phase[:, None].float() / 3.0 + lead * 0], -1)
        q = self.pe(self.w_in(torch.cat([weather, feat], -1)))
        out = self.dec(q, mem)
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(torch.cat([out, h[:, -1, None, None].expand(-1, 24, -1)], -1)).squeeze(-1)


class TFFutureCov(nn.Module):
    """TFT-style variable selection, gated recurrent history and attention."""
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.hvars = nn.ModuleList([nn.Linear(1, d) for _ in range(2)])
        self.wvars = nn.ModuleList([nn.Linear(1, d) for _ in range(14)])
        self.selector = nn.Linear(14 * d, 14); self.h_rnn = nn.LSTM(d, d, 2, batch_first=True)
        self.w_rnn = nn.LSTM(d, d, 2, batch_first=True)
        self.gate = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2 * d, d), nn.Sigmoid()); self.head = MLP(d + 1, d, 1)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; clock = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1)
        hh = 0.5 * (self.hvars[0](h[..., None]) + self.hvars[1](clock[..., None]))
        hm, _ = self.h_rnn(hh); tokens = torch.stack([m(weather[..., i:i+1]) for i, m in enumerate(self.wvars)], 2)
        weights = torch.softmax(self.selector(tokens.flatten(-2)), -1)[..., None]
        ww = (tokens * weights).sum(2); wm, _ = self.w_rnn(ww)
        gate = self.gate(torch.cat([hm[:, -1:, :].expand(-1, 24, -1), wm], -1)); z = gate * wm + (1 - gate) * hm[:, -1:, :]
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(torch.cat([z, h[:, -1, None, None].expand(-1, 24, -1)], -1)).squeeze(-1)


class TimeXerFutureCov(nn.Module):
    """TimeXer-style endogenous history token and exogenous weather patches."""
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.h_patch = nn.Linear(4, d); self.w_patch = nn.Linear(14 * 4, d)
        self.pe = PositionalEncoding(d, 16); self.exo = _encoder(d, 2)
        self.cross = nn.MultiheadAttention(d, 4, batch_first=True); self.head = MLP(d + 1, d, 1)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; hp = self.pe(self.h_patch(h.reshape(b, 6, 4))); wp = self.pe(self.w_patch(weather.reshape(b, 6, 14 * 4)))
        ex = self.exo(wp); q = hp.mean(1, keepdim=True).expand(-1, 24, -1)
        z, _ = self.cross(q, ex, ex); z = z + torch.nn.functional.interpolate(ex.transpose(1, 2), 24, mode="linear", align_corners=False).transpose(1, 2)
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.head(torch.cat([z, h[:, -1, None, None].expand(-1, 24, -1)], -1)).squeeze(-1)


class TiDEFutureCov(nn.Module):
    """TiDE-style MLP encoder/decoder for long-horizon known covariates.

    The past target trajectory and future covariate trajectory are encoded into
    a fixed representation, passed through residual dense blocks, and decoded
    to all 24 forecast leads.  This is the dependency-free equivalent used for
    the controlled baseline, with no autoregressive access to future targets.
    """
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.past = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(24 + 24, d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d, d))
        self.future = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(24 * (14 + 4), 2*d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(2*d, 2*d))
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(3*d, 3*d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(3*d, 3*d)) for _ in range(3)])
        self.decoder = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(3*d, 2*d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(2*d, 24))
        self.skip = nn.Linear(24, 24)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; clock = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1)
        lead = torch.arange(1, 25, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1) / 24
        feat = torch.stack([lead, torch.sin(2*math.pi*lead), torch.cos(2*math.pi*lead), phase[:, None].float().expand(-1, 24)/3.], -1)
        p = self.past(torch.cat([h, clock], -1)); f = self.future(torch.cat([weather, feat], -1).flatten(1)); z = torch.cat([p, f], -1)
        for block in self.blocks: z = z + block(z)
        return h[:, -1, None] + self.skip(h - h.mean(-1, keepdim=True)) + self.decoder(z)


class DLinearFutureCov(nn.Module):
    """DLinear-style trend/level linear baseline plus future-weather regression."""
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.level = nn.Linear(24, 24); self.trend = nn.Linear(24, 24)
        self.weather = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14 + 4, d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d, 1))

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; t = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1)
        lead = torch.arange(1, 25, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1) / 24
        feat = torch.stack([lead, torch.sin(2*math.pi*lead), torch.cos(2*math.pi*lead), phase[:, None].float().expand(-1, 24)/3.], -1)
        centered = h - h.mean(-1, keepdim=True); w = self.weather(torch.cat([weather, feat], -1)).squeeze(-1)
        return h.mean(-1, keepdim=True) + self.level(centered) + self.trend(t) + w


class NBeatsXFutureCov(nn.Module):
    """Small N-BEATSx-style residual/backcast blocks with exogenous weather."""
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.inp = nn.Linear(24 + 24*14, d)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d, 2*d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(2*d, 24+24)) for _ in range(4)])
        self.updates = nn.ModuleList([nn.Linear(24, d) for _ in range(4)])
        self.exo = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14+4, d), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d, 1))

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = h.shape[0]; lead = torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b,-1)/24
        feat = torch.stack([lead,torch.sin(2*math.pi*lead),torch.cos(2*math.pi*lead),phase[:,None].float().expand(-1,24)/3.],-1)
        z=self.inp(torch.cat([h,weather.flatten(1)],-1)); forecast=torch.zeros(b,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)
        residual=h
        for block, update in zip(self.blocks, self.updates):
            out=block(z); residual=residual-out[:,:24]; forecast=forecast+out[:,24:]; z=z+0.05*torch.tanh(update(out[:,:24]))
        return h[:,-1,None]+forecast+self.exo(torch.cat([weather,feat],-1)).squeeze(-1)


class TCNFutureCov(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.inp=nn.Conv1d(14+2,d,1); self.blocks=nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Conv1d(d,d,3,padding=1,dilation=1,groups=d),nn.Conv1d(d,d,1),nn.GELU(),nn.Conv1d(d,d,3,padding=2,dilation=2,groups=d),nn.Conv1d(d,d,1),nn.GELU()) for _ in range(3)]); self.head=nn.Conv1d(d,1,1)
    def forward(self,h,weather,phase):
        b=h.shape[0]; lead=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b,-1)/24; ph=phase[:,None].float().expand(-1,24)/3.; z=torch.cat([weather,h[:,-1,None,None].expand(-1,24,1),ph[...,None]],-1).transpose(1,2); z=self.inp(z)
        for block in self.blocks:z=z+block(z)
        return h[:,-1,None]+self.head(z).squeeze(1)


class CNNWeather(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.inp = nn.Conv1d(14, d, 3, padding=1); self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Conv1d(d, d, 5, padding=2), nn.GELU(), nn.Conv1d(d, d, 3, padding=2, dilation=2), nn.GELU(), nn.Conv1d(d, d, 3, padding=4, dilation=4), nn.GELU())
        self.head = nn.Conv1d(d, 1, 1)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase): return self.head(self.net(self.inp(weather.transpose(1, 2)))).squeeze(1)


class ViTWeather(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.inp = nn.Linear(14 + 2, d); self.pe = PositionalEncoding(d, 32); self.enc = _encoder(d, 3); self.head = nn.Linear(d, 1)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = weather.shape[0]; lead = torch.arange(1, 25, device=weather.device, dtype=weather.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1) / 24
        z = self.pe(self.inp(torch.cat([weather, lead[..., None], phase[:, None, None].float().expand(-1, 24, 1) / 3], -1)))
        return self.head(self.enc(z)).squeeze(-1)


class SwinWeather(nn.Module):
    """1-D Swin-style alternating local windows and shifted windows."""
    def __init__(self, d=64):
        super().__init__(); self.inp = nn.Linear(14 + 2, d); self.pe = PositionalEncoding(d, 32)
        self.local = nn.ModuleList([nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d, 4, 2*d, dropout=0., batch_first=True, norm_first=True, activation="gelu") for _ in range(3)])
        self.head = nn.Linear(d, 1)

    def forward(self, h, weather, phase):
        b = weather.shape[0]; lead = torch.arange(1, 25, device=weather.device, dtype=weather.dtype)[None].expand(b, -1) / 24
        z = self.pe(self.inp(torch.cat([weather, lead[..., None], phase[:, None, None].float().expand(-1, 24, 1) / 3], -1)))
        for i, layer in enumerate(self.local):
            if i % 2 == 0:
                z = layer(z)
            else:
                shift = 2; zs = torch.roll(z, shift, 1); zs = layer(zs); z = torch.roll(zs, -shift, 1)
        return self.head(z).squeeze(-1)


def build_model(family, d=64):
    if family == "LSTM_H": return HistoryLSTM(d, "lstm")
    if family == "GRU_H": return HistoryLSTM(d, "gru")
    if family == "PATCHTST_H": return PatchTSTHistory(d)
    if family == "ITRANSFORMER_H": return ITransformerHistory(d)
    if family == "MODERNTCN_H": return ModernTCNHistory(d)
    if family == "TRANSFORMER_FC": return TransformerFutureCov(d)
    if family == "TFT_FC": return TFFutureCov(d)
    if family == "TIMEXER_FC": return TimeXerFutureCov(d)
    if family == "TIDE_FC": return TiDEFutureCov(d)
    if family == "DLINEAR_FC": return DLinearFutureCov(d)
    if family == "NBEATSX_FC": return NBeatsXFutureCov(d)
    if family == "TCN_FC": return TCNFutureCov(d)
    if family == "CNN_W": return CNNWeather(d)
    if family == "VIT_W": return ViTWeather(d)
    if family == "SWIN_W": return SwinWeather(d)
    raise ValueError(family)


def input_group(family):
    if family.endswith("_H"): return "history_only"
    if family.endswith("_FC"): return "future_covariate"
    return "weather_only"
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/code/legacy/column_original.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/legacy/column_original.py

SHA-256: 60be06d445f8e6ea5374605cc08b595c43184ea01957db98533372f3826582d6

```
"""Direct weather-projection replacements for the frozen P0I/CBH backbone."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from wind_forcing.models.p0i_transfer import P0IConditionTransfer

CHANNELS = ("msl", "u10", "v10", "t2m", "z1000", "z925", "q1000", "q925",
            "t1000", "t925", "u1000", "u925", "v1000", "v925")
G0 = 9.80665
KAPPA = 0.2854


def column_coordinates(physical: torch.Tensor, elevation: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Invertible 14-weather-plus-height representation, ordered in three groups."""
    p = physical
    elev = elevation
    theta1000 = p[..., 8]
    theta925 = p[..., 9] * (1000.0 / 925.0) ** KAPPA
    return torch.stack((
        p[..., 0], p[..., 1], p[..., 2], p[..., 3], elev,
        p[..., 10] - p[..., 1], p[..., 12] - p[..., 2], theta1000, p[..., 6],
        p[..., 4] / G0 - elev,
        p[..., 11] - p[..., 10], p[..., 13] - p[..., 12], theta925 - theta1000,
        p[..., 7] - p[..., 6], (p[..., 5] - p[..., 4]) / G0,
    ), dim=-1)


def invert_column_coordinates(values: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    """Used by the numeric information-preservation test."""
    v = values
    elev = v[..., 4]
    u1000, v1000 = v[..., 1] + v[..., 5], v[..., 2] + v[..., 6]
    theta1000 = v[..., 7]
    z1000 = (elev + v[..., 9]) * G0
    p = torch.stack((
        v[..., 0], v[..., 1], v[..., 2], v[..., 3], z1000,
        z1000 + v[..., 14] * G0, v[..., 8], v[..., 8] + v[..., 13],
        theta1000, (theta1000 + v[..., 12]) / ((1000.0 / 925.0) ** KAPPA),
        u1000, u1000 + v[..., 10], v1000, v1000 + v[..., 11],
    ), dim=-1)
    return p, elev


class WeatherProjection(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, variant: str, static: dict, dem_path: str, stats_path: str, width: int):
        super().__init__()
        if variant not in {"E1", "E2L", "E2N", "E2", "E2C", "E3"}:
            raise ValueError(variant)
        if tuple(map(str, static["nwp_channel_name"])) != CHANNELS:
            raise ValueError("Unexpected NWP channel ordering")
        nwp_mean = np.asarray(static["nwp_mean"], dtype=float)
        if not (8e4 < nwp_mean[0] < 1.2e5 and 180 < nwp_mean[3] < 330
                and 180 < nwp_mean[8] < 330 and 180 < nwp_mean[9] < 330
                and 0 < nwp_mean[6] < .1 and 0 < nwp_mean[7] < .1
                and 0 < nwp_mean[4] < nwp_mean[5] < 2e4):
            raise ValueError("Weather physical-unit sanity check failed")
        dem = json.loads(Path(dem_path).read_text())
        records = dem["records"]
        ids = list(map(str, static["station_id"]))
        if len(records) != len(ids) or [r["station_id"] for r in records] != ids:
            raise ValueError("DEM station ordering differs from training cache")
        heights = np.array([r["terrain_elevation_m"] for r in records], dtype=np.float32)
        if not np.isfinite(heights).all():
            raise ValueError("DEM contains nonfinite heights")
        stats = json.loads(Path(stats_path).read_text())
        if stats["station_ids"] != ids or stats["weather_channels"] != list(CHANNELS):
            raise ValueError("Feature statistics do not match cache")
        self.variant = variant
        self.register_buffer("elevation", torch.as_tensor(heights).reshape(1, -1, 1))
        self.register_buffer("nwp_mean", torch.as_tensor(static["nwp_mean"], dtype=torch.float32))
        self.register_buffer("nwp_std", torch.as_tensor(static["nwp_std"], dtype=torch.float32))
        self.register_buffer("column_mean", torch.as_tensor(stats["mean"], dtype=torch.float32))
        self.register_buffer("column_std", torch.as_tensor(stats["std"], dtype=torch.float32))
        self.register_buffer("elevation_mean", torch.tensor(stats["mean"][4], dtype=torch.float32))
        self.register_buffer("elevation_std", torch.tensor(stats["std"][4], dtype=torch.float32))
        if variant == "E1":
            self.network = nn.Linear(15, width)
        elif variant == "E2L":
            # Exact depth/parameter-count control for E2N.  With no
            # intervening activation, the three affine maps compose into one
            # affine weather projection despite the overparameterization.
            self.network = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14, width), nn.Linear(width, width),
                                         nn.Linear(width, width))
        elif variant == "E2N":
            # Capacity-matched nonlinear control with exactly the original
            # 14 normalized weather channels.  This arm changes only the
            # linear-vs-MLP encoder factor relative to E0; it deliberately
            # does not expose terrain elevation to the model.
            self.network = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14, width), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(width, width),
                                         nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(width, width))
        elif variant in {"E2", "E2C"}:
            self.network = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(15, width), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(width, width),
                                         nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(width, width))
        else:
            if width != 64:
                raise ValueError("E3 group widths are specified for d_model=64")
            self.surface = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(5, 16), nn.GELU())
            self.lower = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(5, 24), nn.GELU())
            self.upper = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(5, 24), nn.GELU())
            self.network = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(64, 64), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(64, 64))

    def forward(self, weather: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        if weather.ndim != 4 or weather.shape[-1] != 14 or weather.shape[1] != self.elevation.shape[1]:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected [B,N,T,14], received {tuple(weather.shape)}")
        elev = self.elevation.to(weather).expand(weather.shape[:3])
        if self.variant in {"E2L", "E2N"}:
            return self.network(weather)
        if self.variant in {"E1", "E2"}:
            value = torch.cat((weather, ((elev - self.elevation_mean) / self.elevation_std)[..., None]), -1)
            return self.network(value)
        physical = weather.float() * self.nwp_std + self.nwp_mean
        column = column_coordinates(physical, elev.float())
        value = ((column - self.column_mean) / self.column_std).to(weather)
        if self.variant == "E2C":
            return self.network(value)
        merged = torch.cat((self.surface(value[..., :5]), self.lower(value[..., 5:10]),
                            self.upper(value[..., 10:])), -1)
        return self.network(merged)


class ColumnWeatherCBH(P0IConditionTransfer):
    def __init__(self, cfg: dict, static: dict, forecast_steps: int = 24):
        super().__init__(cfg, static, forecast_steps)
        self.native_projection = WeatherProjection(
            cfg["weather_encoder_variant"], static, cfg["station_dem_path"],
            cfg["column_stats_path"], int(cfg["d_model"]),
        )


def install_builder() -> None:
    """Experiment-local registration; does not edit the frozen source snapshot."""
    import wind_forcing.models as models

    original = models.build_forecast_model

    def build(cfg, static, *, history_steps=24, forecast_steps=24):
        if cfg.get("weather_encoder_variant") in {"E1", "E2L", "E2N", "E2", "E2C", "E3"}:
            return ColumnWeatherCBH(cfg, static, forecast_steps)
        return original(cfg, static, history_steps=history_steps, forecast_steps=forecast_steps)

    models.build_forecast_model = build
```

```
def atomic_json(path, value):
    path = Path(path); path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temp = path.with_name(path.name + f'.{os.getpid()}.tmp')
    temp.write_text(json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False)+'\n')
    os.replace(temp, path)

def sha(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, 'rb') as f:
        for b in iter(lambda:f.read(1<<20), b''): h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()

def static():
    return {p.stem:np.load(p, allow_pickle=False) for p in (CACHE/'static').glob('*.npy')}

def seed_all(seed):
    random.seed(seed); np.random.seed(seed); torch.manual_seed(seed)
    if torch.cuda.is_available(): torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)

def configure_torch():
    torch.set_num_threads(1); torch.set_num_interop_threads(1)
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

def config_for(family, index):
    cfg = dict(CONFIGS[index])
    if family == 'DLINEAR_FC':
        cfg.update(width=64, lr=(2e-4, 1e-3)[index % 2], weight_decay=(1e-4, 1e-2)[index//2])
    return cfg

def ref_id(site, seed): return f'reference_B0_s{site:02d}_r{seed}'

def source_hashes():
    return {str(p.relative_to(RUN)):sha(p) for p in sorted((RUN/'code').rglob('*.py'))}
```

```
"""Single-farm models. Published families are explicitly labeled adaptations."""
from common import *
import copy
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
import reference_original as ref
import baselines_original as old
from column_original import WeatherProjection
from wind_forcing.models.p0i_transfer import P0IConditionTransfer

def baseline(site, seed):
    return ref.build_baseline(site,seed,static())

class CachedCorrection(P0IConditionTransfer):
    """Exact point-alignment CBH graph with precomputed frozen B0 and weather.

    The singleton attention softmax is identically one; only its v/out maps
    affect the result. Inactive source modules stay in checkpoints for audit.
    """
    def forward(self,h,weather,phase,base):
        b=h.shape[0]; leads=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(b,-1)/4
        time=torch.stack((leads/6,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*leads/24),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*leads/24)),-1)
        context=torch.cat((time,self.site[0][None,None].expand(b,24,-1),phase[:,None,None].to(h).expand(-1,24,-1)/3),-1)
        ht=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)/24
        memory=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(torch.stack((h,ht.expand_as(h)),-1)))
        tokens=self.native_projection(weather[:,None])[:,0]
        w=self.align_out(self.align_v(tokens))
        summary=memory.mean(1)[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1)
        delta=base-torch.cat((h[:,-1:],base[:,:-1]),-1)
        conditioned=base
        if self.condition_mode in ('baseline','neutral'): summary=torch.zeros_like(summary)
        if self.condition_mode in ('history','neutral'):
            conditioned=torch.zeros_like(base);delta=torch.zeros_like(delta)
        raw=torch.cat((summary,conditioned[...,None],delta[...,None],leads[...,None]/6),-1)
        cond=self.condition_projection(raw)+self.condition_bias[:,0]
        q=self.context_projection(context)+w+self.transfer_adapter(torch.cat((w,cond),-1))
        correction=self.readout(self.decoder(q,memory)).squeeze(-1)
        anchor=base
        if self.output_mode=='direct': anchor=torch.zeros_like(base)
        elif self.output_mode=='last': anchor=h[:,-1:]
        return anchor+correction

def correction(site,seed,width,arm,state,fast=True):
    s=static(); seed_all(seed); cfg=copy.deepcopy(ref.CBH_CONFIG);cfg['d_model']=width
    cfg['condition_mode']={'CB':'baseline','CH':'history','CNEUTRAL':'neutral','NEUTRAL_LAST':'neutral'}.get(arm,'full')
    cls=CachedCorrection if fast else P0IConditionTransfer
    m=cls(cfg,ref.single_site_static(s,site),24)
    m.load_history_checkpoint(dict(model_config=ref.BASELINE_CONFIG,model_state=state))
    q,sv=ref.canonical_static_vectors(s)
    with torch.no_grad():
        m.site.copy_(sv[site:site+1]);m.baseline_model.forcing_encoder.station_query.station_static.copy_(q[site:site+1])
        m.indices.zero_();m.weights.zero_();m.weights[...,0]=1
    if arm!='E0':
        # Validate global ordering, then slice only the fixed elevation buffer.
        proj=WeatherProjection('E2C',s,str(COLUMN/'station_dem_srtm30m.json'),str(COLUMN/'column_train_stats.json'),width)
        proj.elevation=proj.elevation[:,site:site+1].clone();m.native_projection=proj
    m.output_mode={'DIRECT':'direct','NEUTRAL_LAST':'last'}.get(arm,'baseline')
    return m

class DLinearFC(nn.Module):
    """Moving-average decomposition + linear future-exogenous regression."""
    def __init__(self,d=64):
        super().__init__();self.trend=nn.Linear(24,24);self.season=nn.Linear(24,24);self.weather=nn.Linear(24*14+1,24)
    def forward(self,h,w,p):
        tr=F.avg_pool1d(F.pad(h[:,None],(2,2),mode='replicate'),5,stride=1)[:,0]
        return self.trend(tr)+self.season(h-tr)+self.weather(torch.cat((w.flatten(1),p[:,None].float()/3),-1))

class NBeatsXFC(nn.Module):
    """Generic N-BEATSx adaptation with actual residual backcast chaining."""
    def __init__(self,d=64):
        super().__init__();self.blocks=nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(24+24*14+1,d),nn.ReLU(),nn.Linear(d,d),nn.ReLU(),nn.Linear(d,d),nn.ReLU(),nn.Linear(d,48)) for _ in range(4)])
    def forward(self,h,w,p):
        level=h[:,-1:];r=h-level;f=torch.zeros_like(h); ex=torch.cat((w.flatten(1),p[:,None].float()/3),-1)
        for block in self.blocks:
            o=block(torch.cat((r,ex),-1));r=r-o[:,:24];f=f+o[:,24:]
        return level+f

class TCNFC(nn.Module):
    """Temporal residual convs over all observed history + issued future NWP."""
    def __init__(self,d=64):
        super().__init__();self.h=nn.Sequential(nn.Conv1d(2,d,3,padding=1),nn.GELU(),nn.Conv1d(d,d,3,padding=2,dilation=2),nn.GELU(),nn.Flatten(),nn.Linear(24*d,d))
        self.inp=nn.Conv1d(14+d+2,d,1)
        self.blocks=nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Conv1d(d,d,3,padding=2**i,dilation=2**i),nn.GELU(),nn.Conv1d(d,d,1)) for i in range(4)])
        self.head=nn.Conv1d(d,1,1)
    def forward(self,h,w,p):
        clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device).expand_as(h)
        hs=self.h(torch.stack((h,clock),1))[:,:,None].expand(-1,-1,24)
        lead=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device).float().expand_as(h)/24
        z=self.inp(torch.cat((w.transpose(1,2),hs,lead[:,None],p[:,None,None].float().expand(-1,1,24)/3),1))
        for block in self.blocks:z=z+block(z)
        return h[:,-1:]+self.head(z)[:,0]

class TimeXerFC(nn.Module):
    """Univariate TimeXer adaptation: endogenous patches/global token,
    exogenous variate tokens, alternating endogenous self/global cross attention.
    Future weather is known at forecast issuance; no future power input.
    """
    def __init__(self,d=64):
        super().__init__();self.patch=nn.Linear(4,d);self.pe=old.PositionalEncoding(d,16)
        self.global_token=nn.Parameter(torch.randn(1,1,d)*.02);self.exo=nn.Linear(24,d)
        self.layers=nn.ModuleList([nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d,4,2*d,dropout=0,batch_first=True,norm_first=True,activation='gelu') for _ in range(2)])
        self.cross=nn.ModuleList([nn.MultiheadAttention(d,4,batch_first=True) for _ in range(2)])
        self.norm=nn.ModuleList([nn.LayerNorm(d) for _ in range(2)]);self.head=nn.Linear(7*d,24)
    def forward(self,h,w,p):
        center=h.mean(-1,keepdim=True);scale=h.std(-1,keepdim=True,unbiased=False).clamp_min(1e-3)
        z=self.pe(self.patch(((h-center)/scale).reshape(-1,6,4)))
        z=torch.cat((z,self.global_token.expand(len(h),-1,-1)),1);ex=self.exo(w.transpose(1,2))
        for layer,cross,norm in zip(self.layers,self.cross,self.norm):
            z=layer(z);g=z[:,-1:];a,_=cross(g,ex,ex,need_weights=False);z=torch.cat((z[:,:-1],norm(g+a)),1)
        return self.head(z.flatten(1))*scale+center

class TFTFC(nn.Module):
    """TFT family adaptation with selection, recurrent context, causal attention,
    gated residual paths; point-MAE objective replaces quantile heads.
    """
    def __init__(self,d=64):
        super().__init__();self.hp=nn.Linear(2,d);self.wp=nn.Parameter(torch.randn(14,d)*.02);self.wb=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(14,d))
        self.select=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14,d),nn.ELU(),nn.Linear(d,14))
        self.hr=nn.LSTM(d,d,2,batch_first=True);self.wr=nn.LSTM(d,d,2,batch_first=True)
        self.attn=nn.MultiheadAttention(d,4,batch_first=True)
        self.gate=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.GLU());self.norm=nn.LayerNorm(d)
        self.ff=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.ELU(),nn.Linear(2*d,d));self.gate2=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.GLU());self.norm2=nn.LayerNorm(d)
        self.head=nn.Linear(d,1)
    def forward(self,h,w,p):
        clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device).expand_as(h)
        mem,state=self.hr(self.hp(torch.stack((h,clock),-1)))
        tokens=w[...,None]*self.wp+self.wb;weight=self.select(w).softmax(-1)
        z,_=self.wr((tokens*weight[...,None]).sum(2),state)
        full=torch.cat((mem,z),1);mask=torch.arange(48,device=h.device)[None,:]>(24+torch.arange(24,device=h.device)[:,None])
        a,_=self.attn(z,full,full,attn_mask=mask,need_weights=False)
        z=self.norm(z+self.gate(a));z=self.norm2(z+self.gate2(self.ff(z)))
        return h[:,-1:]+self.head(z).squeeze(-1)

class PatchTSTH(nn.Module):
    """Univariate patch transformer with reversible instance normalization."""
    def __init__(self,d=64):
        super().__init__();self.embed=nn.Linear(4,d);self.pe=old.PositionalEncoding(d,16);self.enc=old._encoder(d,3);self.head=nn.Linear(6*d,24)
    def forward(self,h,w,p):
        mu=h.mean(-1,keepdim=True);sd=h.std(-1,keepdim=True,unbiased=False).clamp_min(1e-3)
        z=self.enc(self.pe(self.embed(((h-mu)/sd).reshape(-1,6,4))))
        return self.head(z.flatten(1))*sd+mu

def named(family,width):
    constructors={'TRANSFORMER_FC':old.TransformerFutureCov,'TIDE_FC':old.TiDEFutureCov,
                  'NBEATSX_FC':NBeatsXFC,'TIMEXER_FC':TimeXerFC,'TFT_FC':TFTFC,'TCN_FC':TCNFC,
                  'DLINEAR_FC':DLinearFC,'GRU_H':lambda d:old.HistoryLSTM(d,'gru'),
                  'PATCHTST_H':PatchTSTH,'CNN_W':old.CNNWeather}
    return constructors[family](width)
```

```
"""Single-site model construction and state utilities for the CBH audit.

The learned parameters of every model created here belong to exactly one site
and one seed.  Global static statistics are retained as fixed covariates so the
shared/independent comparison does not silently change feature definitions.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import hashlib
import json
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.func import functional_call, stack_module_state, vmap


ROOT = Path("/storage/liying/new_try/wind_cluster_forced_evolution")
SOURCE = ROOT / "artifacts/p0i_attribution_closure_20260915_v1/snapshot/src"
CACHE = ROOT / "data/model_cache_pad5_f8"
OUT = ROOT / "artifacts/cbh_per_station_independent_20260918_v1"
SEEDS = tuple(range(42, 52))


BASELINE_CONFIG: dict[str, Any] = {
    "architecture": "forced_evolution",
    "weather_channels": 14,
    "weather_hidden_channels": 48,
    "d_model": 64,
    "station_query_mode": "learned_cells",
    "station_neighborhood_cells": 3,
    "include_wind_geometry": True,
    "include_region_context": False,
    "forcing_path_mode": "difference",
    "time_query_mode": "full_attention",
    "fusion_mode": "history_only",
    "output_level": "hierarchical",
    "history_tcn_layers": 3,
    "trajectory_tcn_layers": 3,
    "attention_heads": 4,
    "dropout": 0.1,
}

CBH_CONFIG: dict[str, Any] = {
    "architecture": "p0i_condition_transfer",
    "baseline_family": "covariate_transformer",
    "d_model": 64,
    "weather_channels": 14,
    "history_visible_steps": 24,
    "dropout": 0.05,
    "alignment_variant": "P0I",
    "native_scope": "native7",
    "transfer_variant": "CBH",
    "condition_mode": "full",
    "history_baseline_model_config": BASELINE_CONFIG,
    "initialize_history_baseline_from_checkpoint": True,
    "freeze_history_baseline": True,
    "output_clamp_min": None,
}

TRAINING_PROTOCOL: dict[str, Any] = {
    "precision": "bf16",
    "cycles_per_batch": 12,
    "validation_cycles_per_batch": 24,
    "baseline_batch_size": 48,
    "baseline_validation_batch_size": 96,
    "learning_rate": 2e-4,
    "weight_decay": 1e-4,
    "epochs": 80,
    "early_stopping_patience": 10,
    "gradient_clip": 1.0,
    "loss": "mae",
    "checkpoint_metric": "single_site_mae_cf",
    "output_clipping": False,
    "ema": False,
    "checkpoint_averaging": False,
    "ramp_loss": False,
    "auxiliary_loss": False,
}


def seed_all(seed: int) -> None:
    random.seed(int(seed))
    np.random.seed(int(seed))
    torch.manual_seed(int(seed))
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(int(seed))


def sha256_file(path: str | Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(path).open("rb") as handle:
        for block in iter(lambda: handle.read(1 << 20), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def tensor_state_hash(state: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor]) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for name, value in sorted(state.items()):
        item = value.detach().cpu().contiguous()
        digest.update(name.encode())
        digest.update(str(item.dtype).encode())
        digest.update(str(tuple(item.shape)).encode())
        digest.update(item.numpy().tobytes())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def atomic_json(path: str | Path, value: Any) -> None:
    output = Path(path)
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = output.with_name(output.name + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
    temporary.write_text(
        json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    os.replace(temporary, output)


def atomic_torch_save(path: str | Path, value: Any) -> None:
    output = Path(path)
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = output.with_name(output.name + f".{os.getpid()}.tmp")
    torch.save(value, temporary)
    os.replace(temporary, output)


def load_static() -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    names = (
        "station_id",
        "station_name",
        "station_latitude",
        "station_longitude",
        "station_capacity_mw",
        "weather_latitude",
        "weather_longitude",
        "nwp_mean",
        "nwp_std",
        "nwp_channel_name",
    )
    return {
        name: np.load(CACHE / "static" / f"{name}.npy", allow_pickle=False)
        for name in names
    }


def single_site_static(full: Mapping[str, np.ndarray], site: int) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    result: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
    for name, value in full.items():
        result[name] = value[site : site + 1].copy() if name.startswith("station_") else value.copy()
    return result


def canonical_static_vectors(full: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    """Return the static vectors used by the original 56-site models."""
    lat = torch.as_tensor(full["station_latitude"], dtype=torch.float32)
    lon = torch.as_tensor(full["station_longitude"], dtype=torch.float32)
    capacity = torch.as_tensor(full["station_capacity_mw"], dtype=torch.float32)
    weather_lat = torch.as_tensor(full["weather_latitude"], dtype=torch.float32)
    weather_lon = torch.as_tensor(full["weather_longitude"], dtype=torch.float32)
    station_query_static = torch.stack(
        (
            (lat - 0.5 * (weather_lat.min() + weather_lat.max()))
            / (weather_lat.max() - weather_lat.min()),
            (lon - 0.5 * (weather_lon.min() + weather_lon.max()))
            / (weather_lon.max() - weather_lon.min()),
            capacity / capacity.sum(),
            torch.log(capacity) / torch.log(capacity).mean(),
        ),
        dim=-1,
    )
    p0i_site = torch.stack(
        (
            (lat - lat.mean()) / lat.std(),
            (lon - lon.mean()) / lon.std(),
            capacity / capacity.mean(),
        ),
        dim=-1,
    )
    return station_query_static, p0i_site


def build_baseline(site: int, seed: int, full_static: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> nn.Module:
    from wind_forcing.models.forced_evolution import build_forced_evolution_model

    seed_all(seed)
    model = build_forced_evolution_model(
        BASELINE_CONFIG,
        single_site_static(full_static, site),
        history_steps=24,
        forecast_steps=24,
    )
    query_static, _ = canonical_static_vectors(full_static)
    with torch.no_grad():
        model.forcing_encoder.station_query.station_static.copy_(query_static[site : site + 1])
    return model


def build_cbh(
    site: int,
    seed: int,
    full_static: Mapping[str, np.ndarray],
    baseline_state: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor],
) -> nn.Module:
    from wind_forcing.models import build_forecast_model
    import wind_forcing.models.p0i_transfer as p0i_transfer

    # The frozen source contains a runtime monotonicity assertion implemented
    # as Python control flow over a Tensor.  torch.func.vmap cannot trace that
    # assertion, so validate offsets in the data audit and use the identical
    # interpolation arithmetic without the Python branch here.
    def vmap_safe_interpolate(nodes, times, leads):
        right = torch.searchsorted(times.contiguous(), leads.contiguous()).clamp(
            1, times.shape[1] - 1
        )
        left = right - 1
        t0, t1 = times.gather(1, left), times.gather(1, right)
        alpha = ((leads - t0) / (t1 - t0)).clamp(0, 1)
        shape = (nodes.shape[0], nodes.shape[1], leads.shape[1], nodes.shape[3])
        v0 = nodes.gather(2, left[:, None, :, None].expand(shape))
        v1 = nodes.gather(2, right[:, None, :, None].expand(shape))
        return v0 + alpha[:, None, :, None] * (v1 - v0)

    p0i_transfer.interpolate = vmap_safe_interpolate

    seed_all(seed)
    model = build_forecast_model(
        CBH_CONFIG,
        single_site_static(full_static, site),
        history_steps=24,
        forecast_steps=24,
    )
    model.load_history_checkpoint(
        {"model_config": BASELINE_CONFIG, "model_state": baseline_state}
    )
    query_static, p0i_site = canonical_static_vectors(full_static)
    with torch.no_grad():
        model.baseline_model.forcing_encoder.station_query.station_static.copy_(
            query_static[site : site + 1]
        )
        model.site.copy_(p0i_site[site : site + 1])
        # Local weather is pre-extracted with the original bilinear weights.
        # The canonical station_weather method is retained, but reads that
        # value exactly once instead of gathering the full 24x25 grid again.
        model.indices.zero_()
        model.weights.zero_()
        model.weights[..., 0] = 1.0
    model.baseline_model.requires_grad_(False)
    model.baseline_model.eval()
    return model


class FunctionalEnsemble:
    """A torch.func ensemble with one independent parameter slice per seed."""

    def __init__(self, models: Sequence[nn.Module], device: torch.device, kind: str) -> None:
        if not models:
            raise ValueError("models cannot be empty")
        self.kind = str(kind)
        self.size = len(models)
        trainable = {name: value.requires_grad for name, value in models[0].named_parameters()}
        params, buffers = stack_module_state(list(models))
        self.params = {
            name: value.detach().to(device).requires_grad_(trainable[name])
            for name, value in params.items()
        }
        self.buffers = {name: value.detach().to(device) for name, value in buffers.items()}
        self.base = copy.deepcopy(models[0]).to("meta")
        self.device = device
        self.parameter_names = tuple(dict(models[0].named_parameters()))
        self.buffer_names = tuple(dict(models[0].named_buffers()))
        self.trainable_names = tuple(name for name in self.parameter_names if trainable[name])
        if self.kind == "baseline":
            self._vmapped = vmap(self._call_baseline, in_dims=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0), randomness="different")
        elif self.kind == "cbh":
            self._vmapped = vmap(
                self._call_cbh,
                in_dims=(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0),
                randomness="different",
            )
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown ensemble kind: {self.kind}")

    def _call_baseline(self, params, buffers, history, cluster, phase):
        output = functional_call(
            self.base,
            (params, buffers),
            (),
            {"station_history": history, "cluster_history": cluster, "origin_phase": phase},
        )
        return output["station_factor"]

    def _call_cbh(self, params, buffers, history, cluster, phase, weather, weather_index, offsets):
        output = functional_call(
            self.base,
            (params, buffers),
            (),
            {
                "station_history": history,
                "cluster_history": cluster,
                "origin_phase": phase,
                "weather": weather,
                "weather_batch_index": weather_index,
                "weather_time_offset_hours": offsets,
            },
        )
        return output["station_factor"]

    def train(self, mode: bool = True) -> None:
        self.base.train(mode)
        if self.kind == "cbh":
            self.base.baseline_model.eval()

    def __call__(self, batch: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor]) -> Mapping[str, torch.Tensor]:
        if self.kind == "baseline":
            prediction = self._vmapped(
                self.params,
                self.buffers,
                batch["station_history"],
                batch["cluster_history"],
                batch["origin_phase"],
            )
        else:
            prediction = self._vmapped(
                self.params,
                self.buffers,
                batch["station_history"],
                batch["cluster_history"],
                batch["origin_phase"],
                batch["weather"],
                batch["weather_batch_index"],
                batch["weather_time_offset_hours"],
            )
        return {"station_factor": prediction}

    def optimizer_parameters(self) -> list[torch.Tensor]:
        return [self.params[name] for name in self.trainable_names]

    def clip_grad_norm_per_model(self, maximum: float) -> torch.Tensor:
        squared = torch.zeros(self.size, device=self.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        for name in self.trainable_names:
            gradient = self.params[name].grad
            if gradient is not None:
                squared += gradient.float().square().flatten(1).sum(1)
        norms = squared.sqrt()
        scale = (float(maximum) / (norms + 1e-6)).clamp(max=1.0)
        for name in self.trainable_names:
            gradient = self.params[name].grad
            if gradient is not None:
                gradient.mul_(scale.reshape((self.size,) + (1,) * (gradient.ndim - 1)).to(gradient))
        return norms

    def state_for(self, index: int) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        result: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        for name in self.parameter_names:
            result[name] = self.params[name][index].detach().cpu().clone()
        for name in self.buffer_names:
            result[name] = self.buffers[name][index].detach().cpu().clone()
        if set(result) != set(self.base.state_dict()):
            missing = set(self.base.state_dict()).difference(result)
            extra = set(result).difference(self.base.state_dict())
            raise RuntimeError(f"Functional state mismatch: missing={missing}, extra={extra}")
        return result


def ensemble_from_states(
    templates: Sequence[nn.Module],
    states: Sequence[Mapping[str, torch.Tensor]],
    device: torch.device,
    kind: str,
) -> FunctionalEnsemble:
    if len(templates) != len(states):
        raise ValueError("template/state count mismatch")
    for model, state in zip(templates, states):
        model.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
    return FunctionalEnsemble(templates, device, kind)
```

```
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import itertools
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import time
from typing import Any, Mapping

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from tqdm import tqdm

from wind_forcing.training import autocast_context, move_batch, save_checkpoint


def aggregate_inputs(batch: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
    keys = (
        "cluster_history",
        "weather",
        "future_hourly_weather_latent",
        "weather_batch_index",
        "weather_time_offset_hours",
        "past_hourly_weather_latent",
        "past_cluster_power",
        "past_weather_time_offset_hours",
        "current_weather_latent",
        "current_weather_time_offset_hours",
        "past_realization_factor",
        "origin_phase",
    )
    return {key: batch[key] for key in keys if key in batch}


def aggregate_loss(
    output: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor | None],
    batch: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor],
    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, float]]:
    prediction = output["cluster_factor"]
    assert prediction is not None
    quantile = float(settings.get("weather_quantile", 0.0))
    if quantile:
        if not 0.0 < quantile < 1.0:
            raise ValueError("weather_quantile must lie strictly between zero and one")
        residual = batch["cluster_future"].float() - prediction.float()
        supervised = torch.maximum(quantile * residual, (quantile - 1.0) * residual).mean()
    else:
        supervised = F.smooth_l1_loss(
            prediction.float(), batch["cluster_future"].float(), beta=0.05
        )
    current_weight = float(settings.get("current_weather_loss_weight", 0.0))
    current_prediction = output.get("current_weather_prediction_factor")
    if current_weight:
        if current_prediction is None:
            raise ValueError("Current-weather loss requires a current prediction")
        current_target = batch["cluster_history"][:, -1:].float()
        if quantile:
            current_residual = current_target - current_prediction.float()
            current_loss = torch.maximum(
                quantile * current_residual, (quantile - 1.0) * current_residual
            ).mean()
        else:
            current_loss = F.smooth_l1_loss(
                current_prediction.float(), current_target, beta=0.05
            )
    else:
        current_loss = supervised.detach() * 0.0
    anchor_weight = float(settings.get("baseline_anchor_loss_weight", 0.0))
    baseline = output.get("baseline_cluster_factor")
    anchor = output.get("anchor_baseline_cluster_factor")
    if anchor_weight:
        if baseline is None or anchor is None:
            raise ValueError("Anchor loss requires baseline and anchor predictions")
        values = F.smooth_l1_loss(baseline.float(), anchor.float(), beta=0.05, reduction="none")
        steps = values.shape[-1]
        lead = torch.arange(1, steps + 1, device=values.device, dtype=values.dtype) / 4.0
        if str(settings.get("anchor_schedule_mode", "horizon_taper")) == "horizon_taper":
            weights = torch.where(lead <= 3.0, 1.0, (4.0 - lead).clamp(0.0, 1.0))
        else:
            weights = torch.ones_like(lead)
        anchor_value = (values * weights.reshape(1, -1)).mean()
    else:
        anchor_value = supervised.detach() * 0.0
    ramp_weight = float(settings.get("ramp_loss_weight", 0.0))
    if ramp_weight:
        prediction_path = torch.cat((batch["cluster_history"][:, -1:], prediction), dim=1)
        target_path = torch.cat((batch["cluster_history"][:, -1:], batch["cluster_future"]), dim=1)
        ramp = F.smooth_l1_loss(
            prediction_path[:, 1:] - prediction_path[:, :-1],
            target_path[:, 1:] - target_path[:, :-1],
            beta=0.05,
        )
    else:
        ramp = supervised.detach() * 0.0
    identification_weight = float(settings.get("response_identification_loss_weight", 0.0))
    if identification_weight:
        identification_prediction = output.get("identification_prediction")
        identification_target = output.get("identification_target")
        if identification_prediction is None or identification_target is None:
            raise ValueError("Response-identification loss requires historical query outputs")
        identification = F.smooth_l1_loss(
            identification_prediction.float(),
            identification_target.float(),
            beta=0.05,
        )
    else:
        identification = supervised.detach() * 0.0
    loss = (
        float(settings.get("cluster_loss_weight", 1.0)) * supervised
        + anchor_weight * anchor_value
        + ramp_weight * ramp
        + identification_weight * identification
        + current_weight * current_loss
    )
    return loss, {
        "cluster": float(supervised.detach()),
        "anchor": float(anchor_value.detach()),
        "ramp": float(ramp.detach()),
        "identification": float(identification.detach()),
        "current_weather": float(current_loss.detach()),
    }


class AggregateMetrics:
    def __init__(self, forecast_steps: int, total_capacity_mw: float) -> None:
        self.steps = forecast_steps
        self.capacity = float(total_capacity_mw)
        self.samples = 0
        self.abs_sum = 0.0
        self.sq_sum = 0.0
        self.target_sum = 0.0
        self.target_sq_sum = 0.0
        self.persistence_abs_sum = 0.0
        self.energy_abs_sum = 0.0
        self.ramp_abs_sum = 0.0
        self.lead_abs = np.zeros(forecast_steps, dtype=np.float64)

    def update(
        self,
        prediction: torch.Tensor,
        target: torch.Tensor,
        history_last: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> None:
        error = (prediction.float() - target.float()) * self.capacity
        target_mw = target.float() * self.capacity
        persistence = (history_last[:, None].float() - target.float()) * self.capacity
        self.samples += len(prediction)
        self.abs_sum += float(error.abs().sum())
        self.sq_sum += float(error.square().sum())
        self.target_sum += float(target_mw.sum())
        self.target_sq_sum += float(target_mw.square().sum())
        self.persistence_abs_sum += float(persistence.abs().sum())
        self.energy_abs_sum += float((error.sum(dim=1) * 0.25).abs().sum())
        prediction_path = torch.cat((history_last[:, None], prediction), dim=1)
        target_path = torch.cat((history_last[:, None], target), dim=1)
        ramp_error = (
            prediction_path[:, 1:] - prediction_path[:, :-1]
            - target_path[:, 1:] + target_path[:, :-1]
        ) * self.capacity
        self.ramp_abs_sum += float(ramp_error.abs().sum())
        self.lead_abs += error.abs().sum(dim=0).cpu().double().numpy()

    def compute(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        count = self.samples * self.steps
        mae = self.abs_sum / count
        target_mean = self.target_sum / count
        target_total_sq = self.target_sq_sum - count * target_mean**2
        persistence = self.persistence_abs_sum / count
        lead = self.lead_abs / self.samples
        return {
            "cluster_mae_mw": mae,
            "cluster_rmse_mw": math.sqrt(self.sq_sum / count),
            "cluster_nmae": mae / self.capacity,
            "cluster_r2": 1.0 - self.sq_sum / target_total_sq,
            "cluster_energy_mae_mwh": self.energy_abs_sum / self.samples,
            "cluster_ramp_mae_mw": self.ramp_abs_sum / count,
            "persistence_mae_mw": persistence,
            "persistence_skill_mae": 1.0 - mae / persistence,
            "lead_cluster_mae_mw": lead.tolist(),
            "horizon_0_1h_mae_mw": float(lead[:4].mean()),
            "horizon_1_3h_mae_mw": float(lead[4:12].mean()),
            "horizon_3_6h_mae_mw": float(lead[12:].mean()),
            "samples": self.samples,
        }


def _progress(loader, description: str, max_batches: int | None = None):
    prefix = os.environ.get("WIND_PROGRESS_PREFIX", "").strip()
    position = os.environ.get("WIND_PROGRESS_POSITION", "").strip()
    total = len(loader) if max_batches is None else min(len(loader), max_batches)
    iterator = loader if max_batches is None else itertools.islice(loader, max_batches)
    return tqdm(
        iterator,
        total=total,
        desc=f"{prefix} {description}".strip(),
        unit="batch",
        dynamic_ncols=True,
        position=int(position) if position else None,
        leave=True,
        mininterval=0.5,
    )


@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate_aggregate(
    model: nn.Module,
    loader,
    device: torch.device,
    precision: str,
    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    max_batches: int | None = None,
    description: str = "Aggregate val",
    collect_predictions: bool = False,
    collect_diagnostics: bool = False,
    correction_state_intervention: str = "none",
    correction_state_lookup: Mapping[int, np.ndarray] | None = None,
    response_intervention: str = "none",
    response_power_lookup: Mapping[int, np.ndarray] | None = None,
    response_weather_lookup: Mapping[int, np.ndarray] | None = None,
) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, np.ndarray] | None]:
    if correction_state_intervention not in {"none", "cyclic_shift", "global_permutation", "reference"}:
        raise ValueError(
            "correction_state_intervention must be none, cyclic_shift, global_permutation, or reference"
        )
    if correction_state_intervention == "global_permutation" and correction_state_lookup is None:
        raise ValueError("global_permutation requires a global state-history lookup")
    valid_response = {
        "none", "power_shuffle", "weather_shuffle", "pair_shift", "response_mean", "support_only"
    }
    if response_intervention not in valid_response:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported response-state intervention: {response_intervention}")
    if response_intervention == "power_shuffle" and response_power_lookup is None:
        raise ValueError("power_shuffle requires a global historical-power lookup")
    if response_intervention == "weather_shuffle" and response_weather_lookup is None:
        raise ValueError("weather_shuffle requires a global historical-weather lookup")
    model.eval()
    if device.type == "cuda":
        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats(device)
    metrics = AggregateMetrics(model.forecast_steps, float(model.total_capacity_mw))
    loss_sum = 0.0
    samples = 0
    inference_seconds = 0.0
    state_attention_sum: torch.Tensor | None = None
    state_attention_samples = 0
    identification_abs_sum = 0.0
    identification_count = 0
    records: dict[str, list[np.ndarray]] = {
        "origin_unix": [], "sample_index": [], "cluster_prediction_mw": [],
        "cluster_target_mw": [], "cluster_history_last_mw": [],
    }
    diagnostic_keys = (
        "dynamics_signal", "equilibrium_factor", "response_rate_per_hour",
        "weather_envelope_factor", "realization_factor", "weather_absolute_factor",
        "current_weather_prediction_factor", "current_discrepancy_factor",
        "discrepancy_persistence",
    )
    for batch in _progress(loader, description, max_batches):
        batch = move_batch(batch, device)
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.synchronize(device)
        started = time.perf_counter()
        with autocast_context(device, precision):
            model_inputs = aggregate_inputs(batch)
            if correction_state_intervention == "cyclic_shift":
                if len(batch["cluster_history"]) < 2:
                    raise ValueError(
                        "cyclic_shift requires evaluation batches with at least two samples"
                    )
                model_inputs["correction_state_history"] = torch.roll(
                    batch["cluster_history"], shifts=1, dims=0
                )
            elif correction_state_intervention == "global_permutation":
                assert correction_state_lookup is not None
                replacement = np.stack(
                    [correction_state_lookup[int(index)] for index in batch["sample_index"].cpu().tolist()]
                )
                model_inputs["correction_state_history"] = torch.from_numpy(replacement).to(
                    device=device, dtype=batch["cluster_history"].dtype
                )
            if response_intervention == "power_shuffle":
                assert response_power_lookup is not None
                replacement = np.stack(
                    [response_power_lookup[int(index)] for index in batch["sample_index"].cpu().tolist()]
                )
                model_inputs["response_past_power_override"] = torch.from_numpy(replacement).to(
                    device=device, dtype=batch["past_cluster_power"].dtype
                )
            elif response_intervention == "weather_shuffle":
                assert response_weather_lookup is not None
                replacement = np.stack(
                    [response_weather_lookup[int(index)] for index in batch["sample_index"].cpu().tolist()]
                )
                model_inputs["response_past_weather_override"] = torch.from_numpy(replacement).to(
                    device=device, dtype=batch["past_hourly_weather_latent"].dtype
                )
            elif response_intervention == "pair_shift":
                model_inputs["response_pair_shift"] = True
            elif response_intervention == "support_only":
                model_inputs["response_support_only"] = True
            output = model(**model_inputs)
            loss, _ = aggregate_loss(output, batch, settings)
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.synchronize(device)
        inference_seconds += time.perf_counter() - started
        prediction = output["cluster_factor"]
        assert prediction is not None
        state_attention = output.get("state_attention")
        identification_prediction = output.get("identification_prediction")
        identification_target = output.get("identification_target")
        if identification_prediction is not None and identification_target is not None:
            identification_abs_sum += float(
                (identification_prediction.float() - identification_target.float()).abs().sum()
            )
            identification_count += int(identification_target.numel())
        if collect_diagnostics and state_attention is not None:
            detached_attention = state_attention.detach().float().sum(dim=0).cpu()
            state_attention_sum = (
                detached_attention
                if state_attention_sum is None
                else state_attention_sum + detached_attention
            )
            state_attention_samples += len(state_attention)
        size = len(prediction)
        loss_sum += float(loss) * size
        samples += size
        metrics.update(prediction, batch["cluster_future"], batch["cluster_history"][:, -1])
        if collect_predictions:
            capacity = float(model.total_capacity_mw)
            records["origin_unix"].append(batch["origin_unix"].cpu().numpy())
            records["sample_index"].append(batch["sample_index"].cpu().numpy())
            records["cluster_prediction_mw"].append(prediction.float().cpu().numpy() * capacity)
            records["cluster_target_mw"].append(batch["cluster_future"].float().cpu().numpy() * capacity)
            records["cluster_history_last_mw"].append(batch["cluster_history"][:, -1].float().cpu().numpy() * capacity)
            if collect_diagnostics:
                for key in diagnostic_keys:
                    value = output.get(key)
                    if value is not None:
                        records.setdefault(key, []).append(value.detach().float().cpu().numpy())
    if not samples:
        raise ValueError("Aggregate loader yielded no samples")
    result = {
        "loss": loss_sum / samples,
        **metrics.compute(),
        "inference_seconds": inference_seconds,
        "samples_per_second": samples / max(inference_seconds, 1e-9),
        "latency_ms_per_origin": 1000.0 * inference_seconds / samples,
        "peak_allocated_mib": (
            float(torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated(device) / 2**20)
            if device.type == "cuda" else None
        ),
        "correction_state_intervention": correction_state_intervention,
        "response_state_intervention": response_intervention,
    }
    if identification_count:
        result["identification_delta_mae_factor"] = identification_abs_sum / identification_count
        result["identification_delta_mae_mw"] = (
            identification_abs_sum / identification_count * float(model.total_capacity_mw)
        )
    if state_attention_sum is not None and state_attention_samples:
        result["mean_state_attention"] = (
            state_attention_sum / state_attention_samples
        ).numpy().tolist()
    archive = None
    if collect_predictions:
        archive = {key: np.concatenate(value) for key, value in records.items() if value}
        order = np.argsort(archive["origin_unix"], kind="stable")
        archive = {key: value[order] for key, value in archive.items()}
    return result, archive


def fit_aggregate(
    model: nn.Module,
    train_loader,
    val_loader,
    settings: Mapping[str, Any],
    device: torch.device,
    checkpoint_path: str | Path,
    checkpoint_metadata: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    precision: str,
    epochs: int | None = None,
    max_train_batches: int | None = None,
    max_val_batches: int | None = None,
) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    model.to(device)
    parameters = [parameter for parameter in model.parameters() if parameter.requires_grad]
    if not parameters:
        raise ValueError("Aggregate training has no trainable parameters")
    learning_rate = float(settings["learning_rate"])
    if hasattr(model, "optimizer_parameter_groups"):
        optimizer_parameters = model.optimizer_parameter_groups(
            learning_rate,
            float(settings.get("decoder_lr_multiplier", 0.1)),
        )
    else:
        optimizer_parameters = parameters
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        optimizer_parameters,
        lr=learning_rate,
        weight_decay=float(settings["weight_decay"]),
    )
    epoch_count = int(settings["epochs"] if epochs is None else epochs)
    scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epoch_count)
    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler("cuda", enabled=device.type == "cuda" and precision == "fp16")
    accumulation = int(settings.get("gradient_accumulation_steps", 1))
    if accumulation < 1:
        raise ValueError("gradient_accumulation_steps must be positive")
    patience = int(settings.get("early_stopping_patience", 10))
    best = float("inf")
    stale = 0
    history: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {"train": [], "val": []}
    for epoch in range(epoch_count):
        sampler = getattr(train_loader, "batch_sampler", None)
        if hasattr(sampler, "set_epoch"):
            sampler.set_epoch(epoch)
        model.train()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        total = 0.0
        samples = 0
        steps = 0
        started = time.perf_counter()
        progress = _progress(train_loader, f"Aggregate train {epoch + 1}/{epoch_count}", max_train_batches)
        for batch_index, batch in enumerate(progress):
            batch = move_batch(batch, device)
            with autocast_context(device, precision):
                output = model(**aggregate_inputs(batch))
                loss, _ = aggregate_loss(output, batch, settings)
                scaled_loss = loss / accumulation
            scaler.scale(scaled_loss).backward()
            size = len(batch["cluster_history"])
            total += float(loss.detach()) * size
            samples += size
            steps += 1
            final_batch = batch_index + 1 == len(progress)
            if steps % accumulation == 0 or final_batch:
                scaler.unscale_(optimizer)
                nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(parameters, float(settings.get("gradient_clip", 1.0)))
                scaler.step(optimizer)
                scaler.update()
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            progress.set_postfix(
                loss=f"{total / samples:.5f}",
                lr="/".join(f"{group['lr']:.1e}" for group in optimizer.param_groups),
            )
        scheduler.step()
        validation, _ = evaluate_aggregate(
            model, val_loader, device, precision, settings,
            max_batches=max_val_batches,
            description=f"Aggregate val {epoch + 1}/{epoch_count}",
        )
        train_record = {"loss": total / samples, "seconds": time.perf_counter() - started}
        history["train"].append(train_record)
        history["val"].append(validation)
        selection_mode = str(settings.get("checkpoint_selection", "validation"))
        if selection_mode not in {"validation", "last"}:
            raise ValueError("checkpoint_selection must be validation or last")
        improved = selection_mode == "last" or float(validation["cluster_mae_mw"]) < best
        if improved:
            best = float(validation["cluster_mae_mw"])
            stale = 0
            save_checkpoint(
                checkpoint_path,
                {
                    **checkpoint_metadata,
                    "model_state": model.state_dict(),
                    "epoch": epoch + 1,
                    "val_metrics": validation,
                    "checkpoint_metric": "cluster_mae_mw",
                },
            )
        elif selection_mode == "validation":
            stale += 1
        print(
            f"[aggregate:{epoch + 1:03d}/{epoch_count:03d}] train={train_record['loss']:.6f} "
            f"val_mae={validation['cluster_mae_mw']:.3f}MW val_rmse={validation['cluster_rmse_mw']:.3f}MW "
            f"best={best:.3f}MW patience={stale}/{patience} checkpoint={'saved' if improved else 'unchanged'}",
            flush=True,
        )
        if selection_mode == "validation" and stale >= patience:
            print(f"[early-stop] aggregate epoch={epoch + 1} best={best:.3f}MW", flush=True)
            break
    return history


def save_archive(path: str | Path, archive: Mapping[str, np.ndarray]) -> None:
    output = Path(path)
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = output.with_name(f".{output.name}.tmp.{os.getpid()}")
    with temporary.open("wb") as stream:
        np.savez_compressed(stream, **archive)
    os.replace(temporary, output)


def save_json(path: str | Path, value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
    output = Path(path)
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = output.with_name(f".{output.name}.tmp.{os.getpid()}")
    with temporary.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        json.dump(value, stream, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2)
    os.replace(temporary, output)
```

```
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import yaml


def load_config(path: str | Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    config_path = Path(path).resolve()
    with config_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        config = yaml.safe_load(stream)
    if not isinstance(config, dict):
        raise ValueError(f"Configuration must be a mapping: {config_path}")
    for section in ("data", "model", "training"):
        if not isinstance(config.get(section), dict):
            raise ValueError(f"Configuration misses mapping section: {section}")

    project_root = config_path.parent.parent
    config["_config_path"] = str(config_path)
    config["_project_root"] = str(project_root)
    required_paths = ("source_h5", "cache_dir")
    optional_paths = (
        "response_cache_dir", "response_latent_cache_root",
        "dynamics_time_cache_dir", "dynamics_latent_cache_root", "realization_cache_root",
    )
    for key in (*required_paths, *optional_paths):
        value = config["data"].get(key)
        if value is None and key in required_paths:
            raise ValueError(f"Configuration misses data.{key}")
        if value is None:
            continue
        path_value = Path(str(value))
        if not path_value.is_absolute():
            path_value = (config_path.parent / path_value).resolve()
        config["data"][key] = str(path_value)
    return config
```
