SHA-256: abb551d55486b2af7eb1669aff9a636b3d8d4953e619de5c3852aeebdde6d37b

```
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
from typing import Any, Mapping

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset

from wind_forcing.data.dataset import CycleGroupedBatchSampler


SPLITS = ("train", "val", "test")


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _load(path: Path, mmap: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r" if mmap else None, allow_pickle=False)


def _save(path: Path, values: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, Any]:
    np.save(path, values, allow_pickle=False)
    return {
        "relative_path": "",
        "shape": list(values.shape),
        "dtype": str(values.dtype),
        "sha256": _sha256(path),
        "storage": "owned",
    }


def capacity_density_bilinear(
    weather_latitude: np.ndarray,
    weather_longitude: np.ndarray,
    station_latitude: np.ndarray,
    station_longitude: np.ndarray,
    station_capacity_mw: np.ndarray,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Conservatively scatter station capacities onto four enclosing grid nodes."""
    latitude = np.asarray(weather_latitude, dtype=np.float64)
    longitude = np.asarray(weather_longitude, dtype=np.float64)
    station_lat = np.asarray(station_latitude, dtype=np.float64)
    station_lon = np.asarray(station_longitude, dtype=np.float64)
    capacity = np.asarray(station_capacity_mw, dtype=np.float64)
    if latitude.ndim != 1 or longitude.ndim != 1:
        raise ValueError("Weather coordinates must be one-dimensional")
    if not (np.all(np.diff(latitude) < 0) and np.all(np.diff(longitude) > 0)):
        raise ValueError("Expected descending latitude and ascending longitude")
    if not (station_lat.shape == station_lon.shape == capacity.shape):
        raise ValueError("Station coordinates and capacities have inconsistent shapes")
    if np.any(capacity <= 0) or not np.isfinite(capacity).all():
        raise ValueError("Station capacities must be finite and positive")

    density = np.zeros((len(latitude), len(longitude)), dtype=np.float64)
    for farm_lat, farm_lon, farm_capacity in zip(station_lat, station_lon, capacity):
        north = int(np.searchsorted(-latitude, -farm_lat, side="right") - 1)
        west = int(np.searchsorted(longitude, farm_lon, side="right") - 1)
        if not (0 <= north < len(latitude) - 1 and 0 <= west < len(longitude) - 1):
            raise ValueError("A station lies outside the weather grid")
        west_fraction = float(
            (farm_lon - longitude[west]) / (longitude[west + 1] - longitude[west])
        )
        north_fraction = float(
            (farm_lat - latitude[north + 1]) / (latitude[north] - latitude[north + 1])
        )
        weights = (
            (1.0 - west_fraction) * north_fraction,
            west_fraction * north_fraction,
            (1.0 - west_fraction) * (1.0 - north_fraction),
            west_fraction * (1.0 - north_fraction),
        )
        nodes = ((north, west), (north, west + 1), (north + 1, west), (north + 1, west + 1))
        for (row, column), weight in zip(nodes, weights):
            density[row, column] += farm_capacity * weight
    if not np.isclose(density.sum(), capacity.sum(), atol=1e-5, rtol=1e-10):
        raise ValueError("Capacity scatter failed conservation audit")
    density /= density.sum()
    return density.astype(np.float32)


class AggregateCacheBuilder:
    """Derive a station-free aggregate cache from an audited schema-v1 cache."""

    sample_names = (
        "origin_unix",
        "sample_index",
        "origin_phase",
        "nwp_age_minutes",
        "weather_cycle_index",
        "weather_time_offset_hours",
    )
    cycle_names = ("weather_valid_unix", "cycle_sample_index")

    def __init__(self, source: str | Path, destination: str | Path) -> None:
        self.source = Path(source).resolve()
        self.destination = Path(destination).resolve()

    def _source_manifest(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        path = self.source / "manifest.json"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(path)
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            manifest = json.load(stream)
        if int(manifest.get("schema_version", -1)) != 1:
            raise ValueError("Aggregate derivation requires a schema-v1 model cache")
        return manifest

    def is_current(self) -> bool:
        path = self.destination / "manifest.json"
        if not path.is_file():
            return False
        try:
            with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                manifest = json.load(stream)
            source = self._source_manifest()
            if manifest.get("schema_version") != 2:
                return False
            if manifest.get("parent_preprocess_fingerprint") != source.get("preprocess_fingerprint"):
                return False
            for entry in manifest["arrays"]:
                array = _load(self.destination / entry["relative_path"], mmap=True)
                if list(array.shape) != entry["shape"] or str(array.dtype) != entry["dtype"]:
                    return False
            return True
        except (OSError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            return False

    def audit(self, *, verify_checksums: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Validate the station-free schema and optionally every stored digest."""
        path = self.destination / "manifest.json"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(path)
        with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            manifest = json.load(stream)
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != 2 or manifest.get("task_level") != "aggregate_cluster":
            raise ValueError("Aggregate cache manifest has the wrong schema")
        if manifest.get("station_sample_tensors_present") is not False:
            raise ValueError("Aggregate manifest does not prohibit station sample tensors")
        checked = 0
        for entry in manifest["arrays"]:
            array_path = self.destination / entry["relative_path"]
            array = _load(array_path, mmap=True)
            if list(array.shape) != entry["shape"] or str(array.dtype) != entry["dtype"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Array metadata mismatch: {entry['relative_path']}")
            if "station" in Path(entry["relative_path"]).name and not entry["relative_path"].startswith("static/"):
                raise ValueError(f"Station sample tensor leaked into aggregate cache: {entry['relative_path']}")
            if verify_checksums and _sha256(array_path) != entry["sha256"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Checksum mismatch: {entry['relative_path']}")
            checked += 1
        for split in SPLITS:
            directory_names = {item.name for item in (self.destination / split).glob("*.npy")}
            if any("station" in name for name in directory_names):
                raise ValueError(f"Station array found in aggregate {split} directory")
        density = _load(self.destination / "static" / "capacity_density.npy")
        if density.shape != (24, 25) or np.any(density < 0) or not np.isclose(density.sum(), 1.0, atol=1e-6):
            raise ValueError("Capacity-density map failed normalization audit")
        result = {
            "cache": str(self.destination),
            "preprocess_fingerprint": manifest["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "arrays_checked": checked,
            "checksums_verified": bool(verify_checksums),
            "station_sample_tensors_present": False,
            "capacity_density_sum": float(density.sum()),
        }
        print(f"[aggregate-audit-cache] {result}", flush=True)
        return result

    def build(self) -> Path:
        source_manifest = self._source_manifest()
        if self.is_current():
            print(f"[aggregate-prepare] reusing {self.destination}", flush=True)
            return self.destination
        if self.destination.exists():
            raise FileExistsError(
                f"Stale aggregate cache exists: {self.destination}. Move it aside before rebuilding."
            )
        self.destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        temporary = Path(
            tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f".{self.destination.name}.tmp.", dir=self.destination.parent)
        )
        try:
            manifest = self._build_into(temporary, source_manifest)
            with (temporary / "manifest.json").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                json.dump(manifest, stream, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2)
            os.replace(temporary, self.destination)
        except Exception:
            shutil.rmtree(temporary, ignore_errors=True)
            raise
        print(f"[aggregate-prepare] ready {self.destination}", flush=True)
        return self.destination

    def _build_into(self, root: Path, source_manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        arrays: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        static_source = self.source / "static"
        static_output = root / "static"
        static_output.mkdir()
        static_names = (
            "weather_latitude",
            "weather_longitude",
            "nwp_mean",
            "nwp_std",
            "nwp_channel_name",
        )
        for name in static_names:
            values = np.asarray(_load(static_source / f"{name}.npy"))
            path = static_output / f"{name}.npy"
            entry = _save(path, values)
            entry["relative_path"] = f"static/{name}.npy"
            arrays.append(entry)
        capacity_density = capacity_density_bilinear(
            _load(static_source / "weather_latitude.npy"),
            _load(static_source / "weather_longitude.npy"),
            _load(static_source / "station_latitude.npy"),
            _load(static_source / "station_longitude.npy"),
            _load(static_source / "station_capacity_mw.npy"),
        )
        density_path = static_output / "capacity_density.npy"
        entry = _save(density_path, capacity_density)
        entry["relative_path"] = "static/capacity_density.npy"
        arrays.append(entry)

        total_capacity = float(source_manifest["total_capacity_mw"])
        split_manifest: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for split in SPLITS:
            source_dir = self.source / split
            output_dir = root / split
            output_dir.mkdir()
            factor_history = np.asarray(_load(source_dir / "cluster_history.npy"), dtype=np.float32)
            factor_future = np.asarray(_load(source_dir / "cluster_future.npy"), dtype=np.float32)
            values: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {
                name: np.asarray(_load(source_dir / f"{name}.npy"))
                for name in (*self.sample_names, *self.cycle_names)
            }
            values["cluster_history_mw"] = factor_history * total_capacity
            values["cluster_future_mw"] = factor_future * total_capacity
            warning = np.asarray(_load(source_dir / "future_capacity_warning.npy"), dtype=bool)
            values["cluster_future_capacity_warning"] = warning.any(axis=1)
            for name, value in values.items():
                path = output_dir / f"{name}.npy"
                item = _save(path, value)
                item["relative_path"] = f"{split}/{name}.npy"
                arrays.append(item)

            source_weather = source_dir / "weather.npy"
            output_weather = output_dir / "weather.npy"
            storage = "hardlink"
            try:
                os.link(source_weather, output_weather)
            except OSError:
                shutil.copy2(source_weather, output_weather)
                storage = "copy"
            weather = _load(output_weather, mmap=True)
            arrays.append(
                {
                    "relative_path": f"{split}/weather.npy",
                    "shape": list(weather.shape),
                    "dtype": str(weather.dtype),
                    "sha256": _sha256(output_weather),
                    "storage": storage,
                }
            )
            origins = values["origin_unix"].astype("datetime64[s]")
            split_manifest[split] = {
                "sample_count": int(len(factor_history)),
                "cycle_count": int(len(weather)),
                "origin_start": str(origins.min()),
                "origin_stop": str(origins.max()),
                "weather_shape": list(weather.shape[1:]),
            }
        identity_payload = {
            "parent": source_manifest["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "task": "aggregate_cluster_v1",
            "total_capacity_mw": total_capacity,
            "history_steps": int(source_manifest["history_power_steps"]),
            "forecast_steps": int(source_manifest["forecast_power_steps"]),
        }
        identity = hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(identity_payload, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        return {
            "schema_version": 2,
            "task_level": "aggregate_cluster",
            "source_cache": str(self.source),
            "parent_preprocess_fingerprint": source_manifest["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "preprocess_fingerprint": identity,
            "station_sample_tensors_present": False,
            "weather_values_are_prestandardized": True,
            "power_storage_unit": "MW",
            "model_power_unit": "capacity_factor",
            "capacity_density_method": "four_node_bilinear_scatter",
            "total_capacity_mw": total_capacity,
            "history_power_steps": int(source_manifest["history_power_steps"]),
            "forecast_power_steps": int(source_manifest["forecast_power_steps"]),
            "future_nwp_nodes": int(source_manifest["future_nwp_nodes"]),
            "target_lead_hours": list(source_manifest["target_lead_hours"]),
            "crop": source_manifest["crop"],
            "splits": split_manifest,
            "arrays": arrays,
        }


class AggregateForecastDataset(Dataset[int]):
    sample_names = (
        "origin_unix",
        "sample_index",
        "origin_phase",
        "nwp_age_minutes",
        "weather_cycle_index",
        "weather_time_offset_hours",
        "cluster_history_mw",
        "cluster_future_mw",
        "cluster_future_capacity_warning",
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        cache_dir: str | Path,
        split: str,
        *,
        include_weather: bool = True,
        preload_weather: bool | str = "auto",
        preload_weather_max_gib: float = 16.0,
        verbose: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        if split not in SPLITS:
            raise ValueError(f"split must be one of {SPLITS}")
        self.cache_dir = Path(cache_dir).resolve()
        with (self.cache_dir / "manifest.json").open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            self.manifest = json.load(stream)
        if self.manifest.get("schema_version") != 2 or self.manifest.get("task_level") != "aggregate_cluster":
            raise ValueError("Expected aggregate-cluster schema-v2 cache")
        self.total_capacity_mw = float(self.manifest["total_capacity_mw"])
        static_dir = self.cache_dir / "static"
        self.static = {
            name: _load(static_dir / f"{name}.npy")
            for name in (
                "weather_latitude",
                "weather_longitude",
                "nwp_mean",
                "nwp_std",
                "nwp_channel_name",
                "capacity_density",
            )
        }
        split_dir = self.cache_dir / split
        self.arrays = {name: _load(split_dir / f"{name}.npy") for name in self.sample_names}
        self.include_weather = bool(include_weather)
        self.weather: np.ndarray | None = None
        if self.include_weather:
            mapped = _load(split_dir / "weather.npy", mmap=True)
            setting = str(preload_weather).lower() if isinstance(preload_weather, str) else preload_weather
            preload = setting is True or setting in {"1", "true", "yes"}
            if setting == "auto":
                preload = mapped.nbytes <= int(preload_weather_max_gib * 1024**3)
            self.weather = np.array(mapped, copy=True) if preload else mapped
        self.length = len(self.arrays["origin_unix"])
        if any(len(value) != self.length for value in self.arrays.values()):
            raise ValueError("Aggregate sample arrays have inconsistent lengths")
        if verbose:
            print(
                f"[aggregate-dataset:{split}] samples={self.length} "
                f"weather={self.include_weather} history=[24] target=[24]",
                flush=True,
            )

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return self.length

    def __getitem__(self, index: int) -> int:
        return index

    def close(self) -> None:
        mmap = getattr(self.weather, "_mmap", None)
        if mmap is not None:
            mmap.close()


class AggregateForecastCollator:
    def __init__(self, dataset: AggregateForecastDataset) -> None:
        self.dataset = dataset

    def __call__(self, indices: list[int]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        index = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.int64)
        batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        if self.dataset.include_weather:
            if self.dataset.weather is None:
                raise RuntimeError("Weather store is unavailable")
            cycles = np.asarray(self.dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"][index], dtype=np.int64)
            unique, inverse = np.unique(cycles, return_inverse=True)
            batch["weather"] = torch.from_numpy(
                np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(self.dataset.weather[unique], dtype=np.float32))
            )
            batch["weather_batch_index"] = torch.from_numpy(inverse.astype(np.int64))
        for name, values in self.dataset.arrays.items():
            selected = np.ascontiguousarray(values[index])
            if name == "cluster_history_mw":
                batch["cluster_history"] = torch.from_numpy(
                    selected.astype(np.float32) / self.dataset.total_capacity_mw
                )
            elif name == "cluster_future_mw":
                batch["cluster_future"] = torch.from_numpy(
                    selected.astype(np.float32) / self.dataset.total_capacity_mw
                )
            else:
                if selected.dtype == np.bool_:
                    tensor = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.bool_))
                elif np.issubdtype(selected.dtype, np.floating):
                    tensor = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.float32))
                else:
                    tensor = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.int64))
                batch[name] = tensor
        return batch


def create_aggregate_loader(
    dataset: AggregateForecastDataset,
    *,
    cycles_per_batch: int,
    shuffle: bool,
    seed: int = 0,
    num_workers: int = 0,
) -> DataLoader[dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
        "num_workers": num_workers,
        "pin_memory": torch.cuda.is_available(),
        "persistent_workers": num_workers > 0,
        "collate_fn": AggregateForecastCollator(dataset),
    }
    if dataset.include_weather:
        sampler = CycleGroupedBatchSampler(
            dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"], cycles_per_batch, shuffle, seed
        )
        return DataLoader(dataset, batch_sampler=sampler, **kwargs)
    generator = torch.Generator().manual_seed(int(seed))
    return DataLoader(
        dataset,
        batch_size=4 * cycles_per_batch,
        shuffle=shuffle,
        generator=generator,
        **kwargs,
    )
```

```
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
from typing import Any

import h5py
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset

from wind_forcing.data.dataset import CycleGroupedBatchSampler
from wind_forcing.data.aggregate_response import AggregateResponseForecastDataset


SPLITS = ("train", "val", "test")


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _json_identity(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()


def _atomic_json(path: Path, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    fd, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            json.dump(payload, stream, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2)
        os.replace(name, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(name):
            os.unlink(name)


class AggregateDynamicsTimeCacheBuilder:
    """Cache causal metadata for estimating Pangu weather at forecast origin.

    The deployed future path starts at the first valid time after the origin for
    three of four 15-minute phases.  We therefore use the previous available
    Pangu issue and its two hourly nodes bracketing the origin.  The source HDF5
    stores the left node in the stage-I history window; the immediately next
    lead is resolved through ``run_lead_to_frame``.  At a split boundary where
    that frame is absent, the first node of the newer issue is an explicitly
    audited fallback with the same valid time.
    """

    def __init__(self, source_h5: str | Path, aggregate_cache: str | Path, destination: str | Path) -> None:
        self.source_h5 = Path(source_h5).resolve()
        self.aggregate_cache = Path(aggregate_cache).resolve()
        self.destination = Path(destination).resolve()

    def build(self) -> Path:
        with (self.aggregate_cache / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            aggregate = json.load(stream)
        source_stat = self.source_h5.stat()
        identity_payload = {
            "source_h5": str(self.source_h5),
            "source_size": source_stat.st_size,
            "source_mtime_ns": source_stat.st_mtime_ns,
            "aggregate_fingerprint": aggregate["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "protocol": "previous_available_issue_brackets_origin",
        }
        fingerprint = _json_identity(identity_payload)
        manifest_path = self.destination / "manifest.json"
        if manifest_path.is_file():
            with manifest_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                current = json.load(stream)
            if current.get("preprocess_fingerprint") != fingerprint:
                raise RuntimeError(f"Stale dynamics time cache: {self.destination}")
            self.audit()
            return self.destination

        self.destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        arrays: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        summaries: dict[str, Any] = {}
        with h5py.File(self.source_h5, "r") as handle:
            run_to_frame = handle["nwp/run_lead_to_frame"]
            run_valid = handle["nwp/valid"]
            frame_run = np.asarray(handle["nwp/frame_run_index"], dtype=np.int64)
            issue = np.asarray(handle["nwp/issue_unix"], dtype=np.int64)
            for split in SPLITS:
                output = self.destination / split
                output.mkdir()
                cycles = handle[f"stage1_splits/{split}"]
                samples = handle[f"splits/{split}"]
                aggregate_dir = self.aggregate_cache / split
                aggregate_cycle_ids = np.load(aggregate_dir / "cycle_sample_index.npy", allow_pickle=False)
                if not np.array_equal(aggregate_cycle_ids, np.asarray(cycles["sample_index"], dtype=np.int64)):
                    raise ValueError(f"Cycle order mismatch for {split}")
                aggregate_samples = np.load(aggregate_dir / "sample_index.npy", allow_pickle=False)
                if not np.array_equal(aggregate_samples, np.asarray(samples["sample_index"], dtype=np.int64)):
                    raise ValueError(f"Sample order mismatch for {split}")

                old_runs = np.asarray(cycles["nwp_run_index"][:, 5], dtype=np.int64)
                left_frames = np.asarray(cycles["nwp_frame_index"][:, 5], dtype=np.int64)
                left_leads = np.asarray(cycles["nwp_lead_index"][:, 5], dtype=np.int64)
                right_leads = left_leads + 1
                right_frames = np.full(len(old_runs), -1, dtype=np.int64)
                fallback = np.zeros(len(old_runs), dtype=np.bool_)
                for index, (run, lead) in enumerate(zip(old_runs, right_leads)):
                    if lead < run_to_frame.shape[1] and bool(run_valid[run, lead]):
                        right_frames[index] = int(run_to_frame[run, lead])
                    else:
                        right_frames[index] = int(cycles["nwp_frame_index"][index, 6])
                        fallback[index] = True
                if np.any(right_frames < 0):
                    raise ValueError(f"Unresolved current-weather right frame for {split}")
                if not np.array_equal(frame_run[left_frames], old_runs):
                    raise ValueError(f"Left frame issue mismatch for {split}")

                old_issue = np.asarray(issue[old_runs], dtype=np.int64)
                left_valid = old_issue + (left_leads + 1) * 3600
                right_valid = left_valid + 3600
                cycle_ids = np.asarray(samples["stage1_cycle_index"], dtype=np.int64)
                local = np.searchsorted(aggregate_cycle_ids, cycle_ids)
                origins = np.asarray(samples["origin_unix"], dtype=np.int64)
                offsets = np.stack(
                    (left_valid[local] - origins, right_valid[local] - origins), axis=-1
                ).astype(np.float32) / 3600.0
                if not (np.all(offsets[:, 0] <= 0.0) and np.all(offsets[:, 1] >= 0.0)):
                    raise ValueError(f"Current weather does not bracket origin for {split}")
                issue_pair = np.stack((old_issue, issue[frame_run[right_frames]]), axis=-1)
                if not np.all(issue_pair[local] <= origins[:, None]):
                    raise ValueError(f"Current-weather frame was not available at origin for {split}")

                values = {
                    "left_frame_index": left_frames,
                    "right_frame_index": right_frames,
                    "right_cycle_fallback": fallback,
                    "current_weather_time_offset_hours": offsets,
                    "current_weather_issue_unix": issue_pair[local].astype(np.int64),
                    "sample_index": aggregate_samples.astype(np.int64),
                }
                for name, value in values.items():
                    path = output / f"{name}.npy"
                    np.save(path, value, allow_pickle=False)
                    arrays.append({
                        "relative_path": f"{split}/{name}.npy",
                        "shape": list(value.shape),
                        "dtype": str(value.dtype),
                        "sha256": _sha256(path),
                    })
                summaries[split] = {
                    "sample_count": int(len(origins)),
                    "cycle_count": int(len(old_runs)),
                    "fallback_cycle_count": int(fallback.sum()),
                    "left_offset_min_hours": float(offsets[:, 0].min()),
                    "right_offset_max_hours": float(offsets[:, 1].max()),
                }
        payload = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "task_level": "aggregate_weather_dynamics_time",
            **identity_payload,
            "preprocess_fingerprint": fingerprint,
            "station_sample_tensors_present": False,
            "time_protocol": "both interpolation nodes issued no later than origin",
            "splits": summaries,
            "arrays": arrays,
        }
        _atomic_json(manifest_path, payload)
        self.audit()
        return self.destination

    def audit(self, verify_checksums: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        with (self.destination / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            manifest = json.load(stream)
        if manifest.get("task_level") != "aggregate_weather_dynamics_time":
            raise ValueError("Wrong dynamics-time cache")
        checked = 0
        for entry in manifest["arrays"]:
            path = self.destination / entry["relative_path"]
            values = np.load(path, mmap_mode="r", allow_pickle=False)
            if list(values.shape) != entry["shape"] or str(values.dtype) != entry["dtype"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Dynamics-time metadata mismatch: {entry['relative_path']}")
            if verify_checksums and _sha256(path) != entry["sha256"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Dynamics-time checksum mismatch: {entry['relative_path']}")
            checked += 1
        return {"cache": str(self.destination), "arrays_checked": checked, "causal": True}


class AggregateWeatherDynamicsDataset(Dataset[int]):
    """Response-state dataset plus a seed-specific latent at forecast origin."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        aggregate_cache: str | Path,
        response_cache: str | Path,
        response_latent_cache: str | Path,
        dynamics_time_cache: str | Path,
        dynamics_latent_cache: str | Path,
        split: str,
        *,
        realization_cache: str | Path | None = None,
        exclude_origin_start_unix: int | None = None,
        exclude_origin_stop_unix: int | None = None,
        exclusion_embargo_seconds: int = 0,
        verbose: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self.base = AggregateResponseForecastDataset(
            aggregate_cache, response_cache, response_latent_cache, split, verbose=False
        )
        self.manifest = self.base.manifest
        self.response_manifest = self.base.response_manifest
        self.latent_manifest = self.base.latent_manifest
        self.total_capacity_mw = self.base.total_capacity_mw
        self.static = self.base.static
        self.arrays = self.base.arrays
        time_root = Path(dynamics_time_cache).resolve()
        with (time_root / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            self.dynamics_time_manifest = json.load(stream)
        if self.dynamics_time_manifest.get("aggregate_fingerprint") != self.manifest.get("preprocess_fingerprint"):
            raise ValueError("Dynamics-time and aggregate cache provenance differ")
        current_root = Path(dynamics_latent_cache).resolve() / split
        with (current_root / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            self.dynamics_latent_manifest = json.load(stream)
        if self.dynamics_latent_manifest.get("base_checkpoint_sha256") != self.latent_manifest.get("base_checkpoint_sha256"):
            raise ValueError("Current and future R2 latent caches use different checkpoints")
        self.current_weather_latent = np.load(
            current_root / "current_weather_latent.npy", mmap_mode="r", allow_pickle=False
        )
        offsets = np.load(
            time_root / split / "current_weather_time_offset_hours.npy", mmap_mode="r", allow_pickle=False
        )
        self.arrays = dict(self.arrays)
        self.arrays["current_weather_time_offset_hours"] = offsets
        self.realization_factor = None
        self.realization_manifest = None
        if realization_cache is not None:
            realization_root = Path(realization_cache).resolve() / split
            with (realization_root / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                self.realization_manifest = json.load(stream)
            self.realization_factor = np.load(
                realization_root / "past_realization_factor.npy", mmap_mode="r", allow_pickle=False
            )
        if len(self.current_weather_latent) != len(self.base):
            raise ValueError("Current-weather latent sample count mismatch")
        if self.realization_factor is not None and len(self.realization_factor) != len(self.base):
            raise ValueError("Realization-factor sample count mismatch")
        self.sample_indices = np.arange(len(self.base), dtype=np.int64)
        if exclude_origin_start_unix is not None or exclude_origin_stop_unix is not None:
            if exclude_origin_start_unix is None or exclude_origin_stop_unix is None:
                raise ValueError("Both exclusion range endpoints are required")
            start = int(exclude_origin_start_unix) - int(exclusion_embargo_seconds)
            stop = int(exclude_origin_stop_unix) + int(exclusion_embargo_seconds)
            origins = np.asarray(self.arrays["origin_unix"], dtype=np.int64)
            self.sample_indices = np.flatnonzero((origins < start) | (origins > stop)).astype(np.int64)
            if not len(self.sample_indices):
                raise ValueError("Dynamics exclusion removed every sample")
        if verbose:
            print(
                f"[aggregate-dynamics:{split}] samples={len(self.sample_indices)}/{len(self.base)} "
                f"current={self.current_weather_latent.shape} realization={self.realization_factor is not None}",
                flush=True,
            )

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return len(self.sample_indices)

    def __getitem__(self, index: int) -> int:
        return index

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.base.close()
        for values in (self.current_weather_latent, self.realization_factor):
            mmap = getattr(values, "_mmap", None)
            if mmap is not None:
                mmap.close()


class AggregateWeatherDynamicsCollator:
    def __init__(self, dataset: AggregateWeatherDynamicsDataset) -> None:
        self.dataset = dataset

    def __call__(self, indices: list[int]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        positions = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.int64)
        index = self.dataset.sample_indices[positions]
        cycles = np.asarray(self.dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"][index], dtype=np.int64)
        unique, inverse = np.unique(cycles, return_inverse=True)
        batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {
            "future_hourly_weather_latent": torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(
                np.asarray(self.dataset.base.future_hourly_latent[unique], dtype=np.float32)
            )),
            "past_hourly_weather_latent": torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(
                np.asarray(self.dataset.base.past_hourly_latent[cycles], dtype=np.float32)
            )),
            "current_weather_latent": torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(
                np.asarray(self.dataset.current_weather_latent[index], dtype=np.float32)
            )),
            "weather_batch_index": torch.from_numpy(inverse.astype(np.int64)),
        }
        if self.dataset.realization_factor is not None:
            batch["past_realization_factor"] = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(
                np.asarray(self.dataset.realization_factor[index], dtype=np.float32)
            ))
        for name, values in self.dataset.arrays.items():
            selected = np.ascontiguousarray(values[index])
            if name in {"cluster_history_mw", "cluster_future_mw", "past_cluster_power_mw"}:
                key = {
                    "cluster_history_mw": "cluster_history",
                    "cluster_future_mw": "cluster_future",
                    "past_cluster_power_mw": "past_cluster_power",
                }[name]
                batch[key] = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.float32) / self.dataset.total_capacity_mw)
            elif selected.dtype == np.bool_:
                batch[name] = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.bool_))
            elif np.issubdtype(selected.dtype, np.floating):
                batch[name] = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.float32))
            else:
                batch[name] = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.int64))
        return batch


def create_aggregate_dynamics_loader(
    dataset: AggregateWeatherDynamicsDataset,
    *,
    cycles_per_batch: int,
    shuffle: bool,
    seed: int = 0,
    num_workers: int = 0,
) -> DataLoader[dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    sampler = CycleGroupedBatchSampler(
        np.asarray(dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"])[dataset.sample_indices],
        cycles_per_batch, shuffle, seed
    )
    return DataLoader(
        dataset,
        batch_sampler=sampler,
        num_workers=num_workers,
        pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
        persistent_workers=num_workers > 0,
        collate_fn=AggregateWeatherDynamicsCollator(dataset),
    )
```

```
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

import h5py
import numpy as np
from numpy.lib.format import open_memmap
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset

from wind_forcing.data.dataset import CycleGroupedBatchSampler


SPLITS = ("train", "val", "test")
HISTORY_NWP_NODES = 6


def _sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def _load(path: Path, *, mmap: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r" if mmap else None, allow_pickle=False)


def _save(path: Path, values: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, Any]:
    np.save(path, values, allow_pickle=False)
    return {
        "relative_path": "",
        "shape": list(values.shape),
        "dtype": str(values.dtype),
        "sha256": _sha256(path),
    }


class AggregateResponseCacheBuilder:
    """Build causal historical Pangu--aggregate-power pairs from the source HDF5."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        source_h5: str | Path,
        aggregate_cache: str | Path,
        destination: str | Path,
        *,
        chunk_cycles: int = 32,
    ) -> None:
        self.source_h5 = Path(source_h5).resolve()
        self.aggregate_cache = Path(aggregate_cache).resolve()
        self.destination = Path(destination).resolve()
        self.chunk_cycles = int(chunk_cycles)
        if self.chunk_cycles < 1:
            raise ValueError("chunk_cycles must be positive")

    def _aggregate_manifest(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        with (self.aggregate_cache / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            manifest = json.load(stream)
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != 2 or manifest.get("task_level") != "aggregate_cluster":
            raise ValueError("Expected the audited aggregate-cluster schema-v2 cache")
        return manifest

    def _identity(self, aggregate: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
        stat = self.source_h5.stat()
        payload = {
            "source_h5": str(self.source_h5),
            "source_size_bytes": stat.st_size,
            "source_mtime_ns": stat.st_mtime_ns,
            "aggregate_fingerprint": aggregate["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "history_nwp_nodes": HISTORY_NWP_NODES,
            "crop": aggregate["crop"],
            "protocol": "historical_issue_and_realized_cluster_power_pairs",
        }
        return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()

    def is_current(self) -> bool:
        manifest_path = self.destination / "manifest.json"
        if not manifest_path.is_file():
            return False
        try:
            with manifest_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                manifest = json.load(stream)
            aggregate = self._aggregate_manifest()
            if manifest.get("schema_version") != 1:
                return False
            if manifest.get("preprocess_fingerprint") != self._identity(aggregate):
                return False
            for entry in manifest["arrays"]:
                array = _load(self.destination / entry["relative_path"], mmap=True)
                if list(array.shape) != entry["shape"] or str(array.dtype) != entry["dtype"]:
                    return False
            return True
        except (OSError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            return False

    def build(self) -> Path:
        aggregate = self._aggregate_manifest()
        if self.is_current():
            print(f"[response-cache] reusing {self.destination}", flush=True)
            return self.destination
        if self.destination.exists():
            raise FileExistsError(
                f"Stale response cache exists: {self.destination}. Move it aside before rebuilding."
            )
        self.destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        temporary = Path(
            tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=f".{self.destination.name}.tmp.", dir=self.destination.parent)
        )
        try:
            manifest = self._build_into(temporary, aggregate)
            (temporary / "manifest.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
            )
            os.replace(temporary, self.destination)
        except Exception:
            shutil.rmtree(temporary, ignore_errors=True)
            raise
        print(f"[response-cache] ready {self.destination}", flush=True)
        return self.destination

    def _build_into(self, root: Path, aggregate: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
        arrays: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        split_summary: dict[str, Any] = {}
        crop = aggregate["crop"]
        row_slice = slice(int(crop["row_start"]), int(crop["row_stop"]))
        column_slice = slice(int(crop["column_start"]), int(crop["column_stop"]))
        total_capacity = float(aggregate["total_capacity_mw"])
        with h5py.File(self.source_h5, "r") as handle:
            if int(handle.attrs.get("schema_version", -1)) != 3:
                raise ValueError("Response cache requires the source schema-v3 HDF5")
            nwp_values = handle["nwp/values"]
            for split in SPLITS:
                output = root / split
                output.mkdir()
                sample_group = handle[f"splits/{split}"]
                cycle_group = handle[f"stage1_splits/{split}"]
                aggregate_dir = self.aggregate_cache / split
                aggregate_sample = _load(aggregate_dir / "sample_index.npy")
                source_sample = np.asarray(sample_group["sample_index"], dtype=np.int64)
                if not np.array_equal(aggregate_sample, source_sample):
                    raise ValueError(f"Aggregate/source sample order mismatch for {split}")
                aggregate_cycle_ids = _load(aggregate_dir / "cycle_sample_index.npy")
                source_cycle_ids = np.asarray(cycle_group["sample_index"], dtype=np.int64)
                if not np.array_equal(aggregate_cycle_ids, source_cycle_ids):
                    raise ValueError(f"Aggregate/source cycle order mismatch for {split}")
                sample_cycle_ids = np.asarray(sample_group["stage1_cycle_index"], dtype=np.int64)
                cycle_index = np.searchsorted(source_cycle_ids, sample_cycle_ids)
                if (
                    np.any(cycle_index >= len(source_cycle_ids))
                    or not np.array_equal(source_cycle_ids[cycle_index], sample_cycle_ids)
                ):
                    raise ValueError(f"Response cycle mapping is invalid for {split}")

                frame_index = np.asarray(
                    cycle_group["nwp_frame_index"][:, :HISTORY_NWP_NODES], dtype=np.int64
                )
                valid_unix = np.asarray(
                    cycle_group["nwp_valid_unix"][:, :HISTORY_NWP_NODES], dtype=np.int64
                )
                issue_unix = np.asarray(
                    cycle_group["nwp_issue_unix"][:, :HISTORY_NWP_NODES], dtype=np.int64
                )
                lead_index = np.asarray(
                    cycle_group["nwp_lead_index"][:, :HISTORY_NWP_NODES], dtype=np.int64
                )
                origins = _load(aggregate_dir / "origin_unix.npy")
                sample_valid = valid_unix[cycle_index]
                sample_issue = issue_unix[cycle_index]
                if not np.all(sample_issue <= sample_valid):
                    raise ValueError(f"Historical Pangu issue is later than validity for {split}")
                if not np.all(sample_valid <= origins[:, None]):
                    raise ValueError(f"Historical Pangu validity exceeds origin for {split}")
                if not np.all(sample_issue <= origins[:, None]):
                    raise ValueError(f"Historical Pangu issue exceeds origin for {split}")
                if not np.all(np.diff(sample_valid, axis=1) == 3600):
                    raise ValueError(f"Historical Pangu nodes are not hourly for {split}")

                power_factor = np.asarray(sample_group["history_node_power"], dtype=np.float32)
                if power_factor.shape != (len(origins), HISTORY_NWP_NODES):
                    raise ValueError(f"Historical node-power shape is invalid for {split}")
                power_mw = power_factor * total_capacity
                if not np.isfinite(power_mw).all():
                    raise ValueError(f"Historical node power is non-finite for {split}")

                weather_shape = (
                    len(source_cycle_ids), HISTORY_NWP_NODES, int(nwp_values.shape[1]),
                    int(crop["shape"][0]), int(crop["shape"][1]),
                )
                weather_path = output / "past_weather.npy"
                weather = open_memmap(weather_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float32, shape=weather_shape)
                for start in range(0, len(source_cycle_ids), self.chunk_cycles):
                    stop = min(start + self.chunk_cycles, len(source_cycle_ids))
                    indices = frame_index[start:stop]
                    unique, inverse = np.unique(indices, return_inverse=True)
                    frames = np.asarray(nwp_values[unique], dtype=np.float32)
                    frames = frames[:, :, row_slice, column_slice]
                    packed = frames[inverse.reshape(indices.shape)]
                    if not np.isfinite(packed).all():
                        raise ValueError(f"Historical weather contains non-finite values for {split}")
                    weather[start:stop] = packed
                weather.flush()
                del weather

                values = {
                    "past_weather_valid_unix": valid_unix,
                    "past_weather_issue_unix": issue_unix,
                    "past_weather_lead_index": lead_index,
                    "past_weather_cycle_index": cycle_index.astype(np.int64),
                    "past_weather_time_offset_hours": (
                        (sample_valid - origins[:, None]).astype(np.float32) / 3600.0
                    ),
                    "past_cluster_power_mw": power_mw.astype(np.float32),
                    "sample_index": source_sample,
                }
                weather_entry = {
                    "relative_path": f"{split}/past_weather.npy",
                    "shape": list(weather_shape),
                    "dtype": "float32",
                    "sha256": _sha256(weather_path),
                }
                arrays.append(weather_entry)
                for name, value in values.items():
                    path = output / f"{name}.npy"
                    entry = _save(path, value)
                    entry["relative_path"] = f"{split}/{name}.npy"
                    arrays.append(entry)
                split_summary[split] = {
                    "sample_count": int(len(origins)),
                    "cycle_count": int(len(source_cycle_ids)),
                    "past_weather_shape": list(weather_shape[1:]),
                    "past_power_shape": list(power_mw.shape[1:]),
                    "maximum_issue_minus_valid_seconds": int((sample_issue - sample_valid).max()),
                    "maximum_valid_minus_origin_seconds": int((sample_valid - origins[:, None]).max()),
                }
        return {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "task_level": "aggregate_response_state",
            "source_h5": str(self.source_h5),
            "aggregate_cache": str(self.aggregate_cache),
            "aggregate_fingerprint": aggregate["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "preprocess_fingerprint": self._identity(aggregate),
            "station_sample_tensors_present": False,
            "history_nwp_nodes": HISTORY_NWP_NODES,
            "time_protocol": "all historical issue <= valid <= forecast origin",
            "power_storage_unit": "MW",
            "splits": split_summary,
            "arrays": arrays,
        }

    def audit(self, *, verify_checksums: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        with (self.destination / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            manifest = json.load(stream)
        if manifest.get("task_level") != "aggregate_response_state":
            raise ValueError("Wrong response-cache task level")
        if manifest.get("station_sample_tensors_present") is not False:
            raise ValueError("Response cache must not contain station sample tensors")
        checked = 0
        for entry in manifest["arrays"]:
            path = self.destination / entry["relative_path"]
            array = _load(path, mmap=True)
            if list(array.shape) != entry["shape"] or str(array.dtype) != entry["dtype"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Response-cache metadata mismatch: {entry['relative_path']}")
            if verify_checksums and _sha256(path) != entry["sha256"]:
                raise ValueError(f"Response-cache checksum mismatch: {entry['relative_path']}")
            checked += 1
        for split in SPLITS:
            directory = self.destination / split
            if any("station" in path.name for path in directory.glob("*.npy")):
                raise ValueError(f"Station tensor found in response cache: {split}")
            origin = _load(self.aggregate_cache / split / "origin_unix.npy")
            cycle = _load(directory / "past_weather_cycle_index.npy")
            valid = _load(directory / "past_weather_valid_unix.npy")[cycle]
            issue = _load(directory / "past_weather_issue_unix.npy")[cycle]
            if not (np.all(issue <= valid) and np.all(valid <= origin[:, None])):
                raise ValueError(f"Response-cache causal-time audit failed: {split}")
        result = {
            "cache": str(self.destination),
            "preprocess_fingerprint": manifest["preprocess_fingerprint"],
            "arrays_checked": checked,
            "checksums_verified": bool(verify_checksums),
            "station_sample_tensors_present": False,
            "causal_time_protocol": True,
        }
        print(f"[response-cache-audit] {result}", flush=True)
        return result


class AggregateResponseForecastDataset(Dataset[int]):
    """Aggregate samples backed by seed-specific frozen R2 hourly latent caches."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        aggregate_cache: str | Path,
        response_cache: str | Path,
        latent_cache: str | Path,
        split: str,
        *,
        verbose: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        if split not in SPLITS:
            raise ValueError(f"split must be one of {SPLITS}")
        self.aggregate_cache = Path(aggregate_cache).resolve()
        self.response_cache = Path(response_cache).resolve()
        self.latent_cache = Path(latent_cache).resolve()
        with (self.aggregate_cache / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            self.manifest = json.load(stream)
        with (self.response_cache / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            self.response_manifest = json.load(stream)
        with (self.latent_cache / split / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            self.latent_manifest = json.load(stream)
        if self.response_manifest.get("aggregate_fingerprint") != self.manifest.get("preprocess_fingerprint"):
            raise ValueError("Aggregate and response caches have different provenance")
        if self.latent_manifest.get("response_fingerprint") != self.response_manifest.get("preprocess_fingerprint"):
            raise ValueError("Latent and response caches have different provenance")
        if self.latent_manifest.get("split") != split:
            raise ValueError("Latent-cache split mismatch")
        self.total_capacity_mw = float(self.manifest["total_capacity_mw"])
        static = self.aggregate_cache / "static"
        self.static = {
            name: _load(static / f"{name}.npy")
            for name in (
                "weather_latitude", "weather_longitude", "nwp_mean", "nwp_std",
                "nwp_channel_name", "capacity_density",
            )
        }
        aggregate_dir = self.aggregate_cache / split
        response_dir = self.response_cache / split
        self.arrays = {
            name: _load(aggregate_dir / f"{name}.npy")
            for name in (
                "origin_unix", "sample_index", "origin_phase", "nwp_age_minutes",
                "weather_cycle_index", "weather_time_offset_hours", "cluster_history_mw",
                "cluster_future_mw", "cluster_future_capacity_warning",
            )
        }
        self.arrays.update(
            {
                name: _load(response_dir / f"{name}.npy")
                for name in (
                    "past_weather_cycle_index", "past_weather_time_offset_hours",
                    "past_cluster_power_mw",
                )
            }
        )
        if not np.array_equal(
            self.arrays["weather_cycle_index"], self.arrays["past_weather_cycle_index"]
        ):
            raise ValueError("Past/future cycle indices are not aligned")
        self.future_hourly_latent = _load(
            self.latent_cache / split / "future_hourly_latent.npy", mmap=True
        )
        self.past_hourly_latent = _load(
            self.latent_cache / split / "past_hourly_latent.npy", mmap=True
        )
        self.length = len(self.arrays["origin_unix"])
        if any(len(values) != self.length for values in self.arrays.values()):
            raise ValueError("Response-state sample arrays have inconsistent lengths")
        if verbose:
            print(
                f"[aggregate-response-dataset:{split}] samples={self.length} "
                f"future_latent={self.future_hourly_latent.shape} "
                f"past_latent={self.past_hourly_latent.shape}",
                flush=True,
            )

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return self.length

    def __getitem__(self, index: int) -> int:
        return index

    def close(self) -> None:
        for values in (self.future_hourly_latent, self.past_hourly_latent):
            mmap = getattr(values, "_mmap", None)
            if mmap is not None:
                mmap.close()


class AggregateResponseCollator:
    def __init__(self, dataset: AggregateResponseForecastDataset) -> None:
        self.dataset = dataset

    def __call__(self, indices: list[int]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        index = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.int64)
        cycles = np.asarray(self.dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"][index], dtype=np.int64)
        unique, inverse = np.unique(cycles, return_inverse=True)
        batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {
            "future_hourly_weather_latent": torch.from_numpy(
                np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(self.dataset.future_hourly_latent[unique], dtype=np.float32))
            ),
            "weather_batch_index": torch.from_numpy(inverse.astype(np.int64)),
            "past_hourly_weather_latent": torch.from_numpy(
                np.ascontiguousarray(np.asarray(self.dataset.past_hourly_latent[cycles], dtype=np.float32))
            ),
        }
        for name, values in self.dataset.arrays.items():
            selected = np.ascontiguousarray(values[index])
            if name in {"cluster_history_mw", "cluster_future_mw", "past_cluster_power_mw"}:
                key = {
                    "cluster_history_mw": "cluster_history",
                    "cluster_future_mw": "cluster_future",
                    "past_cluster_power_mw": "past_cluster_power",
                }[name]
                batch[key] = torch.from_numpy(
                    selected.astype(np.float32) / self.dataset.total_capacity_mw
                )
            else:
                if selected.dtype == np.bool_:
                    tensor = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.bool_))
                elif np.issubdtype(selected.dtype, np.floating):
                    tensor = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.float32))
                else:
                    tensor = torch.from_numpy(selected.astype(np.int64))
                batch[name] = tensor
        return batch


def create_aggregate_response_loader(
    dataset: AggregateResponseForecastDataset,
    *,
    cycles_per_batch: int,
    shuffle: bool,
    seed: int = 0,
    num_workers: int = 0,
) -> DataLoader[dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    sampler = CycleGroupedBatchSampler(
        dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"], cycles_per_batch, shuffle, seed
    )
    return DataLoader(
        dataset,
        batch_sampler=sampler,
        num_workers=num_workers,
        pin_memory=torch.cuda.is_available(),
        persistent_workers=num_workers > 0,
        collate_fn=AggregateResponseCollator(dataset),
    )


def response_cache_manifest_identity(cache: str | Path) -> str:
    with (Path(cache) / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        return str(json.load(stream)["preprocess_fingerprint"])
```
