```
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator, Sequence

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Dataset, Sampler


SPLITS = ("train", "val", "test")


def _load_manifest(cache_dir: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = cache_dir / "manifest.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Prepared model cache is missing: {path}. Run prepare_model_data.py first."
        )
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
        manifest = json.load(stream)
    if int(manifest.get("schema_version", -1)) != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported model-cache schema: {manifest.get('schema_version')}")
    return manifest


def _load_npy(path: Path, *, mmap: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.load(path, mmap_mode="r" if mmap else None, allow_pickle=False)


def _tensor(values: np.ndarray, dtype: torch.dtype | None = None) -> torch.Tensor:
    array = np.ascontiguousarray(values)
    tensor = torch.from_numpy(array)
    return tensor if dtype is None else tensor.to(dtype=dtype)


class PreparedCache:
    def __init__(self, cache_dir: str | Path) -> None:
        self.cache_dir = Path(cache_dir).resolve()
        self.manifest = _load_manifest(self.cache_dir)
        static_dir = self.cache_dir / "static"
        names = (
            "station_id",
            "station_name",
            "station_latitude",
            "station_longitude",
            "station_capacity_mw",
            "weather_latitude",
            "weather_longitude",
            "nwp_mean",
            "nwp_std",
            "nwp_channel_name",
        )
        self.static = {name: _load_npy(static_dir / f"{name}.npy") for name in names}
        station_count = int(self.manifest["station_count"])
        if self.static["station_capacity_mw"].shape != (station_count,):
            raise ValueError("Prepared station metadata has an inconsistent shape")


class _WeatherStore:
    def __init__(
        self,
        path: Path,
        preload: bool | str,
        preload_max_gib: float,
        verbose: bool,
    ) -> None:
        mmap = _load_npy(path, mmap=True)
        self.nbytes = int(mmap.nbytes)
        setting = str(preload).lower() if isinstance(preload, str) else preload
        should_preload = bool(setting is True or setting in {"1", "true", "yes"})
        if setting == "auto":
            should_preload = self.nbytes <= int(preload_max_gib * 1024**3)
        if setting in {False, "0", "false", "no", "mmap"}:
            should_preload = False
        self.values = np.array(mmap, copy=True) if should_preload else mmap
        self.backend = "ram" if should_preload else "mmap"
        if verbose:
            print(
                f"[weather-store] backend={self.backend}, "
                f"shape={list(self.values.shape)}, size={self.nbytes / 1024**3:.2f} GiB"
            )

    def take(self, indices: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        return np.asarray(self.values[indices], dtype=np.float32)

    def close(self) -> None:
        mmap = getattr(self.values, "_mmap", None)
        if mmap is not None:
            mmap.close()


class ForecastDataset(Dataset[int]):
    """RAM-resident targets and indexed access to unique weather cycles."""

    sample_array_names = (
        "origin_unix",
        "sample_index",
        "origin_phase",
        "nwp_age_minutes",
        "weather_cycle_index",
        "weather_time_offset_hours",
        "station_history",
        "station_future",
        "cluster_history",
        "cluster_future",
        "future_capacity_warning",
    )
    cycle_array_names = (
        "weather_valid_unix",
        "weather_station_target",
        "weather_station_capacity_warning",
        "cycle_sample_index",
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        cache_dir: str | Path,
        split: str,
        *,
        preload_weather: bool | str = "auto",
        preload_weather_max_gib: float = 16.0,
        include_weather: bool = True,
        response_state_cache_dir: str | Path | None = None,
        response_state_donor_mode: str = "none",
        verbose: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        if split not in SPLITS:
            raise ValueError(f"split must be one of {SPLITS}")
        self.cache = PreparedCache(cache_dir)
        self.split = split
        split_dir = self.cache.cache_dir / split
        self.arrays = {
            name: _load_npy(split_dir / f"{name}.npy")
            for name in self.sample_array_names
        }
        self.include_weather = bool(include_weather)
        self.cycle_arrays = (
            {
                name: _load_npy(split_dir / f"{name}.npy")
                for name in self.cycle_array_names
            }
            if self.include_weather
            else {}
        )
        self.weather = (
            _WeatherStore(
                split_dir / "weather.npy",
                preload_weather,
                preload_weather_max_gib,
                verbose,
            )
            if self.include_weather
            else None
        )
        self.length = int(len(self.arrays["origin_unix"]))
        self.teacher_arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
        if response_state_donor_mode not in {"none", "strong_sample"}:
            raise ValueError("response_state_donor_mode must be none or strong_sample")
        self.response_state_donor_mode = response_state_donor_mode
        self.response_state_manifest: dict[str, Any] | None = None
        self.response_state_arrays: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
        self.past_weather: _WeatherStore | None = None
        if response_state_cache_dir is not None:
            state_root = Path(response_state_cache_dir).resolve()
            with (state_root / "manifest.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                self.response_state_manifest = json.load(stream)
            if self.response_state_manifest.get("base_cache_fingerprint") != self.manifest.get(
                "preprocess_fingerprint"
            ):
                raise ValueError("Response-state sidecar and forecast cache have different provenance")
            state_split = state_root / split
            for name in (
                "past_weather_cycle_index",
                "past_weather_time_offset_hours",
                "past_station_power",
                "strong_sample_donor_index",
            ):
                self.response_state_arrays[name] = _load_npy(state_split / f"{name}.npy")
            self.past_weather = _WeatherStore(
                state_split / "past_weather.npy",
                preload_weather,
                preload_weather_max_gib,
                verbose,
            )
            if any(len(value) != self.length for value in self.response_state_arrays.values()):
                raise ValueError("Response-state sample arrays have inconsistent lengths")
        lengths = {name: len(values) for name, values in self.arrays.items()}
        if len(set(lengths.values())) != 1 or self.length == 0:
            raise ValueError(f"Prepared sample arrays have inconsistent lengths: {lengths}")
        if self.include_weather:
            cycle_count = int(len(self.cycle_arrays["cycle_sample_index"]))
            if self.weather is None or len(self.weather.values) != cycle_count:
                raise ValueError("Weather cycles and cycle metadata have different lengths")
            cycle_index = self.arrays["weather_cycle_index"]
            if cycle_index.min() < 0 or cycle_index.max() >= cycle_count:
                raise ValueError("Sample weather_cycle_index falls outside the cycle array")
        else:
            cycle_count = 0
        if verbose:
            print(
                f"[forecast-dataset:{split}] samples={self.length}, cycles={cycle_count}, "
                f"include_weather={self.include_weather}, "
                f"history={list(self.arrays['station_history'].shape[1:])}, "
                f"target={list(self.arrays['station_future'].shape[1:])}"
            )

    @property
    def static(self) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        return self.cache.static

    @property
    def manifest(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.cache.manifest

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return self.length

    def attach_teacher_archive(self, path: str | Path) -> None:
        """Attach aligned ensemble targets used only by the training collator."""
        with np.load(Path(path), allow_pickle=False) as archive:
            required = {
                "sample_index",
                "origin_unix",
                "teacher_station_factor_mean",
                "teacher_station_factor_std",
                "teacher_cluster_factor_mean",
                "teacher_cluster_factor_std",
            }
            missing = required.difference(archive.files)
            if missing:
                raise ValueError(f"Teacher archive is missing fields: {sorted(missing)}")
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(
                archive["sample_index"], self.arrays["sample_index"]
            )
            np.testing.assert_array_equal(
                archive["origin_unix"], self.arrays["origin_unix"]
            )
            arrays = {
                name: np.asarray(archive[name], dtype=np.float32)
                for name in required.difference({"sample_index", "origin_unix"})
            }
        station_shape = self.arrays["station_future"].shape
        cluster_shape = self.arrays["cluster_future"].shape
        if arrays["teacher_station_factor_mean"].shape != station_shape:
            raise ValueError("Teacher station mean has the wrong shape")
        if arrays["teacher_station_factor_std"].shape != station_shape:
            raise ValueError("Teacher station standard deviation has the wrong shape")
        if arrays["teacher_cluster_factor_mean"].shape != cluster_shape:
            raise ValueError("Teacher cluster mean has the wrong shape")
        if arrays["teacher_cluster_factor_std"].shape != cluster_shape:
            raise ValueError("Teacher cluster standard deviation has the wrong shape")
        if any(not np.isfinite(values).all() for values in arrays.values()):
            raise ValueError("Teacher archive contains non-finite values")
        self.teacher_arrays = arrays

    def __getitem__(self, index: int) -> int:
        if index < 0:
            index += self.length
        if index < 0 or index >= self.length:
            raise IndexError(index)
        return index

    def close(self) -> None:
        if self.weather is not None:
            self.weather.close()
        if self.past_weather is not None:
            self.past_weather.close()

    def __del__(self) -> None:
        weather = getattr(self, "weather", None)
        if weather is not None:
            weather.close()
        past_weather = getattr(self, "past_weather", None)
        if past_weather is not None:
            past_weather.close()


def apply_stratified_weather_permutation(
    dataset: ForecastDataset,
    *,
    seed: int,
    power_bins: int = 10,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Replace each sample's weather cycle within an in-distribution stratum."""
    if not dataset.include_weather:
        raise ValueError("Weather permutation requires a weather-enabled dataset")
    if power_bins < 2:
        raise ValueError("power_bins must be at least two")

    origins = np.asarray(dataset.arrays["origin_unix"], dtype=np.int64)
    months = (
        origins.astype("datetime64[s]").astype("datetime64[M]").astype(np.int64)
        % 12
    ) + 1
    phases = np.asarray(dataset.arrays["origin_phase"], dtype=np.int64)
    nwp_age = np.asarray(dataset.arrays["nwp_age_minutes"], dtype=np.int64)
    current_power = np.asarray(
        dataset.arrays["cluster_history"][:, -1], dtype=np.float64
    )
    quantile_edges = np.quantile(
        current_power, np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, power_bins + 1)
    )
    power_bin = np.searchsorted(
        quantile_edges[1:-1], current_power, side="right"
    )
    strata = np.stack((months, phases, nwp_age, power_bin), axis=1)
    _, inverse = np.unique(strata, axis=0, return_inverse=True)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    donor_index = np.empty(len(dataset), dtype=np.int64)
    stratum_sizes: list[int] = []
    for stratum in range(int(inverse.max()) + 1):
        members = np.flatnonzero(inverse == stratum)
        if len(members) < 2:
            raise ValueError(
                "Weather-permutation stratum has fewer than two samples; "
                "use fewer power bins"
            )
        shuffled = members.copy()
        rng.shuffle(shuffled)
        shift = int(rng.integers(1, len(shuffled)))
        donor_index[shuffled] = np.roll(shuffled, shift)
        stratum_sizes.append(int(len(members)))

    if np.any(donor_index == np.arange(len(dataset))):
        raise RuntimeError("Controlled weather permutation contains fixed samples")
    donor_offsets = dataset.arrays["weather_time_offset_hours"][donor_index]
    np.testing.assert_allclose(
        donor_offsets,
        dataset.arrays["weather_time_offset_hours"],
        atol=0.0,
        rtol=0.0,
    )
    original_cycle = np.asarray(
        dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"], dtype=np.int64
    )
    permuted_cycle = original_cycle[donor_index].copy()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(
        np.sort(permuted_cycle), np.sort(original_cycle)
    )
    same_cycle = int(np.sum(permuted_cycle == original_cycle))
    if same_cycle != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Controlled weather permutation retained one or more original cycles"
        )
    dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"] = permuted_cycle
    checksum = hashlib.sha256(donor_index.tobytes()).hexdigest()
    return {
        "seed": int(seed),
        "power_bins": int(power_bins),
        "stratification": "UTC_month x origin_phase x NWP_age x current_power_quantile",
        "strata": len(stratum_sizes),
        "minimum_stratum_size": min(stratum_sizes),
        "maximum_stratum_size": max(stratum_sizes),
        "fixed_samples": int(np.sum(donor_index == np.arange(len(dataset)))),
        "same_weather_cycles": same_cycle,
        "donor_index_sha256": checksum,
        "power_quantile_edges": quantile_edges.tolist(),
    }


class SpatialPretrainDataset(Dataset[int]):
    def __init__(
        self,
        cache_dir: str | Path,
        split: str,
        *,
        preload_weather: bool | str = "auto",
        preload_weather_max_gib: float = 16.0,
        verbose: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        if split not in SPLITS:
            raise ValueError(f"split must be one of {SPLITS}")
        self.cache = PreparedCache(cache_dir)
        self.split = split
        split_dir = self.cache.cache_dir / split
        self.weather = _WeatherStore(
            split_dir / "weather.npy",
            preload_weather,
            preload_weather_max_gib,
            verbose,
        )
        self.target = _load_npy(split_dir / "weather_station_target.npy")
        self.warning = _load_npy(split_dir / "weather_station_capacity_warning.npy")
        self.valid_unix = _load_npy(split_dir / "weather_valid_unix.npy")
        self.cycle_sample_index = _load_npy(split_dir / "cycle_sample_index.npy")
        self.length = int(len(self.target))
        if not all(
            len(values) == self.length
            for values in (self.weather.values, self.warning, self.valid_unix, self.cycle_sample_index)
        ):
            raise ValueError("Spatial-pretraining cycle arrays have inconsistent lengths")
        if verbose:
            print(
                f"[spatial-dataset:{split}] cycles={self.length}, "
                f"weather={list(self.weather.values.shape[1:])}, "
                f"target={list(self.target.shape[1:])}"
            )

    @property
    def static(self) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        return self.cache.static

    @property
    def manifest(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.cache.manifest

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return self.length

    def __getitem__(self, index: int) -> int:
        if index < 0:
            index += self.length
        if index < 0 or index >= self.length:
            raise IndexError(index)
        return index

    def close(self) -> None:
        self.weather.close()

    def __del__(self) -> None:
        weather = getattr(self, "weather", None)
        if weather is not None:
            weather.close()


class CycleGroupedBatchSampler(Sampler[list[int]]):
    """Shuffle weather cycles while keeping their quarter-hour origins together."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        cycle_index: Sequence[int],
        cycles_per_batch: int,
        shuffle: bool,
        seed: int = 0,
    ) -> None:
        if cycles_per_batch < 1:
            raise ValueError("cycles_per_batch must be positive")
        values = np.asarray(cycle_index, dtype=np.int64)
        if values.ndim != 1 or not len(values):
            raise ValueError("cycle_index must be a non-empty vector")
        self.cycles_per_batch = cycles_per_batch
        self.shuffle = shuffle
        self.seed = int(seed)
        self.epoch = 0
        self.unique_cycles = np.unique(values)
        self.members = {
            int(cycle): np.flatnonzero(values == cycle).astype(np.int64)
            for cycle in self.unique_cycles
        }

    def set_epoch(self, epoch: int) -> None:
        self.epoch = int(epoch)

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        return (len(self.unique_cycles) + self.cycles_per_batch - 1) // self.cycles_per_batch

    def __iter__(self) -> Iterator[list[int]]:
        cycles = self.unique_cycles.copy()
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + self.epoch)
        if self.shuffle:
            rng.shuffle(cycles)
        for start in range(0, len(cycles), self.cycles_per_batch):
            selected = cycles[start : start + self.cycles_per_batch]
            batch = np.concatenate([self.members[int(cycle)] for cycle in selected])
            if self.shuffle:
                rng.shuffle(batch)
            yield batch.tolist()


class ForecastCollator:
    def __init__(self, dataset: ForecastDataset) -> None:
        self.dataset = dataset

    def __call__(self, indices: list[int]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        sample_index = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.int64)
        batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}
        if self.dataset.include_weather:
            sample_cycles = np.asarray(
                self.dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"][sample_index], dtype=np.int64
            )
            unique_cycles, weather_batch_index = np.unique(
                sample_cycles, return_inverse=True
            )
            if self.dataset.weather is None:
                raise RuntimeError("Weather storage is unavailable")
            batch.update(
                weather=_tensor(
                    self.dataset.weather.take(unique_cycles), torch.float32
                ),
                weather_batch_index=_tensor(weather_batch_index, torch.long),
                weather_station_target=_tensor(
                    self.dataset.cycle_arrays["weather_station_target"][unique_cycles],
                    torch.float32,
                ),
                weather_station_capacity_warning=_tensor(
                    self.dataset.cycle_arrays[
                        "weather_station_capacity_warning"
                    ][unique_cycles],
                    torch.bool,
                ),
            )
        for name, values in self.dataset.arrays.items():
            selected = values[sample_index]
            if selected.dtype == np.bool_:
                dtype = torch.bool
            elif np.issubdtype(selected.dtype, np.floating):
                dtype = torch.float32
            else:
                dtype = torch.long
            batch[name] = _tensor(selected, dtype)
        for name, values in self.dataset.teacher_arrays.items():
            batch[name] = _tensor(values[sample_index], torch.float32)
        if self.dataset.response_state_manifest is not None:
            state_arrays = self.dataset.response_state_arrays
            state_index = sample_index
            if self.dataset.response_state_donor_mode == "strong_sample":
                state_index = np.asarray(
                    state_arrays["strong_sample_donor_index"][sample_index], dtype=np.int64
                )
            batch["state_station_history"] = _tensor(
                self.dataset.arrays["station_history"][state_index], torch.float32
            )
            batch["state_cluster_history"] = _tensor(
                self.dataset.arrays["cluster_history"][state_index], torch.float32
            )
            batch["past_station_power"] = _tensor(
                state_arrays["past_station_power"][state_index], torch.float32
            )
            past_cycles = np.asarray(
                state_arrays["past_weather_cycle_index"][state_index], dtype=np.int64
            )
            unique_past, past_batch_index = np.unique(past_cycles, return_inverse=True)
            if self.dataset.past_weather is None:
                raise RuntimeError("Historical response-state weather is unavailable")
            batch["past_weather"] = _tensor(
                self.dataset.past_weather.take(unique_past), torch.float32
            )
            batch["past_weather_batch_index"] = _tensor(past_batch_index, torch.long)
            batch["past_weather_time_offset_hours"] = _tensor(
                state_arrays["past_weather_time_offset_hours"][state_index], torch.float32
            )
            batch["response_state_sample_index"] = _tensor(state_index, torch.long)
        return batch


class SpatialPretrainCollator:
    def __init__(self, dataset: SpatialPretrainDataset) -> None:
        self.dataset = dataset

    def __call__(self, indices: list[int]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        index = np.asarray(indices, dtype=np.int64)
        return {
            "weather": _tensor(self.dataset.weather.take(index), torch.float32),
            "station_target": _tensor(self.dataset.target[index], torch.float32),
            "capacity_warning": _tensor(self.dataset.warning[index], torch.bool),
            "weather_valid_unix": _tensor(self.dataset.valid_unix[index], torch.long),
            "cycle_sample_index": _tensor(
                self.dataset.cycle_sample_index[index], torch.long
            ),
        }


def _loader_kwargs(num_workers: int, prefetch_factor: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if num_workers < 0 or prefetch_factor < 1:
        raise ValueError("num_workers must be non-negative and prefetch_factor positive")
    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
        "num_workers": num_workers,
        "pin_memory": torch.cuda.is_available(),
        "persistent_workers": num_workers > 0,
    }
    if num_workers > 0:
        kwargs["prefetch_factor"] = prefetch_factor
    return kwargs


def create_forecast_loader(
    dataset: ForecastDataset,
    *,
    cycles_per_batch: int,
    shuffle: bool,
    seed: int = 0,
    num_workers: int = 0,
    prefetch_factor: int = 2,
) -> DataLoader[dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    if not dataset.include_weather:
        generator = torch.Generator()
        generator.manual_seed(int(seed))
        return DataLoader(
            dataset,
            batch_size=4 * cycles_per_batch,
            shuffle=shuffle,
            generator=generator,
            collate_fn=ForecastCollator(dataset),
            **_loader_kwargs(num_workers, prefetch_factor),
        )
    sampler = CycleGroupedBatchSampler(
        dataset.arrays["weather_cycle_index"], cycles_per_batch, shuffle, seed
    )
    return DataLoader(
        dataset,
        batch_sampler=sampler,
        collate_fn=ForecastCollator(dataset),
        **_loader_kwargs(num_workers, prefetch_factor),
    )


def create_spatial_pretrain_loader(
    dataset: SpatialPretrainDataset,
    *,
    batch_size: int,
    shuffle: bool,
    num_workers: int = 0,
    prefetch_factor: int = 2,
) -> DataLoader[dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    return DataLoader(
        dataset,
        batch_size=batch_size,
        shuffle=shuffle,
        collate_fn=SpatialPretrainCollator(dataset),
        **_loader_kwargs(num_workers, prefetch_factor),
    )
```

```
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CropSpec:
    row_start: int
    row_stop: int
    column_start: int
    column_stop: int
    north: float
    south: float
    west: float
    east: float
    resolution: float
    requested_padding_grid_points: int

    @property
    def height(self) -> int:
        return self.row_stop - self.row_start

    @property
    def width(self) -> int:
        return self.column_stop - self.column_start


def _decode_strings(values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    return np.asarray(
        [value.decode("utf-8") if isinstance(value, bytes) else str(value) for value in values],
        dtype=str,
    )


def _exact_axis_index(axis: np.ndarray, value: float) -> int:
    matches = np.flatnonzero(np.isclose(axis, value, atol=1e-8, rtol=0.0))
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise ValueError(f"Requested crop boundary {value:.8f} is absent from source axis")
    return int(matches[0])


def compute_station_envelope_crop(
    latitude: Sequence[float],
    longitude: Sequence[float],
    station_latitude: Sequence[float],
    station_longitude: Sequence[float],
    padding_grid_points: int,
) -> CropSpec:
    lat = np.asarray(latitude, dtype=np.float64)
    lon = np.asarray(longitude, dtype=np.float64)
    station_lat = np.asarray(station_latitude, dtype=np.float64)
    station_lon = np.asarray(station_longitude, dtype=np.float64)
    if padding_grid_points < 0:
        raise ValueError("padding_grid_points must be non-negative")
    if lat.ndim != 1 or lon.ndim != 1 or len(lat) < 2 or len(lon) < 2:
        raise ValueError("Weather coordinates must be non-trivial one-dimensional axes")
    lat_step = np.diff(lat)
    lon_step = np.diff(lon)
    resolution = float(abs(lat_step[0]))
    if not (
        np.allclose(lat_step, -resolution)
        and np.allclose(lon_step, resolution)
        and np.isclose(abs(lon_step[0]), resolution)
    ):
        raise ValueError("Expected descending latitude and ascending longitude grids")
    if not (
        np.isfinite(station_lat).all()
        and np.isfinite(station_lon).all()
        and station_lat.size
        and station_lat.shape == station_lon.shape
    ):
        raise ValueError("Station coordinates are invalid")

    north = (
        math.ceil(float(station_lat.max()) / resolution) * resolution
        + padding_grid_points * resolution
    )
    south = (
        math.floor(float(station_lat.min()) / resolution) * resolution
        - padding_grid_points * resolution
    )
    west = (
        math.floor(float(station_lon.min()) / resolution) * resolution
        - padding_grid_points * resolution
    )
    east = (
        math.ceil(float(station_lon.max()) / resolution) * resolution
        + padding_grid_points * resolution
    )
    row_start = _exact_axis_index(lat, north)
    row_stop = _exact_axis_index(lat, south) + 1
    column_start = _exact_axis_index(lon, west)
    column_stop = _exact_axis_index(lon, east) + 1
    return CropSpec(
        row_start=row_start,
        row_stop=row_stop,
        column_start=column_start,
        column_stop=column_stop,
        north=north,
        south=south,
        west=west,
        east=east,
        resolution=resolution,
        requested_padding_grid_points=padding_grid_points,
    )


def _save_array(directory: Path, name: str, values: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, Any]:
    path = directory / f"{name}.npy"
    np.save(path, values, allow_pickle=False)
    return {"file": path.name, "shape": list(values.shape), "dtype": str(values.dtype)}


class ModelDataPreparer:
    """Build model-ready arrays without spatial or temporal interpolation."""

    def __init__(self, data_config: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.config = dict(data_config)
        self.source = Path(str(self.config["source_h5"])).resolve()
        self.destination = Path(str(self.config["cache_dir"])).resolve()
        self.padding = int(self.config.get("region_padding_grid_points", 5))
        self.history_power_steps = int(self.config.get("history_power_steps", 24))
        self.forecast_power_steps = int(self.config.get("forecast_power_steps", 24))
        self.history_nwp_nodes = int(self.config.get("history_nwp_nodes", 6))
        self.future_nwp_nodes = int(self.config.get("future_nwp_nodes", 8))
        self.chunk_cycles = int(self.config.get("cache_chunk_cycles", 32))
        self.weather_dtype = np.dtype(str(self.config.get("weather_storage_dtype", "float32")))
        if self.weather_dtype not in (np.dtype("float32"), np.dtype("float16")):
            raise ValueError("weather_storage_dtype must be float32 or float16")
        if min(
            self.history_power_steps,
            self.forecast_power_steps,
            self.future_nwp_nodes,
            self.chunk_cycles,
        ) < 1:
            raise ValueError("Window lengths and cache_chunk_cycles must be positive")

    def _identity(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        stat = self.source.stat()
        relevant = {
            "region_padding_grid_points": self.padding,
            "history_power_steps": self.history_power_steps,
            "forecast_power_steps": self.forecast_power_steps,
            "history_nwp_nodes": self.history_nwp_nodes,
            "future_nwp_nodes": self.future_nwp_nodes,
            "weather_storage_dtype": str(self.weather_dtype),
        }
        fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(
            json.dumps(relevant, sort_keys=True).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        return {
            "source_h5": str(self.source),
            "source_size_bytes": int(stat.st_size),
            "source_mtime_ns": int(stat.st_mtime_ns),
            "preprocess_fingerprint": fingerprint,
        }

    def is_current(self) -> bool:
        manifest_path = self.destination / "manifest.json"
        if not manifest_path.is_file():
            return False
        try:
            with manifest_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                manifest = json.load(stream)
            if any(manifest.get(key) != value for key, value in self._identity().items()):
                return False
            for entry in manifest["arrays"]:
                path = self.destination / entry["relative_path"]
                array = np.load(path, mmap_mode="r", allow_pickle=False)
                if list(array.shape) != entry["shape"] or str(array.dtype) != entry["dtype"]:
                    return False
            return True
        except (KeyError, OSError, TypeError, ValueError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            return False

    def build(self) -> Path:
        if not self.source.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Source HDF5 does not exist: {self.source}")
        if self.is_current():
            print(f"[prepare] Reusing current model cache: {self.destination}")
            return self.destination
        if self.destination.exists():
            raise FileExistsError(
                f"A stale or incomplete cache already exists: {self.destination}. "
                "Move it aside before rebuilding."
            )

        self.destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        temporary = Path(
            tempfile.mkdtemp(
                prefix=f".{self.destination.name}.tmp.", dir=self.destination.parent
            )
        )
        try:
            manifest = self._build_into(temporary)
            with (temporary / "manifest.json").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
                json.dump(manifest, stream, ensure_ascii=True, indent=2)
            os.replace(temporary, self.destination)
        except Exception:
            shutil.rmtree(temporary, ignore_errors=True)
            raise
        print(f"[prepare] Model cache ready: {self.destination}")
        return self.destination

    def _build_into(self, output_root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
        arrays: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        with h5py.File(self.source, "r") as handle:
            if not bool(handle["nwp"].attrs.get("values_are_prestandardized", False)):
                raise ValueError("Expected pre-standardized NWP values in source HDF5")
            station_lat = np.asarray(handle["stations/latitude"], dtype=np.float64)
            station_lon = np.asarray(handle["stations/longitude"], dtype=np.float64)
            capacity = np.asarray(handle["stations/capacity_mw"], dtype=np.float32)
            total_capacity = float(handle["stations"].attrs["total_capacity_mw"])
            source_lat = np.asarray(handle["crop/latitude"], dtype=np.float64)
            source_lon = np.asarray(handle["crop/longitude"], dtype=np.float64)
            crop = compute_station_envelope_crop(
                source_lat, source_lon, station_lat, station_lon, self.padding
            )
            if len(capacity) != 56 or not np.isclose(capacity.sum(), total_capacity, atol=1e-3):
                raise ValueError("Station capacity metadata is inconsistent")

            static_dir = output_root / "static"
            static_dir.mkdir()
            static_values = {
                "station_id": _decode_strings(np.asarray(handle["stations/id"])),
                "station_name": _decode_strings(np.asarray(handle["stations/name"])),
                "station_latitude": station_lat.astype(np.float32),
                "station_longitude": station_lon.astype(np.float32),
                "station_capacity_mw": capacity,
                "weather_latitude": source_lat[crop.row_start : crop.row_stop],
                "weather_longitude": source_lon[crop.column_start : crop.column_stop],
                "nwp_mean": np.asarray(handle["normalization/mean"], dtype=np.float32),
                "nwp_std": np.asarray(handle["normalization/std"], dtype=np.float32),
                "nwp_channel_name": _decode_strings(
                    np.asarray(handle["normalization/channel_short_name"])
                ),
            }
            for name, values in static_values.items():
                entry = _save_array(static_dir, name, values)
                arrays.append({"relative_path": f"static/{entry['file']}", **entry})

            power_time = np.asarray(handle["power/timestamp_unix"], dtype=np.int64)
            station_power = np.asarray(handle["power/station_mw"], dtype=np.float32)
            capacity_warning = np.asarray(handle["power/capacity_warning"], dtype=bool)
            split_summaries: dict[str, Any] = {}
            for split in SPLITS:
                summary, split_entries = self._build_split(
                    handle,
                    split,
                    output_root / split,
                    crop,
                    power_time,
                    station_power,
                    capacity_warning,
                    capacity,
                    total_capacity,
                )
                split_summaries[split] = summary
                arrays.extend(split_entries)

        return {
            "schema_version": 1,
            **self._identity(),
            "no_spatial_interpolation": True,
            "no_temporal_interpolation": True,
            "weather_values_are_prestandardized": True,
            "crop": {**asdict(crop), "shape": [crop.height, crop.width]},
            "station_count": int(len(capacity)),
            "total_capacity_mw": total_capacity,
            "history_power_steps": self.history_power_steps,
            "forecast_power_steps": self.forecast_power_steps,
            "future_nwp_nodes": self.future_nwp_nodes,
            "target_lead_hours": (
                np.arange(1, self.forecast_power_steps + 1, dtype=np.float64) / 4.0
            ).tolist(),
            "splits": split_summaries,
            "arrays": arrays,
        }

    def _build_split(
        self,
        handle: h5py.File,
        split: str,
        output_dir: Path,
        crop: CropSpec,
        power_time: np.ndarray,
        station_power: np.ndarray,
        capacity_warning: np.ndarray,
        capacity: np.ndarray,
        total_capacity: float,
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
        output_dir.mkdir()
        entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        cycle_group = handle[f"stage1_splits/{split}"]
        sample_group = handle[f"splits/{split}"]
        cycle_ids = np.asarray(cycle_group["sample_index"], dtype=np.int64)
        sample_cycle_ids = np.asarray(sample_group["stage1_cycle_index"], dtype=np.int64)
        local_cycle_index = np.searchsorted(cycle_ids, sample_cycle_ids)
        if (
            np.any(local_cycle_index >= len(cycle_ids))
            or not np.array_equal(cycle_ids[local_cycle_index], sample_cycle_ids)
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"Sample-to-cycle mapping is inconsistent for {split}")

        node_start = self.history_nwp_nodes
        node_stop = node_start + self.future_nwp_nodes
        if node_stop > cycle_group["nwp_frame_index"].shape[1]:
            raise ValueError("Requested future NWP nodes exceed the source cycle window")
        frame_index = cycle_group["nwp_frame_index"][:, node_start:node_stop]
        valid_unix = np.asarray(
            cycle_group["nwp_valid_unix"][:, node_start:node_stop], dtype=np.int64
        )
        nwp_store = handle["nwp/values"]
        weather_shape = (
            len(cycle_ids),
            self.future_nwp_nodes,
            int(nwp_store.shape[1]),
            crop.height,
            crop.width,
        )
        weather_path = output_dir / "weather.npy"
        weather = open_memmap(
            weather_path, mode="w+", dtype=self.weather_dtype, shape=weather_shape
        )
        with tqdm(total=len(cycle_ids), desc=f"Preparing weather ({split})", unit="cycle") as bar:
            for start in range(0, len(cycle_ids), self.chunk_cycles):
                stop = min(start + self.chunk_cycles, len(cycle_ids))
                indices = np.asarray(frame_index[start:stop], dtype=np.int64)
                unique, inverse = np.unique(indices, return_inverse=True)
                values = np.asarray(nwp_store[unique], dtype=np.float32)
                values = values[
                    :,
                    :,
                    crop.row_start : crop.row_stop,
                    crop.column_start : crop.column_stop,
                ]
                packed = values[inverse.reshape(indices.shape)]
                if not np.isfinite(packed).all():
                    raise ValueError(f"Non-finite weather values encountered in {split}")
                weather[start:stop] = packed.astype(self.weather_dtype, copy=False)
                bar.update(stop - start)
        weather.flush()
        del weather
        entries.append(
            {
                "relative_path": f"{split}/weather.npy",
                "file": "weather.npy",
                "shape": list(weather_shape),
                "dtype": str(self.weather_dtype),
            }
        )

        valid_positions = np.searchsorted(power_time, valid_unix)
        if (
            np.any(valid_positions >= len(power_time))
            or not np.array_equal(power_time[valid_positions], valid_unix)
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"NWP valid times do not align with station power for {split}")
        weather_target = (
            station_power[valid_positions] / capacity.reshape(1, 1, -1)
        ).transpose(0, 2, 1).astype(np.float32)
        weather_warning = capacity_warning[valid_positions].transpose(0, 2, 1)
        if not np.isfinite(weather_target).all():
            raise ValueError(f"Stage-I station targets contain missing values for {split}")

        origins = np.asarray(sample_group["origin_unix"], dtype=np.int64)
        power_start = np.asarray(sample_group["power_start_index"], dtype=np.int64)
        history_offsets = np.arange(self.history_power_steps, dtype=np.int64)
        future_offsets = self.history_power_steps + np.arange(
            self.forecast_power_steps, dtype=np.int64
        )
        history_positions = power_start[:, None] + history_offsets
        future_positions = power_start[:, None] + future_offsets
        station_history = (
            station_power[history_positions] / capacity.reshape(1, 1, -1)
        ).transpose(0, 2, 1).astype(np.float32)
        station_future = (
            station_power[future_positions] / capacity.reshape(1, 1, -1)
        ).transpose(0, 2, 1).astype(np.float32)
        future_warning = capacity_warning[future_positions].transpose(0, 2, 1)
        if not np.isfinite(station_history).all() or not np.isfinite(station_future).all():
            raise ValueError(f"Accepted sample power contains missing values for {split}")
        cluster_history = np.asarray(sample_group["history_power"], dtype=np.float32)
        cluster_future = np.asarray(sample_group["future_power"], dtype=np.float32)
        reconstructed = (
            station_future * capacity.reshape(1, -1, 1)
        ).sum(axis=1) / total_capacity
        if not np.allclose(reconstructed, cluster_future, atol=2e-6, rtol=1e-6):
            raise ValueError(f"Station and cluster future targets disagree for {split}")

        sample_values = {
            "origin_unix": origins,
            "sample_index": np.asarray(sample_group["sample_index"], dtype=np.int64),
            "origin_phase": np.asarray(sample_group["origin_phase"], dtype=np.int64),
            "nwp_age_minutes": np.asarray(sample_group["nwp_age_minutes"], dtype=np.int64),
            "weather_cycle_index": local_cycle_index.astype(np.int64),
            "weather_time_offset_hours": (
                valid_unix[local_cycle_index] - origins[:, None]
            ).astype(np.float32)
            / 3600.0,
            "station_history": station_history,
            "station_future": station_future,
            "cluster_history": cluster_history,
            "cluster_future": cluster_future,
            "future_capacity_warning": future_warning,
        }
        cycle_values = {
            "weather_valid_unix": valid_unix,
            "weather_station_target": weather_target,
            "weather_station_capacity_warning": weather_warning,
            "cycle_sample_index": cycle_ids,
        }
        for name, values in {**sample_values, **cycle_values}.items():
            entry = _save_array(output_dir, name, values)
            entries.append(
                {"relative_path": f"{split}/{entry['file']}", **entry}
            )
        return (
            {
                "sample_count": int(len(origins)),
                "cycle_count": int(len(cycle_ids)),
                "weather_shape": list(weather_shape[1:]),
                "station_history_shape": list(station_history.shape[1:]),
                "station_future_shape": list(station_future.shape[1:]),
            },
            entries,
        )
```

```
"""Fail-closed 2024 access for the registered farm-enhancement experiment."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for block in iter(lambda: stream.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(block)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def read_freeze(root: Path) -> dict:
    path = root / "selection/validation_freeze.json"
    if not path.is_file():
        raise RuntimeError("2024 access blocked: validation freeze is missing")
    freeze = json.loads(path.read_text())
    if (freeze.get("protocol_version") not in {2, 3}
            or freeze.get("test_results_read") is not False
            or freeze.get("selected_spec", {}).get("context") == "id_film"
            or not freeze.get("registered_runs")
            or not freeze.get("train_q90_ramp_thresholds")):
        raise RuntimeError("Invalid farm-enhancement validation freeze")
    snapshot = json.loads((root / "code_snapshot.json").read_text())
    if freeze.get("code_sha256") != snapshot["sha256"]:
        raise RuntimeError("Freeze/code snapshot mismatch")
    return freeze


def require_test_authorized(config: dict, checkpoint: str | Path) -> None:
    experiment = config.get("experiment", {})
    checkpoint = Path(checkpoint).resolve()
    path_root = next((p for p in checkpoint.parents if p.name == "farm_interaction_enhancement_v1"), None)
    if not (path_root is not None or str(experiment.get("id", "")).startswith("farm_interaction_enhancement_v1")
            or config.get("model", {}).get("response_output_mode") in {"cf_residual", "bounded_cf_mix"}):
        return
    root = path_root if path_root is not None else Path(experiment["protocol_root"])
    freeze = read_freeze(root)
    for record in freeze["registered_runs"]:
        registered = root / record["checkpoint"]
        if registered.resolve() == checkpoint:
            if sha256(checkpoint) != record["checkpoint_sha256"]:
                raise RuntimeError("Frozen checkpoint changed")
            if "config_sha256" in record:
                import yaml
                config_path = registered.parent / "config.yaml"
                if sha256(config_path) != record["config_sha256"]:
                    raise RuntimeError("Frozen config changed")
                supplied = {k:v for k,v in config.items() if not k.startswith("_")}
                if supplied != yaml.safe_load(config_path.read_text()):
                    raise RuntimeError("Evaluation config differs from frozen config")
            if "validation_metrics_sha256" in record:
                if sha256(registered.parent / "metrics_val.json") != record["validation_metrics_sha256"]:
                    raise RuntimeError("Frozen validation metrics changed")
            return
    raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint is not registered in the validation freeze")
```

```
def forecast_model_uses_weather(model_config):
    architecture = str(model_config.get("architecture", "forced_evolution"))
    if architecture.startswith("history_"):
        return False
    if architecture == "forced_evolution":
        return str(model_config.get("fusion_mode", "state_conditioned")) != "history_only"
    return True


def build_forecast_model(
    model_config,
    static,
    *,
    history_steps=24,
    forecast_steps=24,
):
    architecture = str(model_config.get("architecture", "forced_evolution"))
    if architecture == "p0i_condition_transfer":
        from wind_forcing.models.p0i_transfer import P0IConditionTransfer
        return P0IConditionTransfer(model_config, static, forecast_steps)
    if architecture == "forced_evolution":
        return build_forced_evolution_model(
            model_config,
            static,
            history_steps=history_steps,
            forecast_steps=forecast_steps,
        )
    if architecture == "controlled_transition":
        return build_controlled_transition_model(
            model_config,
            static,
            history_steps=history_steps,
            forecast_steps=forecast_steps,
        )
    if architecture == "incremental_correction":
        return build_incremental_correction_model(
            model_config,
            static,
            history_steps=history_steps,
            forecast_steps=forecast_steps,
        )
    if architecture == "local_to_global":
        return build_local_to_global_model(
            model_config,
            static,
            history_steps=history_steps,
            forecast_steps=forecast_steps,
        )
    if architecture == "local_state_identifiability":
        return build_local_state_identifiability_model(
            model_config,
            static,
            history_steps=history_steps,
            forecast_steps=forecast_steps,
        )
    if architecture.startswith("history_"):
        return build_history_baseline(
            model_config, static, history_steps, forecast_steps
        )
    if architecture.startswith("weather_"):
        return build_weather_baseline(model_config, static, forecast_steps)
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported model.architecture: {architecture}")

__all__ = [
    "ForcedEvolutionModel",
    "ControlledTransitionModel",
    "IncrementalCorrectionModel",
    "LocalToGlobalModel",
    "LocalStateIdentifiabilityModel",
    "SpatialPretrainModel",

    "build_forced_evolution_model",

    "build_spatial_pretrain_model",
    "forecast_model_uses_weather",
    "build_aggregate_model",
]
```

```
from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import math
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

import torch
from torch import nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from wind_forcing.models.baselines import DLinearHead
from wind_forcing.models.components import CausalResidualBlock, inverse_softplus


def _heads(d_model: int, requested: int) -> int:
    value = min(int(requested), d_model)
    while value > 1 and d_model % value:
        value -= 1
    return value


class LeadEmbedding(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model: int, forecast_steps: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        leads = torch.arange(1, forecast_steps + 1, dtype=torch.float32) / 4.0
        self.register_buffer("lead_hours", leads)
        self.network = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(5, d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d_model, d_model), nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        )

    def forward(self, batch: int, *, dtype: torch.dtype, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor:
        lead = self.lead_hours.to(device=device, dtype=dtype)
        angle = lead * (2.0 * math.pi / 24.0)
        feature = torch.stack(
            (lead / 6.0, torch.sin(angle), torch.cos(angle), torch.sin(4 * angle), torch.cos(4 * angle)),
            dim=-1,
        )
        return self.network(feature).unsqueeze(0).expand(batch, -1, -1)


def history_features(history: torch.Tensor, visible_steps: int) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    if history.ndim != 2 or not 1 <= visible_steps <= history.shape[1]:
        raise ValueError("Invalid aggregate history or visible window")
    batch, steps = history.shape
    mask = torch.arange(steps, device=history.device) >= steps - visible_steps
    mask = mask.reshape(1, steps).expand(batch, -1)
    values = history * mask.to(history)
    delta = torch.zeros_like(history)
    if visible_steps > 1:
        delta[:, -visible_steps + 1 :] = (
            history[:, -visible_steps + 1 :] - history[:, -visible_steps:-1]
        )
    relative = torch.arange(-(steps - 1), 1, device=history.device, dtype=history.dtype) / 4.0
    relative = relative.reshape(1, steps).expand(batch, -1)
    features = torch.stack((values, delta, relative / 6.0, mask.to(history)), dim=-1)
    return features, mask


class AggregateHistoryForecast(nn.Module):
    """Station-free history forecast and reusable B(H) implementation."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        mode: str,
        *,
        total_capacity_mw: float,
        history_steps: int,
        forecast_steps: int,
        visible_steps: int,
        d_model: int,
        layers: int,
        attention_heads: int,
        dropout: float,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if mode not in {"persistence", "dlinear", "tcn", "patchtst"}:
            raise ValueError("Unsupported aggregate history mode")
        self.mode = mode
        self.history_steps = int(history_steps)
        self.forecast_steps = int(forecast_steps)
        self.visible_steps = int(visible_steps)
        self.d_model = int(d_model)
        self.register_buffer("total_capacity_mw", torch.tensor(float(total_capacity_mw)))
        self.zero_gradient_anchor = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(()))
        if mode == "dlinear":
            self.network: nn.Module = DLinearHead(history_steps, forecast_steps)
        elif mode == "tcn":
            self.input_projection = nn.Conv1d(4, d_model, 1)
            self.network = nn.Sequential(
                *[CausalResidualBlock(d_model, 2**layer, dropout) for layer in range(layers)]
            )
            self.head = nn.Linear(d_model, forecast_steps)
            nn.init.zeros_(self.head.weight)
            nn.init.zeros_(self.head.bias)
        elif mode == "patchtst":
            self.patch_length = 4
            self.patch_stride = 2
            count = 1 + (history_steps - self.patch_length) // self.patch_stride
            self.input_projection = nn.Linear(4 * self.patch_length, d_model)
            self.position = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1, count, d_model))
            nn.init.normal_(self.position, std=0.02)
            layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
                d_model,
                _heads(d_model, attention_heads),
                4 * d_model,
                dropout,
                activation="gelu",
                batch_first=True,
                norm_first=True,
            )
            self.network = nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, max(1, layers), nn.LayerNorm(d_model))
            self.head = nn.Linear(d_model, forecast_steps)

    def forward(self, *, cluster_history: torch.Tensor, **_: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if cluster_history.shape[1:] != (self.history_steps,):
            raise ValueError("cluster_history has the wrong shape")
        history_latent: torch.Tensor | None = None
        if self.mode == "persistence":
            prediction = cluster_history[:, -1:].expand(-1, self.forecast_steps)
            prediction = prediction + self.zero_gradient_anchor * 0.0
        else:
            features, _ = history_features(cluster_history, self.visible_steps)
            if self.mode == "dlinear":
                delta = self.network(features[..., 0])
            elif self.mode == "tcn":
                encoded = self.network(self.input_projection(features.transpose(1, 2)))
                history_latent = encoded[:, :, -1]
                delta = self.head(history_latent)
            else:
                patches = features.unfold(1, self.patch_length, self.patch_stride)
                patches = patches.reshape(patches.shape[0], patches.shape[1], -1)
                encoded = self.network(self.input_projection(patches) + self.position)
                delta = self.head(encoded.mean(dim=1))
            prediction = F.softplus(
                inverse_softplus(cluster_history[:, -1:].clamp_min(1e-4)) + delta
            )
        return {
            "cluster_factor": prediction,
            "baseline_cluster_factor": prediction,
            "anchor_baseline_cluster_factor": None,
            "weather_features": None,
            "state_attention": None,
            "weather_attention": None,
            "history_latent": history_latent,
        }

    def encode_latent(self, cluster_history: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Return the representation used by a TCN baseline forecast head."""
        if self.mode != "tcn":
            raise ValueError("A reusable history latent is only defined for the TCN baseline")
        features, _ = history_features(cluster_history, self.visible_steps)
        encoded = self.network(self.input_projection(features.transpose(1, 2)))
        return encoded[:, :, -1]


class RegionalCNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, channels: int, d_model: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        hidden = max(32, d_model // 2)
        self.input = nn.Conv2d(channels, hidden, 3, padding=1)
        self.block1 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.GroupNorm(4, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Conv2d(hidden, hidden, 3, padding=1)
        )
        self.block2 = nn.Sequential(
            nn.GroupNorm(4, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Conv2d(hidden, hidden, 3, padding=1)
        )
        self.output = nn.Sequential(
            nn.GroupNorm(4, hidden), nn.GELU(), nn.Conv2d(hidden, d_model, 1)
        )

    def forward(self, weather: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        leading = weather.shape[:-3]
        frames = weather.reshape(-1, *weather.shape[-3:])
        values = self.input(frames)
        values = values + self.block1(values)
        values = values + self.block2(values)
        return self.output(values).reshape(*leading, -1, *weather.shape[-2:])


class FullPathQuery(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d_model: int, heads: int, dropout: float) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.heads = _heads(d_model, heads)
        self.head_dim = d_model // self.heads
        self.q = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.k = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.v = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.out = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.time_bias = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2, 16), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(16, self.heads))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(
        self,
        query: torch.Tensor,
        tokens: torch.Tensor,
        token_times: torch.Tensor,
        *,
        value_override: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        batch, queries, _ = query.shape
        count = tokens.shape[1]
        q = self.q(query).reshape(batch, queries, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        k = self.k(tokens).reshape(batch, count, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
        if value_override is None:
            v = self.v(tokens).reshape(batch, count, self.heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2)
            v = v[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, queries, -1, -1)
        else:
            v = self.v(value_override).reshape(
                batch, queries, count, self.heads, self.head_dim
            ).permute(0, 3, 1, 2, 4)
        lead = torch.arange(1, queries + 1, device=query.device, dtype=query.dtype) / 4.0
        delta = lead.reshape(1, queries, 1) - token_times[:, None]
        bias = self.time_bias(torch.stack((delta / 6.0, delta.abs() / 6.0), dim=-1))
        score = torch.einsum("bhqd,bhkd->bhqk", q, k) / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        score = score + bias.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)
        attention = torch.softmax(score, dim=-1)
        if value_override is None:
            output = torch.einsum("bhqk,bhqkd->bhqd", self.dropout(attention), v)
        else:
            output = torch.einsum("bhqk,bhqkd->bhqd", self.dropout(attention), v)
        output = output.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, queries, self.d_model)
        return self.out(output), attention.mean(dim=1)


def linear_interpolate(
    values: torch.Tensor, node_times: torch.Tensor, target_times: torch.Tensor
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Sample-specific interpolation with boundary replication, never extrapolation."""
    if values.shape[:2] != node_times.shape:
        raise ValueError("values and node_times disagree")
    batch, nodes = node_times.shape
    targets = target_times.shape[1]
    right = torch.searchsorted(node_times.contiguous(), target_times.contiguous(), right=False)
    right = right.clamp(1, nodes - 1)
    left = right - 1
    left_time = node_times.gather(1, left)
    right_time = node_times.gather(1, right)
    fraction = ((target_times - left_time) / (right_time - left_time).clamp_min(1e-6)).clamp(0, 1)
    tail = values.shape[2:]
    gather_shape = (batch, targets) + (1,) * len(tail)
    expand_shape = (batch, targets) + tail
    left_value = values.gather(1, left.reshape(gather_shape).expand(expand_shape))
    right_value = values.gather(1, right.reshape(gather_shape).expand(expand_shape))
    weight = fraction.reshape(gather_shape)
    before = target_times <= node_times[:, :1]
    after = target_times >= node_times[:, -1:]
    output = left_value * (1.0 - weight) + right_value * weight
    output = torch.where(
        before.reshape(gather_shape), values[:, :1].expand(expand_shape), output
    )
    output = torch.where(
        after.reshape(gather_shape), values[:, -1:].expand(expand_shape), output
    )
    return output


class AggregateWeatherEncoder(nn.Module):
    MODES = {
        "raw_interpolation",
        "latent_interpolation",
        "full_path",
        "target_value",
        "target_value_no_time",
        "dynamic_pool",
        "regional_tokens",
        "patch_regional",
        "joint_spacetime",
        "factorized_spacetime",
    }

    def __init__(
        self,
        mode: str,
        *,
        channels: int,
        d_model: int,
        heads: int,
        dropout: float,
        forecast_steps: int,
        capacity_density: Sequence[Sequence[float]],
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if mode not in self.MODES:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported aggregate weather mode: {mode}")
        self.mode = mode
        self.d_model = int(d_model)
        density = torch.as_tensor(capacity_density, dtype=torch.float32)
        if density.ndim != 2 or not torch.isclose(density.sum(), torch.tensor(1.0), atol=1e-5):
            raise ValueError("capacity_density must be a normalized 2-D map")
        self.register_buffer("capacity_density", density)
        self.lead = LeadEmbedding(d_model, forecast_steps)
        self.path_query = FullPathQuery(d_model, heads, dropout)
        if mode in {"patch_regional", "joint_spacetime", "factorized_spacetime"}:
            self.patch = nn.Conv2d(channels, d_model, kernel_size=(4, 5), stride=(4, 5))
            layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
                d_model, _heads(d_model, heads), 2 * d_model, dropout,
                activation="gelu", batch_first=True, norm_first=True,
            )
            self.patch_encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, 2, nn.LayerNorm(d_model))
            patch_density = F.avg_pool2d(density[None, None], (4, 5), (4, 5)).flatten()
            row = torch.linspace(-1, 1, 6).reshape(6, 1).expand(6, 5).flatten()
            col = torch.linspace(-1, 1, 5).reshape(1, 5).expand(6, 5).flatten()
            self.register_buffer("patch_metadata", torch.stack((row, col, patch_density), dim=-1))
            self.patch_position = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(3, d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d_model, d_model))
        else:
            self.cnn = RegionalCNN(channels, d_model)
        self.region_queries = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(4, d_model) * 0.02)
        self.region_attention = nn.MultiheadAttention(
            d_model, _heads(d_model, heads), dropout=dropout, batch_first=True
        )
        self.value_adapter = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model + 2, 2 * d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(2 * d_model, d_model)
        )
        self.dynamic_score = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model + 3, d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d_model, 1)
        )
        self.factor_time = nn.MultiheadAttention(
            d_model, _heads(d_model, heads), dropout=dropout, batch_first=True
        )
        self.factor_space = nn.MultiheadAttention(
            d_model, _heads(d_model, heads), dropout=dropout, batch_first=True
        )
        # All weather routes pass through the same residual adapter family.  A
        # wider bottleneck on the lighter CNN routes keeps total trainable
        # capacity aligned with the patch routes without changing B or C.
        adapter_hidden = 16 if mode in {
            "patch_regional", "joint_spacetime", "factorized_spacetime"
        } else 512
        self.budget_adapter = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model, adapter_hidden),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(adapter_hidden, d_model),
        )

    def _capacity_pool(self, grid: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.einsum("...dhw,hw->...d", grid, self.capacity_density.to(grid))

    def encode_hourly(self, weather: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Encode native NWP nodes before target-time interpolation.

        This path is intentionally exposed for the frozen, seed-specific latent
        cache used by response-state identification.
        """
        if self.mode != "latent_interpolation":
            raise ValueError("Native hourly latent caching is only defined for R2")
        return self._capacity_pool(self.cnn(weather))

    def forward_from_hourly(
        self,
        hourly_unique: torch.Tensor,
        weather_batch_index: torch.Tensor,
        weather_time_offset_hours: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if self.mode != "latent_interpolation":
            raise ValueError("Cached hourly latents are only supported by R2")
        batch = len(weather_batch_index)
        hourly = hourly_unique.index_select(0, weather_batch_index)
        target_times = self.lead.lead_hours.to(hourly).reshape(1, -1).expand(batch, -1)
        target = linear_interpolate(hourly, weather_time_offset_hours, target_times)
        target = target + self.budget_adapter(target)
        return {
            "weather_features": target,
            "hourly_weather_tokens": hourly,
            "weather_attention": None,
            "spatial_attention": None,
        }

    def _patch_tokens(self, weather: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        cycles, nodes = weather.shape[:2]
        tokens = self.patch(weather.flatten(0, 1)).flatten(2).transpose(1, 2)
        tokens = tokens + self.patch_position(self.patch_metadata.to(tokens))[None]
        return self.patch_encoder(tokens).reshape(cycles, nodes, 30, self.d_model)

    def _regional_tokens(self, grid: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        cycles, nodes, dim, height, width = grid.shape
        cells = grid.permute(0, 1, 3, 4, 2).reshape(cycles * nodes, height * width, dim)
        query = self.region_queries[None].expand(cycles * nodes, -1, -1)
        pooled, _ = self.region_attention(query, cells, cells, need_weights=False)
        return pooled.reshape(cycles, nodes, 4, dim)

    def forward(
        self,
        weather: torch.Tensor,
        weather_batch_index: torch.Tensor,
        weather_time_offset_hours: torch.Tensor,
    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor | None]:
        batch = len(weather_batch_index)
        lead = self.lead(batch, dtype=weather.dtype, device=weather.device)
        target_times = self.lead.lead_hours.to(weather).reshape(1, -1).expand(batch, -1)
        spatial_attention: torch.Tensor | None = None
        if self.mode == "raw_interpolation":
            samples = weather.index_select(0, weather_batch_index)
            interpolated = linear_interpolate(samples, weather_time_offset_hours, target_times)
            grid = self.cnn(interpolated)
            target = self._capacity_pool(grid)
            hourly = None
            attention = None
        elif self.mode in {"joint_spacetime", "factorized_spacetime"}:
            patch = self._patch_tokens(weather).index_select(0, weather_batch_index)
            tokens = patch.reshape(batch, -1, self.d_model)
            token_times = weather_time_offset_hours[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, 30).reshape(batch, -1)
            if self.mode == "joint_spacetime":
                first, a1 = self.factor_time(lead, tokens, tokens, need_weights=True, average_attn_weights=False)
                target, a2 = self.factor_space(first, tokens, tokens, need_weights=True, average_attn_weights=False)
                attention = (a1.mean(dim=1) + a2.mean(dim=1)) / 2.0
            else:
                time_tokens = patch.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(batch * 30, 8, self.d_model)
                query = lead[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, 30, -1).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
                query = query.reshape(batch * 30, lead.shape[1], self.d_model)
                time_read, _ = self.factor_time(query, time_tokens, time_tokens, need_weights=False)
                time_read = time_read.reshape(batch, 30, lead.shape[1], self.d_model).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
                spatial_query = lead.reshape(batch * lead.shape[1], 1, self.d_model)
                spatial_value = time_read.reshape(batch * lead.shape[1], 30, self.d_model)
                target, a2 = self.factor_space(
                    spatial_query, spatial_value, spatial_value,
                    need_weights=True, average_attn_weights=False,
                )
                target = target.reshape(batch, lead.shape[1], self.d_model)
                attention = a2.mean(dim=1).reshape(batch, lead.shape[1], 30)
            hourly = patch.mean(dim=2)
        elif self.mode == "patch_regional":
            patch = self._patch_tokens(weather)
            cycles, nodes = patch.shape[:2]
            query = self.region_queries[None].expand(cycles * nodes, -1, -1)
            values = patch.reshape(cycles * nodes, 30, self.d_model)
            regions, weights = self.region_attention(
                query, values, values, need_weights=True, average_attn_weights=False
            )
            regions = regions.reshape(cycles, nodes * 4, self.d_model).index_select(0, weather_batch_index)
            times = weather_time_offset_hours[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, 4).reshape(batch, -1)
            target, attention = self.path_query(lead, regions, times)
            hourly = regions.reshape(batch, 8, 4, self.d_model).mean(dim=2)
            spatial_attention = weights.mean(dim=1).reshape(cycles, nodes, 4, 30)
        else:
            grid = self.cnn(weather)
            if self.mode == "dynamic_pool":
                cycles, nodes, dim, height, width = grid.shape
                row = torch.linspace(-1, 1, height, device=grid.device, dtype=grid.dtype)
                col = torch.linspace(-1, 1, width, device=grid.device, dtype=grid.dtype)
                yy, xx = torch.meshgrid(row, col, indexing="ij")
                meta = torch.stack((yy, xx, self.capacity_density.to(grid)), dim=-1)
                cells = grid.permute(0, 1, 3, 4, 2)
                logits = self.dynamic_score(
                    torch.cat((cells, meta.reshape(1, 1, height, width, 3).expand(cycles, nodes, -1, -1, -1)), dim=-1)
                ).squeeze(-1)
                weights = torch.softmax(logits.flatten(-2), dim=-1).reshape(cycles, nodes, height, width)
                hourly_unique = torch.einsum("ctdhw,cthw->ctd", grid, weights)
                spatial_attention = weights
            elif self.mode == "regional_tokens":
                regions = self._regional_tokens(grid)
                samples = regions.index_select(0, weather_batch_index).reshape(batch, 32, self.d_model)
                times = weather_time_offset_hours[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, 4).reshape(batch, 32)
                target, attention = self.path_query(lead, samples, times)
                target = target + self.budget_adapter(target)
                hourly = regions.mean(dim=2).index_select(0, weather_batch_index)
                return {
                    "weather_features": target,
                    "hourly_weather_tokens": hourly,
                    "weather_attention": attention,
                    "spatial_attention": None,
                }
            else:
                hourly_unique = self._capacity_pool(grid)
            hourly = hourly_unique.index_select(0, weather_batch_index)
            if self.mode == "latent_interpolation":
                target = linear_interpolate(hourly, weather_time_offset_hours, target_times)
                attention = None
            else:
                value_override = None
                if self.mode in {"target_value", "target_value_no_time"}:
                    delta = target_times[:, :, None] - weather_time_offset_hours[:, None]
                    time = torch.zeros_like(delta) if self.mode == "target_value_no_time" else delta
                    expanded = hourly[:, None].expand(-1, target_times.shape[1], -1, -1)
                    value_override = self.value_adapter(
                        torch.cat((expanded, time[..., None] / 6.0, time.abs()[..., None] / 6.0), dim=-1)
                    )
                target, attention = self.path_query(
                    lead, hourly, weather_time_offset_hours, value_override=value_override
                )
        target = target + self.budget_adapter(target)
        return {
            "weather_features": target,
            "hourly_weather_tokens": hourly,
            "weather_attention": attention,
            "spatial_attention": spatial_attention,
        }


class AggregateStateEncoder(nn.Module):
    MODES = {"current", "recent_1h", "recent_3h", "full_6h", "multiscale", "horizon"}

    def __init__(self, mode: str, d_model: int, heads: int, dropout: float, forecast_steps: int) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if mode not in self.MODES:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported aggregate state mode: {mode}")
        self.mode = mode
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.visible_steps_override: int | None = None
        self.token = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(5, d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.LayerNorm(d_model))
        layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
            d_model, _heads(d_model, heads), 2 * d_model, dropout,
            activation="gelu", batch_first=True, norm_first=True,
        )
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, 2, nn.LayerNorm(d_model))
        self.pool = nn.MultiheadAttention(d_model, _heads(d_model, heads), dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.shared_query = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(1, 1, d_model) * 0.02)
        self.lead = LeadEmbedding(d_model, forecast_steps)

    def _inputs(self, history: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        batch, steps = history.shape
        if steps != 24:
            raise ValueError("State encoder expects 24 historical quarter hours")
        if self.mode == "multiscale":
            older = history[:, :20].reshape(batch, 5, 4).mean(dim=-1)
            values = torch.cat((older, history[:, -4:]), dim=1)
            times = torch.tensor(
                [-5.5, -4.5, -3.5, -2.5, -1.5, -0.75, -0.5, -0.25, 0.0],
                device=history.device, dtype=history.dtype,
            ).reshape(1, 9).expand(batch, -1)
            duration = torch.cat(
                (torch.ones(batch, 5, device=history.device, dtype=history.dtype),
                 torch.full((batch, 4), 0.25, device=history.device, dtype=history.dtype)),
                dim=1,
            )
            delta = torch.zeros_like(values)
            delta[:, 1:] = values[:, 1:] - values[:, :-1]
            features = torch.stack((values, delta, times / 6.0, torch.ones_like(values), duration), dim=-1)
            pad = torch.zeros(batch, 15, 5, device=history.device, dtype=history.dtype)
            features = torch.cat((pad, features), dim=1)
            mask = torch.zeros(batch, 24, device=history.device, dtype=torch.bool)
            mask[:, -9:] = True
            if self.visible_steps_override is not None:
                mask[:, :-min(int(self.visible_steps_override), 4)] = False
            return features, mask
        visible = {"current": 1, "recent_1h": 4, "recent_3h": 12}.get(self.mode, 24)
        if self.visible_steps_override is not None:
            visible = min(visible, int(self.visible_steps_override))
        base, mask = history_features(history, visible)
        duration = torch.full_like(history, 0.25)
        return torch.cat((base, duration[..., None]), dim=-1), mask

    def forward(self, history: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        features, mask = self._inputs(history)
        encoded = self.encoder(self.token(features), src_key_padding_mask=~mask)
        batch = len(history)
        if self.mode == "horizon":
            query = self.lead(batch, dtype=history.dtype, device=history.device)
        else:
            query = self.shared_query.to(history).expand(batch, -1, -1)
        pooled, attention = self.pool(
            query, encoded, encoded, key_padding_mask=~mask,
            need_weights=True, average_attn_weights=False,
        )
        if self.mode != "horizon":
            pooled = pooled.expand(-1, 24, -1)
            attention = attention.expand(-1, -1, 24, -1)
        return pooled, attention.mean(dim=1)


class AggregateCorrectionModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        baseline: AggregateHistoryForecast,
        weather_encoder: AggregateWeatherEncoder,
        state_encoder: AggregateStateEncoder,
        *,
        d_model: int,
        forecast_steps: int,
        trajectory_layers: int,
        dropout: float,
        conditioning: str,
        fusion: str,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        if conditioning not in {"none", "state", "weather", "state_weather"}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid correction conditioning")
        if fusion not in {"blackbox", "additive"}:
            raise ValueError("Invalid correction fusion")
        self.baseline = baseline
        self.anchor_baseline = copy.deepcopy(baseline).eval().requires_grad_(False)
        self.weather_encoder = weather_encoder
        self.state_encoder = state_encoder
        self.conditioning = conditioning
        self.fusion = fusion
        self.forecast_steps = forecast_steps
        self.register_buffer("total_capacity_mw", baseline.total_capacity_mw.detach().clone())
        self.lead = LeadEmbedding(d_model, forecast_steps)
        self.fusion_network = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(3 * d_model, 2 * d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(2 * d_model, d_model), nn.LayerNorm(d_model),
        )
        self.trajectory = nn.Sequential(
            *[CausalResidualBlock(d_model, 2**layer, dropout) for layer in range(trajectory_layers)]
        )
        self.head = nn.Conv1d(d_model, 1, 1)
        nn.init.normal_(self.head.weight, std=1e-3)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.head.bias)

    def load_history_state(self, state: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor]) -> None:
        self.baseline.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
        self.anchor_baseline.load_state_dict(state, strict=True)
        self.anchor_baseline.eval().requires_grad_(False)

    def _decode(self, representation: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.head(self.trajectory(representation.transpose(1, 2))).squeeze(1)

    def forward(
        self,
        *,
        cluster_history: torch.Tensor,
        correction_state_history: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        weather: torch.Tensor,
        weather_batch_index: torch.Tensor,
        weather_time_offset_hours: torch.Tensor,
        **_: Any,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        baseline = self.baseline(cluster_history=cluster_history)["cluster_factor"]
        with torch.no_grad():
            anchor = self.anchor_baseline(cluster_history=cluster_history)["cluster_factor"]
        state_active = self.conditioning in {"state", "state_weather"}
        lead = self.lead(len(cluster_history), dtype=cluster_history.dtype, device=cluster_history.device)
        if state_active:
            state_history = (
                cluster_history if correction_state_history is None else correction_state_history
            )
            if state_history.shape != cluster_history.shape:
                raise ValueError(
                    "correction_state_history must have the same shape as cluster_history"
                )
            state, state_attention = self.state_encoder(state_history)
        else:
            # Keep the module and checkpoint keys for parameter-matched controls,
            # but do not spend compute on a conditioner whose output is inactive.
            state = torch.zeros_like(lead)
            state_attention = None
        weather_output = self.weather_encoder(weather, weather_batch_index, weather_time_offset_hours)
        weather_features = weather_output["weather_features"]
        assert weather_features is not None
        weather_active = self.conditioning in {"weather", "state_weather"}
        state_input = state if state_active else torch.zeros_like(state)
        weather_input = weather_features if weather_active else torch.zeros_like(weather_features)
        if self.fusion == "additive":
            zero_state = torch.zeros_like(state_input)
```
