"""Shared per-farm forecasts. No mixing of examples or future power labels."""
from settings import *
import sys,math,random
import numpy as np,torch
from torch import nn
sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code/legacy'))
sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code/legacy/vendor'))
import baselines_original as baselines
from column_original import column_coordinates
from wind_forcing.models.p0i_legacy import ResidualConv

def seed_all(seed):
    random.seed(seed);np.random.seed(seed);torch.manual_seed(seed)
    if torch.cuda.is_available():torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
def configure():
    torch.set_num_threads(1)
    try:torch.set_num_interop_threads(1)
    except RuntimeError:pass
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=False;torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32=False;torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark=False
    torch.backends.mha.set_fastpath_enabled(False)
def zero_last(m):
    z=[x for x in m.modules() if isinstance(x,nn.Linear)][-1];nn.init.zeros_(z.weight)
    if z.bias is not None:nn.init.zeros_(z.bias)

class NeighborReadout(nn.Module):
    """Original N3 readout: pooled 8-neighbor history with neighbor geometry keys."""
    def __init__(self,width):
        super().__init__();topo=np.load(ASSETS/'topology.npz')
        self.register_buffer('geometry',torch.from_numpy(topo['geometry']));self.register_buffer('mask',torch.from_numpy(topo['mask']))
        self.query=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(30,32),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(32,32));self.key=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(6,32),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(32,32));self.value=nn.Linear(2,32,bias=False);self.out=nn.Linear(32,width,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.out.weight)
    def forward(self,b):
        h=b['h'];n=len(h);geo=self.geometry[b['site']];k=geo.shape[1]
        p=b['near'][:,None].expand(-1,k,-1);delta=torch.diff(p,dim=-1,prepend=p[:,:,:1]);t=p.shape[-1]
        age=torch.arange(-21,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)/4
        inp=torch.cat([p[...,None],delta[...,None],age[None,None,:,None].expand(n,k,t,1)/6,geo[:,:,None].expand(-1,-1,t,-1)/100],-1)
        key=self.key(inp.flatten(1,2));value=self.value(torch.stack([p,delta],-1).flatten(1,2))
        lead=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)/4;time=torch.stack([lead/6,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead/24),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead/24)],-1)
        q=self.query(torch.cat([h[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1),time[None].expand(n,-1,-1),b['phase'][:,None,None].to(h).expand(-1,24,1)/3,b['physical'][...,[1,2]]/10],-1))
        key=torch.cat([key,h.new_zeros(n,1,32)],1);value=torch.cat([value,h.new_zeros(n,1,32)],1)
        mask=torch.cat([self.mask[b['site']][:,:,None].expand(-1,-1,t).flatten(1),torch.ones(n,1,device=h.device,dtype=torch.bool)],1)
        logits=(q@key.transpose(1,2))/math.sqrt(32);weights=logits.masked_fill(~mask[:,None],-1e4).softmax(-1)
        return self.out(weights@value)

class SharedModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,spec):
        super().__init__();self.spec=spec;self.family=spec['family'];self.condition=spec.get('condition','neutral');cfg=spec['config'];d=cfg['width'];drop=cfg['dropout'];self.d=d
        seed_all(spec['seed'])
        static=np.load(ASSETS/'site_static.npy');self.register_buffer('static',torch.from_numpy(static));self.embedding=nn.Embedding(len(static),8);self.site_projection=nn.Linear(12,d)
        self.rm=spec.get('rm',False)
        self.has_region=spec.get('input','R3')=='R3' and self.family!='B0'
        if self.family=='B0':
            self.history_projection=nn.Linear(2,d);self.history_encoder=nn.Sequential(*[ResidualConv(d,2**i,drop) for i in range(3)])
            self.query=nn.Linear(4,d);self.attn=nn.MultiheadAttention(d,4,dropout=drop,batch_first=True);self.head=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2*d,d),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,1));zero_last(self.head);return
        if self.family=='W0':
            self.history_projection=nn.Linear(12 if self.rm else 2,d)
            self.history_encoder=nn.TransformerEncoder(nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d,4,2*d,drop,batch_first=True,norm_first=True),2,enable_nested_tensor=False)
            self.weather_projection=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(15,d),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,d),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,d))
            self.align_v=nn.Linear(d,d,bias=False);self.align_out=nn.Linear(d,d);self.context_projection=nn.Linear(16,d)
            self.decoder=nn.TransformerDecoder(nn.TransformerDecoderLayer(d,4,2*d,drop,batch_first=True,norm_first=True),2)
            self.head=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,d//2),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d//2,1));zero_last(self.head)
            stats=json.loads((ASSETS/'normalization.json').read_text())
            for name,value in [('column_mean',stats['column_mean']),('column_std',stats['column_std']),('elevation',np.load(ASSETS/'elevation.npy'))]:self.register_buffer(name,torch.as_tensor(value,dtype=torch.float32))
        elif self.family=='TF':
            self.original=baselines.TransformerFutureCov(d);zero_last(self.original.head)
            if self.rm:self.original.h_in=nn.Linear(12,d)
            for m in self.original.modules():
                if isinstance(m,nn.Dropout):m.p=drop
                if isinstance(m,nn.MultiheadAttention):m.dropout=drop
        elif self.family=='TIDE':
            self.original=baselines.TiDEFutureCov(d);self.future_extra=nn.Linear(24*d,2*d,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.future_extra.weight);self.dropout=nn.Dropout(drop)
        elif self.family=='TFT':
            self.hp=nn.Linear(2,d);self.wp=nn.Parameter(torch.randn(14,d)*.02);self.wb=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(14,d));self.select=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14,d),nn.ELU(),nn.Linear(d,14))
            self.hr=nn.LSTM(d,d,2,dropout=drop,batch_first=True);self.wr=nn.LSTM(d,d,2,dropout=drop,batch_first=True);self.attn=nn.MultiheadAttention(d,4,dropout=drop,batch_first=True)
            self.gate=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.GLU());self.norm=nn.LayerNorm(d);self.ff=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.ELU(),nn.Dropout(drop),nn.Linear(2*d,d));self.gate2=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.GLU());self.norm2=nn.LayerNorm(d);self.head=nn.Linear(d,1);zero_last(self.head)
        else:raise ValueError(self.family)
        if self.family=='W0' or self.condition!='neutral':
            self.condition_projection=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d+3,d),nn.GELU(),nn.LayerNorm(d));self.transfer_adapter=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2*d,d),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,d));zero_last(self.transfer_adapter);self.condition_bias=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1,24,d))
        if self.has_region:
            self.sel=nn.Linear(22,d,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.sel.weight)
            if not self.rm:
                self.regional=nn.Linear(12,d,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.regional.weight);self.neighbor=NeighborReadout(d)

    def condition_response(self,b,w,memory,lead):
        n=len(w);summary=w.new_zeros(n,24,self.d);base=w.new_zeros(n,24);delta=base
        if self.condition in ['cb','cbh','anchor','wrong_cb','wrong_cbh']:
            base=b['base'];delta=b['base_delta']
        if self.condition in ['cbh','wrong_cbh']:
            # Both real and wrong CBH perform the same extra encoder call.
            h=b['condition_h'];clock=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24
            summary=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(torch.stack([h,clock],-1))).mean(1)[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1)
        raw=torch.cat([summary,base[...,None],delta[...,None],lead[...,None]],-1)
        cond=self.condition_projection(raw)+self.condition_bias
        return self.transfer_adapter(torch.cat([w,cond],-1))

    def forward(self,b):
        h=b['h'];n=len(h);site=torch.cat([self.static[b['site']],self.embedding(b['site'])],-1);sv=self.site_projection(site)
        lead=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(n,-1)/24
        tf=torch.stack([lead,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead),b['phase'][:,None].to(h).expand(-1,24)/3],-1)
        if self.family=='B0':
            clock=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24;memory=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(torch.stack([h,clock],-1))+sv[:,None])
            q=self.query(tf)+sv[:,None];a,_=self.attn(q,memory,memory,need_weights=False);return h[:,-1:]+self.head(torch.cat([q,a],-1)).squeeze(-1)
        extra=sv[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1)
        if self.has_region:
            extra=extra+self.sel(b['x'])
            if not self.rm:extra=extra+self.regional(b['region'])[:,None]+self.neighbor(b)
        if self.family=='W0':
            time=torch.stack([lead,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead/4),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead/4)],-1)
            context=torch.cat([time,site[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1),b['phase'][:,None,None].to(h).expand(-1,24,1)/3],-1)
            clock=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24
            hin=torch.stack([h,clock],-1)
            if self.rm:hin=torch.cat([hin,b['rm_history']],-1)
            memory=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(hin)+(sv[:,None] if self.rm else 0))
            col=column_coordinates(b['physical'],self.elevation[b['site']][:,None].expand(-1,24));w=self.align_out(self.align_v(self.weather_projection((col-self.column_mean)/self.column_std)))
            q=self.context_projection(context)+w+self.condition_response(b,w,memory,lead)+extra
            out=self.head(self.decoder(q,memory)).squeeze(-1);anchor=b['base'] if self.condition=='anchor' else h[:,-1:];return anchor+out
        if self.family=='TF':
            m=self.original;clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)
            hin=torch.stack([h,clock],-1)
            if self.rm:hin=torch.cat([hin,b['rm_history']],-1)
            memory=m.h_enc(m.pe(m.h_in(hin)+sv[:,None]))
            w=m.w_in(torch.cat([b['weather'],tf],-1));q=m.pe(w+extra)
            if hasattr(self,'condition_projection'):q=q+self.condition_response(b,w,memory,lead)
            out=m.dec(q,memory);return h[:,-1:]+m.head(torch.cat([out,h[:,-1:,None].expand(-1,24,1)],-1)).squeeze(-1)
        if self.family=='TIDE':
            m=self.original;clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h);p=m.past(torch.cat([h,clock],-1))+sv
            f=m.future(torch.cat([b['weather'],tf],-1).flatten(1))+self.future_extra(extra.flatten(1));z=torch.cat([p,f],-1)
            for block in m.blocks:z=z+self.dropout(block(z))
            return h[:,-1:]+m.skip(h-h.mean(-1,keepdim=True))+m.decoder(z)
        clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h);mem,state=self.hr(self.hp(torch.stack([h,clock],-1))+sv[:,None]);w=b['weather'];tokens=w[...,None]*self.wp+self.wb;weight=self.select(w).softmax(-1)
        z,_=self.wr((tokens*weight[...,None]).sum(2)+extra,state);full=torch.cat([mem,z],1);mask=torch.arange(48,device=h.device)[None]>(24+torch.arange(24,device=h.device)[:,None])
        a,_=self.attn(z,full,full,attn_mask=mask,need_weights=False);z=self.norm(z+self.gate(a));z=self.norm2(z+self.gate2(self.ff(z)));return h[:,-1:]+self.head(z).squeeze(-1)
```

```
"""Availability-aware Pangu selection. All stored timestamps are UTC Unix seconds."""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
import numpy as np

HOUR = 3600
LATENCY = 15 * HOUR
PERIOD = 24 * HOUR
LOCAL = ZoneInfo('Asia/Shanghai')
BAD_START = int(datetime(2024, 11, 1, tzinfo=LOCAL).timestamp())
BAD_END = int(datetime(2024, 12, 1, tzinfo=LOCAL).timestamp())
BAD_UTC_START = int(datetime(2024, 11, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp())
BAD_UTC_END = int(datetime(2024, 12, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp())


def unix(value: str, timezone_name: str = 'Asia/Shanghai') -> int:
    """Localize naive power timestamps; preserve timezone-aware NWP timestamps."""
    stamp = datetime.fromisoformat(value)
    if stamp.tzinfo is None:
        stamp = stamp.replace(tzinfo=ZoneInfo(timezone_name))
    return int(stamp.timestamp())


def select_issue(origin):
    origin = np.asarray(origin, dtype=np.int64)
    return ((origin - LATENCY) // PERIOD) * PERIOD


def plan_times(origin):
    origin = np.asarray(origin, dtype=np.int64)
    if np.any(origin % 900):
        raise ValueError('Power origins must lie on the 15-minute time grid')
    issue = select_issue(origin)
    base = (origin // HOUR) * HOUR
    future = base[:, None] + np.arange(8, dtype=np.int64)[None] * HOUR
    past = base[:, None] + np.arange(-6, 0, dtype=np.int64)[None] * HOUR
    available = issue + LATENCY
    assert np.all(available <= origin) and np.all(origin < available + PERIOD)
    assert np.all(issue % PERIOD == 0)
    assert np.all(future[:, 0] <= origin + 900)
    assert np.all(future[:, -1] >= origin + 6 * HOUR)
    assert np.all((future - issue[:, None]) <= 45 * HOUR)
    return dict(issue=issue, available=available, base=base, future=future, past=past)


def november_exclusions(origin, year=2024):
    """Conservative exclusion of local power/weather windows and bad issue files.

    Covers the UTC filename month as well as the business calendar month. This
    also prevents December boundary samples from reusing a November issue.
    """
    BAD_START = int(datetime(year, 11, 1, tzinfo=LOCAL).timestamp())
    BAD_END = int(datetime(year, 12, 1, tzinfo=LOCAL).timestamp())
    BAD_UTC_START = int(datetime(year, 11, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp())
    BAD_UTC_END = int(datetime(year, 12, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc).timestamp())
    origin = np.asarray(origin, dtype=np.int64)
    p = plan_times(origin)
    overlaps = lambda a, b: (a < BAD_END) & (b >= BAD_START)
    return {
        'power_window_touches_local_november': overlaps(origin - 23 * 900, origin + 24 * 900),
        'weather_window_touches_local_november': overlaps(p['past'][:, 0], p['future'][:, -1]),
        'issue_in_utc_november': (p['issue'] >= BAD_UTC_START) & (p['issue'] < BAD_UTC_END),
        'issue_in_local_november': (p['issue'] >= BAD_START) & (p['issue'] < BAD_END),
    }


def source_path(root: str | Path, issue: int, lead: int) -> Path:
    if lead < 0 or lead > 96:
        raise ValueError(f'Unsupported Pangu lead: {lead}')
    stamp = datetime.fromtimestamp(int(issue), tz=timezone.utc)
    step = f'{int(lead):02d}'
    return Path(root) / str(stamp.year) / step / f'{stamp:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S}+00:00-{step}.npy'


def interpolate_nodes(nodes, offsets, queries):
    """Hourly linear interpolation without silent endpoint extrapolation."""
    nodes = np.asarray(nodes)
    offsets = np.asarray(offsets)
    queries = np.asarray(queries, dtype=np.float32)
    if queries.ndim == 1:
        queries = np.broadcast_to(queries, (len(nodes), len(queries)))
    if not np.allclose(np.diff(offsets, axis=1), 1):
        raise ValueError('Expected contiguous hourly nodes')
    x = queries - offsets[:, :1]
    if np.any(x < -1e-6) or np.any(x > nodes.shape[1] - 1 + 1e-6):
        raise ValueError('Required forecast valid time is outside available nodes')
    lo = np.minimum(np.floor(x).astype(np.int64), nodes.shape[1] - 2)
    frac = (x - lo)[..., None]
    rows = np.arange(len(nodes))[:, None]
    return (nodes[rows, lo] * (1 - frac) + nodes[rows, lo + 1] * frac).astype(np.float32)
```

```
"""Explicit units and validity masks; diagnostic proxies, not measured hub physics."""
import numpy as np

G = 9.80665
RD = 287.05
KAPPA = 0.2854
OMEGA = 7.2921159e-5

def thermodynamics(weather, elevation_m, hub_height_m=100.):
    """Physical channels in Pa/K/m²s⁻²/m s⁻¹. No CF upper bound is imposed."""
    w = np.asarray(weather, dtype=np.float64)
    t = w[..., 3]
    z = np.asarray(elevation_m, dtype=np.float64)
    # Dry hypsometric approximation; MSLP is NOT local surface pressure.
    column_t = np.maximum(t + 0.0065*z/2, 180.)
    ps = w[..., 0] * np.exp(-G*z/(RD*column_t))
    thub = np.maximum(t - 0.0065*hub_height_m, 180.)
    phub = ps*np.exp(-G*hub_height_m/(RD*np.maximum((t+thub)/2, 180.)))
    rho = phub/(RD*thub)
    theta_s = t*(1e5/np.maximum(ps, 1.))**KAPPA
    theta_925 = w[..., 9]*(1000./925.)**KAPPA
    height_925 = w[..., 5]/G-z
    valid_925 = height_925 > hub_height_m
    du, dv = w[..., 11]-w[..., 1], w[..., 13]-w[..., 2]
    height = np.maximum(height_925-10., 1.)
    theta_gradient = np.where(valid_925, 100.*(theta_925-theta_s)/height, 0.)
    shear = np.where(valid_925, 100.*np.hypot(du,dv)/height, 0.)
    rb = np.where(valid_925, G/np.maximum(theta_s,180.)*(theta_925-theta_s)*height/(du*du+dv*dv+1.), 0.)
    # Neutral logarithmic interpolation is only a proxy; no inferred roughness/flux.
    alpha = np.where(valid_925, np.log(hub_height_m/10.)/np.log(np.maximum(height_925,hub_height_m+1.)/10.), 0.)
    uh, vh = w[...,1]+alpha*du, w[...,2]+alpha*dv
    v10 = np.hypot(w[...,1],w[...,2]); vhub=np.hypot(uh,vh)
    density = np.stack((rho/1.225, (rho/1.225)**(1/3)*v10/10., rho*v10**3/1000.),-1)
    stability = np.stack((theta_gradient, shear, np.clip(rb,-5,5), valid_925.astype(float), vhub/10.),-1)
    return density.astype('f4'), stability.astype('f4')

def dynamic_fields(patch, latitude, latitude_step, longitude_step, weights):
    """[cycle,node,row,col,14] physical weather -> station differential features.

    z is geopotential, so geostrophic wind uses grad(z)/f, with no extra g.
    Signed grid spacing handles north-to-south latitude ordering.
    """
    w=np.asarray(patch,dtype=np.float64)
    dx=np.deg2rad(longitude_step)*6371000.*np.cos(np.deg2rad(latitude))
    dy=np.deg2rad(latitude_step)*6371000.
    coriolis=2*OMEGA*np.sin(np.deg2rad(latitude))
    if abs(coriolis)<1e-5: raise ValueError('Geostrophic proxy is invalid near the equator')
    wt=np.asarray(weights).reshape(w.shape[-3:-1])
    at=lambda a:(a*wt).sum((-2,-1))
    ug=-at(np.gradient(w[...,5],dy,axis=-2,edge_order=2))/coriolis
    vg=at(np.gradient(w[...,5],dx,axis=-1,edge_order=2))/coriolis
    dtdx=at(np.gradient(w[...,9],dx,axis=-1,edge_order=2))
    dtdy=at(np.gradient(w[...,9],dy,axis=-2,edge_order=2))
    point=(w*wt[None,None,:,:,None]).sum((-3,-2))
    adv=-(point[...,11]*dtdx+point[...,13]*dtdy)*3600.
    dpdt=np.gradient(point[...,0],1.,axis=1)/100.
    dvdt=np.gradient(np.hypot(point[...,1],point[...,2]),1.,axis=1)
    return np.stack((ug/10.,vg/10.,(point[...,1]-ug)/10.,(point[...,2]-vg)/10.,adv,dpdt,dvdt),-1).astype('f4')

def calendar(origin_unix, leads_hours):
    valid=np.asarray(origin_unix)[:,None]+np.rint(np.asarray(leads_hours)[None]*3600).astype('i8')
    local=valid+8*3600
    days=local.astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[D]')
    year_start=days.astype('datetime64[Y]').astype('datetime64[D]')
    year_end=(days.astype('datetime64[Y]')+1).astype('datetime64[D]')
    year_fraction=((days-year_start).astype(float)+(local%86400)/86400.)/(year_end-year_start).astype(float)
    angle=2*np.pi*(local%86400)/86400.
    return np.stack((np.sin(angle),np.cos(angle),np.sin(2*np.pi*year_fraction),np.cos(2*np.pi*year_fraction)),-1).astype('f4')

def operating_state(history, flat_threshold):
    """History-only plateau descriptors. No curtailment/icing labels inferred."""
    h=np.asarray(history); d=np.diff(h,axis=-1); flat=np.abs(d)<=flat_threshold
    duration=np.cumprod(flat[:,::-1],axis=1).sum(1)/23.
    return np.stack((h[:,-4:].std(1),h[:,-8:].std(1),np.ptp(h[:,-4:],axis=1),
                     h[:,-1]-h[:,-5],h[:,-1]-h[:,0],flat[:,-4:].mean(1),duration),-1).astype('f4')

def neighbor_geometry(lat, lon, maximum_k=8, radius_km=150.):
    lat=np.asarray(lat);lon=np.asarray(lon);n=len(lat)
    phi=np.deg2rad(lat);lam=np.deg2rad(lon)
    dphi=phi[None,:]-phi[:,None];dlam=lam[None,:]-lam[:,None]
    a=np.sin(dphi/2)**2+np.cos(phi[:,None])*np.cos(phi[None,:])*np.sin(dlam/2)**2
    distance=2*6371*np.arcsin(np.sqrt(np.clip(a,0,1)))
    np.fill_diagonal(distance,np.inf)
    ids=np.argsort(distance,axis=1,kind='stable')[:,:maximum_k]
    dist=np.take_along_axis(distance,ids,axis=1)
    # Vector FROM neighbor TO target, east/north in km.
    dx=(lon[:,None]-lon[ids])*np.pi/180*6371*np.cos(phi[:,None])
    dy=(lat[:,None]-lat[ids])*np.pi/180*6371
    return ids,np.stack((dx,dy,dist),-1).astype('f4'),dist<=radius_km
```

import itertools

def create():
    families={'TF_BASE':('TF','BASE'),'TF_R3':('TF','R3'),'TIDE_R3':('TIDE','R3'),'TFT_R3':('TFT','R3'),'W0_R3':('W0','R3')}

    for ci,cfg in enumerate(configs):

            for group,(family,inp) in families.items():specs.append(dict(id=f'TUNE_{group}_c{ci}_r{seed}',stage='tune',group=group,family=family,input=inp,condition='neutral',seed=seed,config=cfg,epochs=30,patience=6,dependencies=[]))

"""Power-only frozen QC, retained-neighbor recomputation, train-only statistics."""
from settings import *
import numpy as np,h5py,csv,shutil
from physics import thermodynamics,operating_state,neighbor_geometry
from operational import interpolate_nodes,select_issue

def save(p,x):
    p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);np.save(p,np.asarray(x))

def column(p,e):
    e=np.full(p.shape[:-1],e,dtype='f4')
    return np.stack([p[...,0],p[...,1],p[...,2],p[...,3],e,p[...,10]-p[...,1],p[...,12]-p[...,2],p[...,8],p[...,6],p[...,4]/9.80665-e,p[...,11]-p[...,10],p[...,13]-p[...,12],p[...,9]*(1000/925)**.2854-p[...,8],p[...,7]-p[...,6],(p[...,5]-p[...,4])/9.80665],-1)

def qc():
    rules=json.loads((RUN/'QC_RULES.json').read_text());rows=[]
    with h5py.File(PROJECT/'data/jilin_56farm_pad8.h5','r') as f:
        times=f['power/timestamp_unix'][:]
        years=(times+28800).astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[Y]').astype(int)+1970
        # Slice before reading, never load 2024 power values.
        ii=np.flatnonzero(np.isin(years,rules['cohort_years']))
        raw=f['power/station_mw'][ii]
        cap=f['stations/capacity_mw'][:]
        ids=f['stations/id'].asstr()[:];names=f['stations/name'].asstr()[:]
        power=raw/cap
    for s in range(len(ids)):
        reasons=[];stats={}
        for y in rules['cohort_years']:
            v=power[years[ii]==y,s];finite=np.isfinite(v);z=v[finite]
            q=float(np.quantile(z,.99)) if len(z) else 0.
            constant=np.zeros(len(v),bool)
            breaks=np.flatnonzero(np.r_[True,~(np.isfinite(v[1:])&np.isfinite(v[:-1])&(np.abs(np.diff(v))<=rules['constant_tolerance_capacity_factor'])),True])
            for a,b in zip(breaks[:-1],breaks[1:]):
                if b-a>=rules['constant_minimum_run_steps'] and rules['constant_nonzero_range'][0]<v[a]<rules['constant_nonzero_range'][1]:constant[a:b]=True
            gross=float(((z<rules['gross_out_of_range'][0])|(z>rules['gross_out_of_range'][1])).mean()) if len(z) else 1.
            d=dict(finite_fraction=float(finite.mean()),q99=q,gross_fraction=gross,constant_nonzero_fraction=float(constant.sum()/max(1,finite.sum())))
            stats[str(y)]=d
            if d['finite_fraction']<rules['minimum_finite_fraction_each_year']:reasons.append(f'{y}:missing')
            if not rules['minimum_q99_capacity_factor_each_year']<=q<=rules['maximum_q99_capacity_factor_each_year']:reasons.append(f'{y}:q99')
            if gross>rules['maximum_gross_out_of_range_fraction']:reasons.append(f'{y}:gross_range')
            if d['constant_nonzero_fraction']>rules['maximum_nonzero_constant_run_fraction']:reasons.append(f'{y}:constant_meter')
        q=[d['q99'] for d in stats.values()];shift=rules['q99_shift_exclude_if_both']
        if max(q)-min(q)>shift['absolute_change_above'] and max(q)/max(min(q),1e-9)>shift['max_min_ratio_above']:reasons.append('q99_cross_year_shift')
        rows.append(dict(original_site=s,station_id=str(ids[s]),name=str(names[s]),keep=not reasons,reasons=reasons,years=stats))
    keep=[r['original_site'] for r in rows if r['keep']]
    audit=dict(timestamp=now(),rules_sha256=sha(RUN/'QC_RULES.json'),retained=keep,removed=[r for r in rows if not r['keep']],records=rows,read_years=[2022,2023],model_errors_read=False)
    atomic_json(ASSETS/'QC_AUDIT.json',audit)
    atomic_json(ASSETS/'COHORT.json',dict(original_indices=keep,station_ids=[rows[s]['station_id'] for s in keep],names=[rows[s]['name'] for s in keep],count=len(keep),qc_rules_sha256=audit['rules_sha256']))
    with (ASSETS/'qc_by_farm.csv').open('w') as f:
        w=csv.writer(f);w.writerow(['id','name','keep','reasons','q99_2022','q99_2023','coverage2022','coverage2023','constant2022','constant2023'])
        for r in rows:w.writerow([r['station_id'],r['name'],r['keep'],';'.join(r['reasons']),*[r['years'][str(y)][k] for k in ['q99','finite_fraction','constant_nonzero_fraction'] for y in [2022,2023]]])
    print('QC',len(keep),'retained',[(r['name'],r['reasons']) for r in audit['removed']],flush=True)
    return np.asarray(keep)

def prepare():
    protocol=json.loads((SOURCE/'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())
    fingerprint=protocol['preprocess_fingerprint']
    assert protocol['protocol']=='daily_08_BJT_latency15h'
    assert protocol['regional_power_delay_minutes']==0
    for name in ['SUCCESS.json','INDEPENDENT_AUDIT.json']:
        marker=json.loads((SOURCE/name).read_text())
        assert marker['preprocess_fingerprint']==fingerprint
        if name=='INDEPENDENT_AUDIT.json':assert marker['passed']
    (DATA/'READY.json').unlink(missing_ok=True)
    keep=qc();static=SOURCE/'cache/static'
    frozen=json.loads((RUN/'selection_inputs/COHORT.json').read_text())
    assert keep.tolist()==frozen['original_indices'], 'Preserve the existing power-QC cohort'
    assert json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text())==frozen
    for name in ['station_id','station_name','station_latitude','station_longitude','station_capacity_mw']:
        save(ASSETS/(name+'.npy'),np.load(static/(name+'.npy'))[keep])
    for name in ['nwp_mean','nwp_std','nwp_channel_name']:shutil.copy2(static/(name+'.npy'),ASSETS/(name+'.npy'))
    elevation=np.load(SOURCE/'physical/elevation.npy')[keep];save(ASSETS/'elevation.npy',elevation)
    lat=np.load(ASSETS/'station_latitude.npy');lon=np.load(ASSETS/'station_longitude.npy');cap=np.load(ASSETS/'station_capacity_mw.npy')

    stats={};mu=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_mean.npy');sd=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_std.npy')

from operational import select_issue,source_path,BAD_START,BAD_END,BAD_UTC_START,BAD_UTC_END
import numpy as np,h5py,csv,argparse,time,fcntl

def build(raw_root):
    out=RUN/'lm_archive';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    lock=(out/'build.lock').open('a+');fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX|fcntl.LOCK_NB)
    cohort=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text());keep=cohort['original_indices']
    bundle=SOURCE;static=bundle/'cache/static'
    mu=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_mean.npy');sd=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_std.npy')
    ids=np.load(static/'bilinear_patch_index.npy')[keep];weights=np.load(static/'bilinear_weight.npy')[keep]
    lat=np.load(static/'weather_latitude.npy');lon=np.load(static/'weather_longitude.npy')
    rr=np.rint((90-lat)/.25).astype(int);cc=np.rint(lon/.25).astype(int)
    assert np.all(np.diff(rr)==1) and np.all(np.diff(cc)==1)
    start=int(np.datetime64('2022-01-01T00:00:00','s').astype(int))-28800
    end=int(np.datetime64('2025-01-01T00:00:00','s').astype(int))-28800
    times=np.arange(start,end,3600,dtype='i8');issues=select_issue(times);leads=(times-issues)//3600
    assert np.all(issues+54000<=times) and np.all((leads>=15)&(leads<39))
    # Mask bad valid times AND issue files in either calendar convention.
    bad=((times>=BAD_START)&(times<BAD_END))|((times>=BAD_UTC_START)&(times<BAD_UTC_END))|((issues>=BAD_START)&(issues<BAD_END))|((issues>=BAD_UTC_START)&(issues<BAD_UTC_END))
    weather=np.full((len(times),55,14),np.nan,'f4');power=np.full((len(times),55),np.nan,'f4');source=np.zeros(len(times),'i1')
    fi=np.load(bundle/'frame_issue_unix.npy');fl=np.load(bundle/'frame_lead_hours.npy');done=np.load(bundle/'raw_done.npy')
    lookup={(int(a),int(b)):i for i,(a,b) in enumerate(zip(fi,fl)) if done[i]}
    raw=np.load(bundle/'raw_frames.npy',mmap_mode='r');missing=[];receipts=[]
    def sample(crop):
        assert crop.shape==(14,len(lat),len(lon)) and np.isfinite(crop).all()
        normalized=(crop-mu[:,None,None])/sd[:,None,None]
        return (normalized.reshape(14,-1)[:,ids]*weights[None]).sum(-1).T.astype('f4')
    begin=time.monotonic()
    for i,(t,it,lead) in enumerate(zip(times,issues,leads)):
        if bad[i]:continue
        key=(int(it),int(lead))
        if key in lookup:weather[i]=sample(np.asarray(raw[lookup[key]]));source[i]=1
        else:

            if path.exists():

                assert a.shape==(1,14,721,1440) and a.dtype==np.float32
                crop=np.array(a[0,:,rr[0]:rr[-1]+1,cc[0]:cc[-1]+1]);crop[[6,7]]=np.maximum(crop[[6,7]],0);weather[i]=sample(crop);source[i]=2
                after=path.stat();assert (before.st_size,before.st_mtime_ns)==(after.st_size,after.st_mtime_ns)
                receipts.append(dict(time=int(t),path=str(path),mtime_ns=before.st_mtime_ns,size=before.st_size))
            else:missing.append(dict(time=int(t),issue=int(it),lead=int(lead),path=str(path)))
        if i%1000==0:atomic_json(out/'STATUS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),stage='hourly_forecast_sampling',completed=i,total=len(times),seconds=time.monotonic()-begin))
    with h5py.File(PROJECT/'data/jilin_56farm_pad8.h5','r') as h5:
        ht=h5['power/timestamp_unix'][:];index=np.searchsorted(ht,times)
        assert (index<len(ht)).all() and np.array_equal(ht[index],times)
        # This is archive construction, not model evaluation or normalization.
        # Excluded November2024 power is not read, even for memory.
        ix=np.flatnonzero(~bad);values=h5['power/station_mw'][index[ix]][:,keep]
        power[ix]=values/np.load(ASSETS/'station_capacity_mw.npy')
    valid=np.isfinite(weather).all(-1)&np.isfinite(power)&(~bad[:,None])
    years=(times+28800).astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[Y]').astype(int)+1970
    speed=np.hypot(weather[...,10]*sd[10]+mu[10],weather[...,12]*sd[12]+mu[12])
    curve=np.full(power.shape,np.nan,'f4');edges=[];means=[]
    for s in range(55):
        ok=valid[:,s]&(years==2022);e=np.quantile(speed[ok,s],np.linspace(0,1,31));binid=np.clip(np.searchsorted(e,speed[ok,s])-1,0,29)
        v=power[ok,s];m=np.array([v[binid==j].mean() if np.any(binid==j) else v.mean() for j in range(30)])
        use=np.flatnonzero(valid[:,s]);curve[use,s]=m[np.clip(np.searchsorted(e,speed[use,s])-1,0,29)]
        edges.append(e);means.append(m)
    for name,a in dict(time=times,issue=issues,lead_hours=leads,weather=weather,power=power,residual=power-curve,mapped_power=curve,valid=valid,bad_period=bad,source=source).items():np.save(out/f'{name}.npy',a)
    np.savez(out/'curve_2022.npz',edges=np.array(edges),means=np.array(means))
    atomic_json(out/'MISSING_SOURCES.json',dict(count=len(missing),raw_root=str(raw_root),raw_root_accessible=raw_root.exists(),missing=missing))
    atomic_json(out/'RAW_RECEIPTS.json',receipts)
    # Exact same-time sampled source values must reproduce old cache nodes.
    audits=[]
    for sp in ['train','val']:
        origin=np.load(DATA/sp/'origin.npy');cy=np.load(DATA/sp/'cycle.npy');offset=np.load(DATA/sp/'offset.npy');nodes=np.load(DATA/sp/'nodes.npy',mmap_mode='r')

        usable=valid[ii].all(1);err=float(np.max(abs(weather[ii[usable]]-nodes[cy[rows[usable]],:,0,:])))
        assert err<3e-5,(sp,err);audits.append(dict(split=sp,samples=int(usable.sum()),max_difference=err))
    coverage={}
    for a,b,label in [('2022-12-16','2023-01-04','requested_year_boundary'),('2023-11-01','2023-12-01','november2023'),('2024-11-01','2024-12-01','november2024')]:
        lo=int(np.datetime64(a,'s').astype(int))-28800;hi=int(np.datetime64(b,'s').astype(int))-28800;sel=(times>=lo)&(times<hi)
        coverage[label]=dict(hours=int(sel.sum()),weather_hours=int(np.isfinite(weather[sel]).all((1,2)).sum()),fully_paired_hours=int(valid[sel].all(1).sum()),masked_hours=int(bad[sel].sum()))
    audit=dict(timestamp=now(),preprocess_fingerprint=json.loads((bundle/'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint'],forecast_archive_complete=len(missing)==0,training_ready=len(missing)==0,total_hours=len(times),cached_hours=int((source==1).sum()),raw_hours=int((source==2).sum()),missing_hours=len(missing),masked_hours=int(bad.sum()),
        per_year={str(y):dict(total_hours=int((years==y).sum()),valid_pairs=int(valid[years==y].sum()),possible_pairs=int((years==y).sum()*55)) for y in [2022,2023,2024]},
        coverage=coverage,cache_equivalence=audits,power_read_years=[2022,2023,2024],evaluation_performed=False,era5_observations_read=False,curve_fit_year=2022,
        coordinates=dict(latitude=[float(lat.min()),float(lat.max())],longitude=[float(lon.min()),float(lon.max())]),
        convention='UTC Unix timestamps; local power UTC+8; available=issue+15h; daily00UTC only; bad target and issue November2024 masked',
        invalid_policy='explicit masks; no substitution with ERA5, newer issues, or another forecast source')
    atomic_json(out/'AUDIT.json',audit);atomic_json(out/'STATUS.json',dict(stage='complete' if not missing else 'awaiting_missing_raw_source',**audit))
    (out/'README_CN.md').write_text(f"# LM 小时配对档案\n\n已有缓存覆盖 {audit['cached_hours']} 小时；缺少原始预报 {len(missing)} 小时。缺测均掩码，不以 ERA5 或其他期次填充。\n\n{json.dumps(coverage,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)}\n\n仅数据准备，没有运行 2024 模型评价。是否可用于训练以 AUDIT.json 的 training_ready 为准。\n")
    print(json.dumps(audit,ensure_ascii=False),flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/code/prepare_rm.py

"""Regional history available through every local-history timestamp from the frozen 55-site cohort, no weather access."""
from settings import *
import numpy as np

def regional_history(h,top):
    # h [time, source station, history]; output [time,target,history,10].
    weights=top['ring_weights'];near=top['near_weights'];flags=top['ring_valid'].T
    x=np.zeros((len(h),h.shape[1],24,10),'f4')
    past=h
    mean=np.einsum('rsj,njt->nstr',weights,past,optimize=True)
    second=np.einsum('rsj,njt->nstr',weights,past**2,optimize=True)
    x[:,:,:,:3]=mean;x[:,:,:,3:6]=np.sqrt(np.maximum(0,second-mean**2))
    x[:,:,:,6]=np.einsum('sj,njt->nst',near,past,optimize=True)
    x[:,:,:,7:]=flags[None,:,None,:]
    # All 24 regional steps match the timestamps of local history, including t.
    return x

def main():
    top=np.load(ASSETS/'topology.npz');root=DATA/'regional_history';root.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    for split in ['train','val']:
        h=np.load(DATA/split/'h.npy',mmap_mode='r')
        p=root/f'{split}.npy';a=np.lib.format.open_memmap(p,mode='w+',dtype='f4',shape=(*h.shape[:2],24,10))
        for start in range(0,len(h),256):a[start:start+256]=regional_history(np.asarray(h[start:start+256]),top)
        a.flush();assert np.isfinite(a).all();print(split,a.shape,flush=True)
    atomic_json(root/'READY.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,preprocess_fingerprint=json.loads((SOURCE/'PROTOCOL.json').read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint'],
        channels=['ring_mean_0','ring_mean_1','ring_mean_2','ring_std_0','ring_std_1','ring_std_2','near_mean','ring_exists_0','ring_exists_1','ring_exists_2'],
        neighbor_delay_minutes=0,first_regional_step_zero=False,scaling='capacity factor, population weighted std, binary flags',
        files={f'{s}.npy':sha(root/f'{s}.npy') for s in ['train','val']}))

if __name__=='__main__':main()
```

from pathlib import Path
import os,json,hashlib,datetime
RUN=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
PROJECT=RUN.parents[1]
DATA=RUN/'data'; ASSETS=RUN/'assets'; JOBS=RUN/'jobs'
SOURCE=Path('/shared_data_ssd3/liying/operational_pangu_daily_20261114_v1')
SEEDS=list(range(42,47))
def now():return datetime.datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat()
def atomic_json(p,x):
    p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);t=p.with_name(p.name+f'.{os.getpid()}.tmp')
    t.write_text(json.dumps(x,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False)+'\n');os.replace(t,p)
def sha(p):
    h=hashlib.sha256()
    with Path(p).open('rb') as f:
        for b in iter(lambda:f.read(4<<20),b''):h.update(b)
    return h.hexdigest()
```

from settings import *
from data import SharedData
from model import SharedModel,configure,seed_all
import torch,numpy as np,time,socket,argparse,traceback,signal

class YieldJob(Exception):pass
def save_torch(p,x):
    tmp=p.with_suffix('.tmp');torch.save(x,tmp);os.replace(tmp,p)
def requested(out):
    if (out/'YIELD_REQUEST.json').exists():raise YieldJob()
def resolve(spec):
    s=dict(spec)
    if 'config_from' in s:
        p=ASSETS/'selected'/f'{s["config_from"]}.json';sel=json.loads(p.read_text());s['config']=sel['config'];s['selection_sha256']=sha(p)
    return s

@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model,data,batch_size,device,out=None,pred_path=None):
    model.eval();nf=data.farms;ab=np.zeros((nf,24));sq=ab.copy();counts=np.zeros(nf);hi_ab=ab.copy();hi_sq=ab.copy();hi_count=np.zeros(nf)
    pred=None
    if pred_path is not None:pred=np.lib.format.open_memmap(pred_path,mode='w+',dtype='f4',shape=(len(data.rows),nf,24))
    for st in range(0,data.n,batch_size):
        if out is not None:requested(out)
        inds=np.arange(st,min(st+batch_size,data.n));b,y,si,_=data.batch(inds,device);p=model(b);err=(p-y).cpu().numpy().astype('f8')
        if not np.isfinite(err).all():raise FloatingPointError('nonfinite predictions')
        np.add.at(ab,si,abs(err));np.add.at(sq,si,err**2);np.add.at(counts,si,1)
        high=b['h'][:,-1].cpu().numpy()>=.75
        np.add.at(hi_ab,si[high],abs(err[high]));np.add.at(hi_sq,si[high],err[high]**2);np.add.at(hi_count,si[high],1)
        if pred is not None:pred.reshape(-1,24)[inds]=p.cpu().numpy()
    if pred is not None:pred.flush()
    lead_mae=ab/counts[:,None];lead_mse=sq/counts[:,None];mae=lead_mae.mean(1);rmse=np.sqrt(lead_mse.mean(1))
    high_rmse=np.sqrt(hi_sq.sum(1)/np.maximum(1,hi_count*24));valid=hi_count>0
    return dict(mae=float(mae.mean()),rmse=float(rmse.mean()),per_farm_mae=mae.tolist(),per_farm_rmse=rmse.tolist(),lead_mae=lead_mae.tolist(),lead_rmse=np.sqrt(lead_mse).tolist(),
        high_power_rmse=float(high_rmse[valid].mean()) if valid.any() else None,high_power_per_farm_rmse=high_rmse.tolist(),high_power_counts=hi_count.astype(int).tolist(),counts=counts.astype(int).tolist(),
        horizons={label:dict(mae=float(lead_mae[:,a:b].mean()),rmse=float(np.sqrt(lead_mse[:,a:b].mean(1)).mean())) for label,a,b in [('0_1h',0,4),('1_3h',4,12),('3_6h',12,24)]})

def run(requested_spec,smoke=False,device='cuda:0',max_batches=None):
    configure();spec=resolve(requested_spec);seed_all(spec['seed']);out=(RUN/'smoke_jobs' if smoke else JOBS)/spec['id'];out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    if not smoke and (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():return
    atomic_json(out/'spec.json',spec)
    if spec['family']=='B0' and spec['fold']>=0:
        f=np.load(ASSETS/f'fold{spec["fold"]}.npz');train=SharedData('train',spec,f['train']);valid=SharedData('train',spec,f['valid'])
    else:train=SharedData('train',spec);valid=SharedData('val',spec)
    if smoke:
        train.rows=train.rows[:32];train.n=len(train.rows)*train.farms;valid.rows=valid.rows[:16];valid.n=len(valid.rows)*valid.farms
    model=SharedModel(spec).to(device);opt=torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(),lr=spec['config']['lr'],weight_decay=spec['config']['weight_decay'])
    epoch=0;cursor=0;history=[];best=float('inf');bad=0;loss_sum=0.;seen=0;active_seconds=0.;micro=512
    ap=out/'adaptive_microbatch.json'
    if ap.exists():micro=int(json.loads(ap.read_text())['microbatch'])
    epoch_started=time.monotonic();epoch_elapsed_prior=0.
    def checkpoint():
        return dict(spec=spec,model=model.state_dict(),optimizer=opt.state_dict(),epoch=epoch,cursor=cursor,history=history,best=best,bad=bad,loss_sum=loss_sum,seen=seen,active_seconds=active_seconds,
            epoch_elapsed=epoch_elapsed_prior+time.monotonic()-epoch_started,rng=torch.get_rng_state(),cuda_rng=torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all() if device.startswith('cuda') else None)
    if (out/'latest.pt').exists():
        ck=torch.load(out/'latest.pt',map_location=device,weights_only=False);assert ck['spec']==spec;model.load_state_dict(ck['model']);opt.load_state_dict(ck['optimizer'])
        epoch=ck['epoch'];cursor=ck['cursor'];history=ck['history'];best=ck['best'];bad=ck['bad'];loss_sum=ck['loss_sum'];seen=ck['seen'];active_seconds=ck['active_seconds'];epoch_elapsed_prior=ck['epoch_elapsed']
        torch.set_rng_state(ck['rng'].cpu())
        if ck['cuda_rng'] is not None:torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all([x.cpu() for x in ck['cuda_rng']])
    epochs=1 if smoke else spec['epochs'];start=time.monotonic();chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0;steps_done=0;last_save=time.monotonic()
    if not (out/'latest.pt').exists():save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint())
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,lambda *_:atomic_json(out/'YIELD_REQUEST.json',dict(reason='signal')))
    try:
        while epoch<epochs and bad<spec['patience']:
            model.train();order=np.random.default_rng(spec['seed']*100000+epoch).permutation(train.n)
            while cursor<train.n:
                requested(out);end=min(cursor+512,train.n);ids=order[cursor:end];opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True);step_loss=0.
                for j in range(0,len(ids),micro):
                    ib=ids[j:j+micro];b,y,_,_=train.batch(ib,device);p=model(b);loss=(p-y).abs().mean()
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss):raise FloatingPointError('nonfinite training loss')
                    (loss*len(ib)/len(ids)).backward();step_loss+=float(loss.detach())*len(ib)
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),1.0,error_if_nonfinite=True);opt.step();loss_sum+=step_loss;seen+=len(ids);cursor=end;steps_done+=1;chunk_steps+=1
                if chunk_steps>=25 or cursor==train.n:
                    elapsed=time.monotonic()-chunk_start;active_seconds+=elapsed
                    atomic_json(out/'progress.json',dict(timestamp=now(),pid=os.getpid(),host=socket.gethostname(),epoch=epoch+1,step=cursor//512,total_steps=(train.n+511)//512,completed_steps=steps_done,chunk_steps=chunk_steps,seconds_per_step=elapsed/chunk_steps,loss=loss_sum/max(seen,1),best_mae=None if best==float('inf') else best,microbatch=micro,gpu_peak_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/2**20 if device.startswith('cuda') else 0,active_seconds=active_seconds))
                    chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0
                if time.monotonic()-last_save>120:save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint());last_save=time.monotonic()
                if max_batches and steps_done>=max_batches:raise YieldJob()
            vm=evaluate(model,valid,max(micro,2*micro),device,out)
            improved=vm['mae']<best
            if improved:
                best=vm['mae'];bad=0;save_torch(out/'best.pt',dict(model=model.state_dict(),spec=spec,epoch=epoch+1,validation=vm,normalization_sha256=sha(ASSETS/'normalization.json'),cohort_sha256=sha(ASSETS/'COHORT.json')))
            else:bad+=1
            row=dict(timestamp=now(),epoch=epoch+1,seconds=epoch_elapsed_prior+time.monotonic()-epoch_started,train_mae=loss_sum/max(seen,1),validation_mae=vm['mae'],validation_rmse=vm['rmse'],best_mae=best)
            history.append(row);atomic_json(out/'history.json',history);print(json.dumps(row),flush=True)
            epoch+=1;cursor=0;loss_sum=0.;seen=0;epoch_elapsed_prior=0.;epoch_started=time.monotonic();save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint());last_save=time.monotonic();chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0
        ck=torch.load(out/'best.pt',map_location=device,weights_only=False);model.load_state_dict(ck['model'])
        evaluation=valid;predict_path=None
        if spec['family']=='B0' and not smoke:
            if spec['fold']>=0:evaluation=SharedData('train',spec,np.load(ASSETS/f'fold{spec["fold"]}.npz')['heldout'])
            predict_path=out/'reference_prediction.npy'
        final=evaluate(model,evaluation,max(512,2*micro),device,out,predict_path)
        result=dict(timestamp=now(),requested_spec=requested_spec,spec=spec,smoke_only=smoke,evaluation_year=2022 if spec['family']=='B0' and spec['fold']>=0 else 2023,validation_selection='2022 internal block' if spec['family']=='B0' and spec['fold']>=0 else '2023 macroMAE',checkpoint_epoch=ck['epoch'],checkpoint_sha256=sha(out/'best.pt'),elapsed_this_session_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,parameter_count=sum(x.numel() for x in model.parameters()),metrics=final)
        if predict_path is not None:result['reference_sha256']=sha(predict_path)
        atomic_json(out/'metrics.json',result);atomic_json(out/'SUCCESS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,smoke_only=smoke));(out/'latest.pt').unlink(missing_ok=True)
    except (YieldJob,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError) as exc:
        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        if isinstance(exc,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError):
            micro//=2
            if micro<32:raise
            atomic_json(ap,dict(microbatch=micro,effective_batch=512))
        else:save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint())
        # OOM rolls back to the last stable checkpoint, including optimizer/RNG;
        # never persist a possibly partial Adam update.
        atomic_json(out/'YIELDED.json',dict(timestamp=now(),reason='cuda_oom' if isinstance(exc,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError) else 'cooperative',epoch=epoch,cursor=cursor));return 75
    return 0

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--job-id',required=True);p.add_argument('--smoke',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--device',default='cuda:0');p.add_argument('--max-batches',type=int);a=p.parse_args()
    specs={s['id']:s for s in json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text())};spec=specs[a.job_id]
    try:rc=run(spec,a.smoke,a.device,a.max_batches)
    except Exception:
        out=(RUN/'smoke_jobs' if a.smoke else JOBS)/spec['id'];atomic_json(out/'FAILURE.json',dict(timestamp=now(),error=traceback.format_exc()));raise
    raise SystemExit(rc)
```

from settings import *
import numpy as np,torch
from physics import thermodynamics,operating_state
from operational import interpolate_nodes

class SharedData:
    def __init__(self,split,spec,rows=None):
        assert split in ['train','val']
        ready=json.loads((DATA/'READY.json').read_text())
        assert ready['passed'] and ready['protocol']=='daily_08_BJT_latency15h'
        assert ready['regional_delay_minutes']==0
        assert spec['preprocess_fingerprint']==ready['preprocess_fingerprint'], 'Stale data/spec combination'
        self.split=split;self.spec=spec;self.root=DATA/split
        self.a={k:np.load(self.root/(k+'.npy'),mmap_mode='r') for k in ['h','y','origin','phase','cycle','offset','nodes','dynamics','near','rings']}
        self.rows=np.arange(len(self.a['h'])) if rows is None else np.asarray(rows)
        self.farms=self.a['h'].shape[1];self.n=len(self.rows)*self.farms
        stats=json.loads((ASSETS/'normalization.json').read_text())['sites']
        self.flat=np.array([stats[str(s)]['flat_threshold'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4')
        self.sm=np.array([stats[str(s)]['sel_mean'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4');self.ss=np.array([stats[str(s)]['sel_std'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4')
        self.rm=np.array([stats[str(s)]['ring_mean'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4');self.rs=np.array([stats[str(s)]['ring_std'] for s in range(self.farms)],'f4')
        self.mu=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_mean.npy');self.sd=np.load(ASSETS/'nwp_std.npy');self.elevation=np.load(ASSETS/'elevation.npy')
        self.rm_history=np.load(DATA/'regional_history'/f'{split}.npy',mmap_mode='r') if spec.get('rm') else None
        self.condition=spec.get('condition','neutral');self.reference=None
        if self.condition in ['cb','cbh','anchor','wrong_cb','wrong_cbh']:
            seed=spec['seed'];self.reference=np.load(DATA/f'reference_r{seed}/{split}.npy',mmap_mode='r')
            if self.condition.startswith('wrong'):
                self.donor=np.load(self.root/f'donor_r{seed}.npy');self.donor_base=np.load(DATA/f'reference_r{seed}/train.npy',mmap_mode='r');self.donor_h=np.load(DATA/'train/h.npy',mmap_mode='r')
        self.total_farms=self.farms
        self.sites=np.array([spec['station']],dtype=int) if 'station' in spec else np.arange(self.total_farms)
        assert ((self.sites>=0)&(self.sites<self.total_farms)).all()
        self.farms=len(self.sites);self.n=len(self.rows)*self.farms

    def batch(self,indices,device='cpu'):
        idx=np.asarray(indices);ti=self.rows[idx//self.farms];local_si=idx%self.farms;si=self.sites[local_si]
        h=np.array(self.a['h'][ti,si]);
        if self.spec.get('history_control')=='constant_current':h[:]=h[:,-1:]
        y=np.asarray(self.a['y'][ti,si]);b=dict(h=h,phase=np.asarray(self.a['phase'][ti]),site=si)
        if self.spec['family']!='B0':
            cy=self.a['cycle'][ti];offset=self.a['offset'][ti]
            weather=interpolate_nodes(self.a['nodes'][cy,si],offset,np.arange(1,25)/4).astype('f4')
            dyn=interpolate_nodes(self.a['dynamics'][cy,si],offset,np.arange(1,25)/4).astype('f4')
            if self.spec.get('weather_control')=='training_mean':weather[:]=0;dyn[:]=0
            p=weather*self.sd+self.mu
            den,stab=thermodynamics(p,self.elevation[si,None]);state=operating_state(h,self.flat[si,None])
            x=np.concatenate([den,dyn,stab,np.broadcast_to(state[:,None],(len(h),24,7))],-1)
            b.update(weather=weather,physical=p,x=((x-self.sm[si,None])/self.ss[si,None]).astype('f4'),region=((self.a['rings'][ti,si]-self.rm[si])/self.rs[si]).astype('f4'),near=np.asarray(self.a['near'][ti,si]))
        if self.spec.get('weather_control')=='training_mean':b['x'][...,:15]=0
        for lo,hi in self.spec.get('mask_sel',[]):b['x'][...,lo:hi]=0
        if self.rm_history is not None:b['rm_history']=np.asarray(self.rm_history[ti,si])
        if self.reference is not None:
            base=np.asarray(self.reference[ti,si]);ch=h
            if self.condition.startswith('wrong'):
                donor=self.donor[ti];base=np.asarray(self.donor_base[donor,si]);ch=np.asarray(self.donor_h[donor,si])
            b.update(base=base,base_delta=base-np.concatenate([ch[:,-1:],base[:,:-1]],-1),condition_h=ch)
        inputs={k:torch.as_tensor(np.array(v),dtype=torch.long if k in ['site','phase'] else torch.float32,device=device) for k,v in b.items()}
        return inputs,torch.as_tensor(np.array(y),device=device),local_si,ti

"""Shared per-farm forecasts. No mixing of examples or future power labels."""
from settings import *
import sys,math,random
import numpy as np,torch
from torch import nn
sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code/legacy'))
sys.path.insert(0,str(RUN/'code/legacy/vendor'))
import baselines_original as baselines
from column_original import column_coordinates
from wind_forcing.models.p0i_legacy import ResidualConv

def seed_all(seed):
    random.seed(seed);np.random.seed(seed);torch.manual_seed(seed)
    if torch.cuda.is_available():torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
def configure():
    torch.set_num_threads(1)
    try:torch.set_num_interop_threads(1)
    except RuntimeError:pass
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32=False;torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32=False;torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark=False
    torch.backends.mha.set_fastpath_enabled(False)
def zero_last(m):
    z=[x for x in m.modules() if isinstance(x,nn.Linear)][-1];nn.init.zeros_(z.weight)
    if z.bias is not None:nn.init.zeros_(z.bias)

class NeighborReadout(nn.Module):
    """Original N3 readout: pooled 8-neighbor history with neighbor geometry keys."""
    def __init__(self,width):
        super().__init__();topo=np.load(ASSETS/'topology.npz')
        self.register_buffer('geometry',torch.from_numpy(topo['geometry']));self.register_buffer('mask',torch.from_numpy(topo['mask']))
        self.query=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(30,32),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(32,32));self.key=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(6,32),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(32,32));self.value=nn.Linear(2,32,bias=False);self.out=nn.Linear(32,width,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.out.weight)
    def forward(self,b):
        h=b['h'];n=len(h);geo=self.geometry[b['site']];k=geo.shape[1]
        p=b['near'][:,None].expand(-1,k,-1);delta=torch.diff(p,dim=-1,prepend=p[:,:,:1]);t=p.shape[-1]
        age=torch.arange(-21,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)/4
        inp=torch.cat([p[...,None],delta[...,None],age[None,None,:,None].expand(n,k,t,1)/6,geo[:,:,None].expand(-1,-1,t,-1)/100],-1)
        key=self.key(inp.flatten(1,2));value=self.value(torch.stack([p,delta],-1).flatten(1,2))
        lead=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)/4;time=torch.stack([lead/6,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead/24),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead/24)],-1)
        q=self.query(torch.cat([h[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1),time[None].expand(n,-1,-1),b['phase'][:,None,None].to(h).expand(-1,24,1)/3,b['physical'][...,[1,2]]/10],-1))
        key=torch.cat([key,h.new_zeros(n,1,32)],1);value=torch.cat([value,h.new_zeros(n,1,32)],1)
        mask=torch.cat([self.mask[b['site']][:,:,None].expand(-1,-1,t).flatten(1),torch.ones(n,1,device=h.device,dtype=torch.bool)],1)
        logits=(q@key.transpose(1,2))/math.sqrt(32);weights=logits.masked_fill(~mask[:,None],-1e4).softmax(-1)
        return self.out(weights@value)

class SharedModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self,spec):
        super().__init__();self.spec=spec;self.family=spec['family'];self.condition=spec.get('condition','neutral');cfg=spec['config'];d=cfg['width'];drop=cfg['dropout'];self.d=d
        seed_all(spec['seed'])
        static=np.load(ASSETS/'site_static.npy');self.register_buffer('static',torch.from_numpy(static));self.embedding=nn.Embedding(len(static),8);self.site_projection=nn.Linear(12,d)
        self.rm=spec.get('rm',False)
        self.has_region=spec.get('input','R3')=='R3' and self.family!='B0'
        if self.family=='B0':
            self.history_projection=nn.Linear(2,d);self.history_encoder=nn.Sequential(*[ResidualConv(d,2**i,drop) for i in range(3)])
            self.query=nn.Linear(4,d);self.attn=nn.MultiheadAttention(d,4,dropout=drop,batch_first=True);self.head=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2*d,d),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,1));zero_last(self.head);return
        if self.family=='W0':
            self.history_projection=nn.Linear(12 if self.rm else 2,d)
            self.history_encoder=nn.TransformerEncoder(nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d,4,2*d,drop,batch_first=True,norm_first=True),2,enable_nested_tensor=False)
            self.weather_variant=spec.get('weather_variant','E2C')
            v=self.weather_variant;nin=14 if v in ['E0','E2L','E2N'] else 15
            if v in ['E0','E1']:self.weather_projection=nn.Linear(nin,d)
            elif v=='E3':self.weather_projection=GroupedWeather(d)
            else:self.weather_projection=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(nin,d),nn.Identity() if v=='E2L' else nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,d),nn.Identity() if v=='E2L' else nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,d))
            self.align_v=nn.Linear(d,d,bias=False);self.align_out=nn.Linear(d,d);self.context_projection=nn.Linear(16,d)
            self.decoder=nn.TransformerDecoder(nn.TransformerDecoderLayer(d,4,2*d,drop,batch_first=True,norm_first=True),2)
            self.head=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,d//2),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d//2,1));zero_last(self.head)
            stats=json.loads((ASSETS/'normalization.json').read_text())
            for name,value in [('column_mean',stats['column_mean']),('column_std',stats['column_std']),('elevation',np.load(ASSETS/'elevation.npy'))]:self.register_buffer(name,torch.as_tensor(value,dtype=torch.float32))
        elif self.family=='TF':
            self.original=baselines.TransformerFutureCov(d);zero_last(self.original.head)
            if self.rm:self.original.h_in=nn.Linear(12,d)
            for m in self.original.modules():
                if isinstance(m,nn.Dropout):m.p=drop
                if isinstance(m,nn.MultiheadAttention):m.dropout=drop
        elif self.family=='TIDE':
            self.original=baselines.TiDEFutureCov(d);self.future_extra=nn.Linear(24*d,2*d,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.future_extra.weight);self.dropout=nn.Dropout(drop)
        elif self.family=='TFT':
            self.hp=nn.Linear(2,d);self.wp=nn.Parameter(torch.randn(14,d)*.02);self.wb=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(14,d));self.select=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14,d),nn.ELU(),nn.Linear(d,14))
            self.hr=nn.LSTM(d,d,2,dropout=drop,batch_first=True);self.wr=nn.LSTM(d,d,2,dropout=drop,batch_first=True);self.attn=nn.MultiheadAttention(d,4,dropout=drop,batch_first=True)
            self.gate=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.GLU());self.norm=nn.LayerNorm(d);self.ff=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.ELU(),nn.Dropout(drop),nn.Linear(2*d,d));self.gate2=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d,2*d),nn.GLU());self.norm2=nn.LayerNorm(d);self.head=nn.Linear(d,1);zero_last(self.head)
        else:raise ValueError(self.family)
        if self.family=='W0' or self.condition!='neutral':
            self.condition_projection=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(d+3,d),nn.GELU(),nn.LayerNorm(d));self.transfer_adapter=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(2*d,d),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(d,d));zero_last(self.transfer_adapter);self.condition_bias=nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1,24,d))
        if self.has_region:
            self.sel=nn.Linear(22,d,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.sel.weight)
            if not self.rm:
                self.regional=nn.Linear(12,d,bias=False);nn.init.zeros_(self.regional.weight);self.neighbor=NeighborReadout(d)

    def condition_response(self,b,w,memory,lead):
        n=len(w);summary=w.new_zeros(n,24,self.d);base=w.new_zeros(n,24);delta=base
        if self.condition in ['cb','cbh','anchor','wrong_cb','wrong_cbh']:
            base=b['base'];delta=b['base_delta']
        if self.condition in ['cbh','wrong_cbh']:
            # Both real and wrong CBH perform the same extra encoder call.
            h=b['condition_h'];clock=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24
            summary=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(torch.stack([h,clock],-1))).mean(1)[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1)
        raw=torch.cat([summary,base[...,None],delta[...,None],lead[...,None]],-1)
        cond=self.condition_projection(raw)+self.condition_bias
        return self.transfer_adapter(torch.cat([w,cond],-1))

    def forward(self,b):
        h=b['h'];n=len(h);site=torch.cat([self.static[b['site']],self.embedding(b['site'])],-1);sv=self.site_projection(site)
        lead=torch.arange(1,25,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(n,-1)/24
        tf=torch.stack([lead,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead),b['phase'][:,None].to(h).expand(-1,24)/3],-1)
        if self.family=='B0':
            clock=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24;memory=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(torch.stack([h,clock],-1))+sv[:,None])
            q=self.query(tf)+sv[:,None];a,_=self.attn(q,memory,memory,need_weights=False);return h[:,-1:]+self.head(torch.cat([q,a],-1)).squeeze(-1)
        extra=sv[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1)
        if self.has_region:
            extra=extra+(self.sel(b['x']) if not self.spec.get('no_sel') else 0)
            if not self.rm:
                if not self.spec.get('no_rings'):extra=extra+self.regional(b['region'])[:,None]
                if not self.spec.get('no_near'):extra=extra+self.neighbor(b)
        if self.family=='W0':
            time=torch.stack([lead,torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead/4),torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead/4)],-1)
            context=torch.cat([time,site[:,None].expand(-1,24,-1),b['phase'][:,None,None].to(h).expand(-1,24,1)/3],-1)
            clock=torch.arange(-23,1,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24
            hin=torch.stack([h,clock],-1)
            if self.rm:hin=torch.cat([hin,b['rm_history']],-1)
            memory=self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(hin)+(sv[:,None] if self.rm else 0))
            col=column_coordinates(b['physical'],self.elevation[b['site']][:,None].expand(-1,24))
            v=self.weather_variant
            wx=(col-self.column_mean)/self.column_std
            if v in ['E0','E2L','E2N']:wx=b['weather']
            elif v in ['E1','E2']:wx=torch.cat([b['weather'],wx[...,4:5]],-1)
            w=self.align_out(self.align_v(self.weather_projection(wx)))
            q=self.context_projection(context)+w+self.condition_response(b,w,memory,lead)+extra
            out=self.head(self.decoder(q,memory)).squeeze(-1);anchor=b['base'] if self.condition=='anchor' else h[:,-1:];return anchor+out
        if self.family=='TF':
            m=self.original;clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)
            hin=torch.stack([h,clock],-1)
            if self.rm:hin=torch.cat([hin,b['rm_history']],-1)
            memory=m.h_enc(m.pe(m.h_in(hin)+sv[:,None]))
            w=m.w_in(torch.cat([b['weather'],tf],-1));q=m.pe(w+extra)
            if hasattr(self,'condition_projection'):q=q+self.condition_response(b,w,memory,lead)
            out=m.dec(q,memory);return h[:,-1:]+m.head(torch.cat([out,h[:,-1:,None].expand(-1,24,1)],-1)).squeeze(-1)
        if self.family=='TIDE':
            m=self.original;clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h);p=m.past(torch.cat([h,clock],-1))+sv
            f=m.future(torch.cat([b['weather'],tf],-1).flatten(1))+self.future_extra(extra.flatten(1));z=torch.cat([p,f],-1)
            for block in m.blocks:z=z+self.dropout(block(z))
            return h[:,-1:]+m.skip(h-h.mean(-1,keepdim=True))+m.decoder(z)
        clock=torch.linspace(-1,0,24,device=h.device,dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h);mem,state=self.hr(self.hp(torch.stack([h,clock],-1))+sv[:,None]);w=b['weather'];tokens=w[...,None]*self.wp+self.wb;weight=self.select(w).softmax(-1)
        z,_=self.wr((tokens*weight[...,None]).sum(2)+extra,state);full=torch.cat([mem,z],1);mask=torch.arange(48,device=h.device)[None]>(24+torch.arange(24,device=h.device)[:,None])
        a,_=self.attn(z,full,full,attn_mask=mask,need_weights=False);z=self.norm(z+self.gate(a));z=self.norm2(z+self.gate2(self.ff(z)));return h[:,-1:]+self.head(z).squeeze(-1)


class GroupedWeather(nn.Module):
    """Three physical groups, scaled to the selected backbone width."""
    def __init__(self,width):
        super().__init__();a=width//4;b=3*width//8;c=width-a-b
        self.groups=nn.ModuleList([nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(5,n),nn.GELU()) for n in [a,b,c]])
        self.out=nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(width,width),nn.GELU(),nn.Linear(width,width))
    def forward(self,x):
        return self.out(torch.cat([m(x[...,5*i:5*i+5]) for i,m in enumerate(self.groups)],-1))
```

"""Prespecified retraining matrix; every seed is fresh, all farms are retained."""
from settings import *
import itertools

def create():
    fp=json.loads((DATA/'READY.json').read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint']
    seeds=SEEDS;specs=[]
    def add(group,family,seed,stage='ablation',inp='R3',**options):
        config_from=family+'_'+inp
        dep=[f'assets/selected/{config_from}.json']
        cond=options.pop('condition','neutral')
        if cond=='cbh':dep.append(f'data/reference_r{seed}/READY.json')
        s=dict(id=f'{group}_r{seed}',stage=stage,group=group,family=family,input=inp,condition=cond,seed=seed,config_from=config_from,epochs=80,patience=10,dependencies=dep,preprocess_fingerprint=fp)
        s.update(options);specs.append(s)
    # W0 local-only requires its own equal-budget tune; TF_BASE already has one.
    configs=[dict(width=w,lr=lr,dropout=d,weight_decay=1e-4) for w,lr,d in itertools.product([64,128],[2e-4,1e-3],[0.,.05])]
    for ci,cfg in enumerate(configs):
        for seed in [40,41]:
            specs.append(dict(id=f'TUNE_W0_BASE_c{ci}_r{seed}',stage='tune',group='W0_BASE',family='W0',input='BASE',condition='neutral',seed=seed,config=cfg,epochs=30,patience=6,dependencies=[],preprocess_fingerprint=fp))
    for seed in seeds:add('SHARED_W0_BASE','W0',seed,stage='shared_local',inp='BASE')
    for condition in ['neutral','cbh']:
        for variant in ['E0','E1','E2L','E2N','E2','E3']:
            for seed in seeds:add(f'ENC_{condition}_{variant}','W0',seed,stage='weather_encoder',condition=condition,weather_variant=variant)
    controls={
        'LOCAL_SEL':dict(no_near=True,no_rings=True),
        'NO_SEL':dict(no_sel=True),
        'NO_NEAR':dict(no_near=True),
        'NO_RINGS':dict(no_rings=True),
        'NO_DENSITY':dict(mask_sel=[[0,3]]),
        'NO_DYNAMICS':dict(mask_sel=[[3,10]]),
        'NO_STABILITY':dict(mask_sel=[[10,15]]),
        'NO_STATE':dict(mask_sel=[[15,22]])}
    for family in ['W0','TF']:
        for label,opts in controls.items():
            for seed in seeds:add(f'INPUT_{family}_{label}',family,seed,stage='input_ablation',**opts)
        for label,opts in {
            'H0W1':dict(history_control='constant_current'),
            'H1W0':dict(weather_control='training_mean'),
            'H0W0':dict(history_control='constant_current',weather_control='training_mean')}.items():
            for seed in seeds:add(f'FACTOR_{family}_{label}',family,seed,stage='history_weather',**opts)
    cohort=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text())
    for station,station_id in enumerate(cohort['station_ids']):
        for seed in seeds:
            for family,inp in itertools.product(['W0','TF'],['BASE','R3']):
                group=f'INDEPENDENT_{family}_{inp}'
                add(group,family,seed,stage='independent',inp=inp,station=station,station_id=station_id)
                specs[-1]['id']=f'{group}_s{station:02d}_r{seed}'
    assert len(specs)==1291 and len({s['id'] for s in specs})==len(specs)
    atomic_json(RUN/'manifest.json',specs)
    from collections import Counter
    atomic_json(RUN/'PROTOCOL.json',dict(timestamp=now(),preprocess_fingerprint=fp,weather_protocol='daily_08_BJT_latency15h',regional_delay_minutes=0,train_year=2022,selection_year=2023,test2024_read=False,seeds=seeds,farms=cohort['count'],budget=dict(Counter(s['stage'] for s in specs)),total_jobs=len(specs),old_protocol_weights_or_metrics_reused=False,initialization='fresh for every job; no pretrained weights',independent='Each optimizer sees only its target farm labels; regional input is the matched factor, using all observed farms through origin. Same train-fitted feature scaling as shared arms; not an unseen-farm transfer experiment.',factorial='H0 repeats current local power across history and recomputes STATE; current-power anchor is retained. W0 replaces weather with training mean, zeroes ALL normalized weather-derived SEL fields; time, site and regional observed power remain. H1W1 is the new shared R3 baseline. Measures local-history x weather given current power and regional observations.',encoders='E0 raw14 affine; E1 raw14+DEM affine; E2L raw14 deep affine; E2N raw14 deep GELU; E2 raw14+DEM GELU; E2C column15 GELU; E3 column groups (quarter,3/8,3/8 width). Neutral and CBH contexts both rerun; E2C controls are the newly trained shared W0_R3 and M2_CBH.',tuning='W0_BASE:8configs x2seeds, same budget as existing families. Other ablations use their own family/input selected config to hold backbone budget fixed. Independent arms reuse same selected config and max80/patience10; same per-farm sample exposure, optimizer update count differs from shared.',dependencies=['daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/COMPLETE.json','daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/COMPLETE.json']))
    print(json.dumps(dict(total=len(specs),stages=dict(Counter(s['stage'] for s in specs)))))
if __name__=='__main__':create()

"""Create a fresh personalization matrix using only completed daily-protocol models."""
from settings import *
import shutil
import numpy as np
import torch


def main():
    shared=PROJECT/'runs/daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1'
    assert (shared/'COMPLETE.json').exists()
    ready=json.loads((shared/'data/READY.json').read_text())
    assert ready['protocol']=='daily_08_BJT_latency15h' and ready['regional_delay_minutes']==0
    fingerprint=ready['preprocess_fingerprint']
    if not DATA.exists():DATA.symlink_to('../daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/data',target_is_directory=True)
    assert DATA.resolve()==(shared/'data').resolve()
    shutil.copytree(shared/'assets',ASSETS,dirs_exist_ok=True)
    for name in ['DATA_FREEZE.json','PROTOCOL.json','QC_RULES.json','SCIENCE_FREEZE.json']:
        shutil.copy2(shared/name,RUN/('SOURCE_'+name))
    cohort=json.loads((ASSETS/'COHORT.json').read_text());sources={}
    for family in ['W0','TF']:
        for seed in SEEDS:
            source=shared/'jobs'/f'BASE_{family}_R3_r{seed}'
            assert (source/'SUCCESS.json').is_file()
            m=json.loads((source/'metrics.json').read_text())
            assert not m['smoke_only'] and m['spec']['preprocess_fingerprint']==fingerprint
            assert sha(source/'best.pt')==m['checkpoint_sha256']
            ck=torch.load(source/'best.pt',map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
            assert ck['normalization_sha256']==sha(ASSETS/'normalization.json')
            assert ck['cohort_sha256']==sha(ASSETS/'COHORT.json')
            shutil.copy2(source/'best.pt',RUN/f'pretrained/{family}_r{seed}.pt')
            shutil.copy2(source/'metrics.json',RUN/f'pretrained/{family}_r{seed}.metrics.json')
            sources[family,seed]=m
    specs=[]
    for station,station_id in enumerate(cohort['station_ids']):
        for seed in SEEDS:
            for mode in ['P1_FULL','P2_HEAD']:
                for family in ['W0','TF']:
                    m=sources[family,seed]
                    specs.append(dict(id=f'{family}_{mode}_s{station:02d}_r{seed}',stage='personalization',group=family+'_'+mode,family=family,mode=mode,station=station,station_id=station_id,seed=seed,config=m['spec']['config'],pretrained=f'pretrained/{family}_r{seed}.pt',source_sha256=m['checkpoint_sha256'],epochs=30,patience=6,finetune_lr=2e-5,weight_decay=1e-4,dependencies=[f'pretrained/{family}_r{seed}.pt'],preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint))
    atomic_json(RUN/'manifest.json',specs)
    atomic_json(RUN/'PROTOCOL.json',dict(timestamp=now(),weather_protocol=ready['protocol'],preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint,source_run=shared.name,regional_delay_minutes=0,farms=cohort['count'],seeds=SEEDS,train_year=2022,selection_year=2023,epochs=30,patience=6,finetune_lr=2e-5,uniform_epochs=[1,6],validation_selection='per-station2023MAE including epoch0; separately report fixedE1 andE6',test2024_read=False,new_farm_transfer_claim=False,previous_protocol_results_reused=False,jobs=len(specs)))
    print('Daily personalization jobs',len(specs),flush=True)


if __name__=='__main__':main()

"""Cache exactly the shared model's inputs, restricted to one target station."""
from settings import *
from data import SharedData
import numpy as np
import torch
import fcntl

class StationData:
    def __init__(self,split,shared_spec,station,smoke=False):
        assert split in ('train','val')
        self.station=int(station);self.farms=1
        folder=RUN/'feature_cache'/f's{station:02d}'/split;folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        ready=folder/'READY.json';source_hash=sha(RUN/'SOURCE_DATA_FREEZE.json')
        with (folder/'build.lock').open('a+') as lock:
            fcntl.flock(lock,fcntl.LOCK_EX)
            if not ready.exists():
                source=SharedData(split,shared_spec);n=len(source.rows);arrays={}
                assert 0<=station<source.farms and source.reference is None
                for start in range(0,n,1024):
                    ix=np.arange(start,min(start+1024,n))
                    b,y,si,ti=source.batch(ix*source.farms+station,'cpu')
                    assert (si==station).all() and np.array_equal(ti,ix)
                    values={k:v.numpy() for k,v in b.items()};values['y']=y.numpy()
                    for key,v in values.items():
                        if key not in arrays:arrays[key]=np.lib.format.open_memmap(folder/(key+'.npy'),mode='w+',dtype=v.dtype,shape=(n,*v.shape[1:]))
                        assert np.isfinite(v).all(),key
                        arrays[key][start:start+len(ix)]=v
                for arr in arrays.values():arr.flush()
                origin=np.asarray(source.a['origin']);years=(origin+28800).astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[Y]').astype(int)+1970
                assert (years==(2022 if split=='train' else 2023)).all()
                if split=='val':assert not (((origin+28800).astype('datetime64[s]').astype('datetime64[M]').astype(int)%12+1)==11).any()
                atomic_json(ready,dict(timestamp=now(),source_hash=source_hash,station=station,split=split,n=n,keys=list(arrays),passed=True))
                del source,arrays
            meta=json.loads(ready.read_text());assert meta['source_hash']==source_hash and meta['station']==station and meta['passed']
        self.a={k:np.load(folder/(k+'.npy'),mmap_mode='r') for k in meta['keys']}
        self.rows=np.arange(meta['n']) if not smoke else np.linspace(0,meta['n']-1,128,dtype=int)
        self.n=len(self.rows)

    def batch(self,indices,device='cpu'):
        ix=self.rows[np.asarray(indices)]
        b={k:torch.as_tensor(np.array(v[ix]),device=device) for k,v in self.a.items() if k!='y'}
        y=torch.as_tensor(np.array(self.a['y'][ix]),device=device)
        assert (b['site']==self.station).all()
        return b,y,np.zeros(len(ix),dtype=int),ix
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/code/train.py

"""Per-station adaptation of immutable shared checkpoints; no 2024 access."""
from settings import *
from model import SharedModel,configure,seed_all
from site_data import StationData
from train_shared import evaluate,save_torch,YieldJob,requested
import torch,numpy as np,time,socket,argparse,traceback,signal

def load_model(spec,device):
    path=RUN/spec['pretrained'];assert sha(path)==spec['source_sha256']
    source=torch.load(path,map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
    model=SharedModel(source['spec']).to(device);model.load_state_dict(source['model'])
    if spec['mode']=='P2_HEAD':
        for p in model.parameters():p.requires_grad_(False)
        head=model.head if spec['family']=='W0' else model.original.head
        for p in head.parameters():p.requires_grad_(True)
        model.embedding.weight.requires_grad_(True)
    else:assert spec['mode']=='P1_FULL'
    # Only the target row is personalized, and AdamW must not decay other rows.
    mask=torch.zeros_like(model.embedding.weight);mask[spec['station']]=1
    model.embedding.weight.register_hook(lambda grad:grad*mask)
    params=[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if p.requires_grad and n!='embedding.weight']
    optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW([dict(params=params,weight_decay=spec['weight_decay']),dict(params=[model.embedding.weight],weight_decay=0.)],lr=spec['finetune_lr'])
    return model,optimizer,source['spec']

def run(spec,smoke=False,device='cuda:0',max_batches=None):
    configure();out=(RUN/'smoke_jobs' if smoke else JOBS)/spec['id'];out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    if (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():return 0
    atomic_json(out/'spec.json',spec)
    model,opt,shared_spec=load_model(spec,device)
    train=StationData('train',shared_spec,spec['station'],smoke);valid=StationData('val',shared_spec,spec['station'],smoke)
    seed_all(spec['seed']*1000+spec['station'])
    epoch=0;cursor=0;history=[];best=float('inf');bad=0;loss_sum=0.;seen=0;active_seconds=0.;micro=512
    ap=out/'adaptive_microbatch.json'
    if ap.exists():micro=int(json.loads(ap.read_text())['microbatch'])
    baseline=None;epoch_started=time.monotonic();epoch_elapsed_prior=0.
    def checkpoint():
        return dict(spec=spec,shared_spec=shared_spec,model=model.state_dict(),optimizer=opt.state_dict(),epoch=epoch,cursor=cursor,history=history,best=best,bad=bad,loss_sum=loss_sum,seen=seen,active_seconds=active_seconds,baseline=baseline,
            epoch_elapsed=epoch_elapsed_prior+time.monotonic()-epoch_started,rng=torch.get_rng_state(),cuda_rng=torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all() if device.startswith('cuda') else None)
    def save_best(at,metrics):
        save_torch(out/'best.pt',dict(model=model.state_dict(),spec=spec,shared_spec=shared_spec,epoch=at,validation=metrics,source_sha256=spec['source_sha256'],normalization_sha256=sha(ASSETS/'normalization.json'),cohort_sha256=sha(ASSETS/'COHORT.json')))
    if (out/'latest.pt').exists():
        ck=torch.load(out/'latest.pt',map_location=device,weights_only=False);assert ck['spec']==spec
        model.load_state_dict(ck['model']);opt.load_state_dict(ck['optimizer'])
        epoch=ck['epoch'];cursor=ck['cursor'];history=ck['history'];best=ck['best'];bad=ck['bad'];loss_sum=ck['loss_sum'];seen=ck['seen'];active_seconds=ck['active_seconds'];epoch_elapsed_prior=ck['epoch_elapsed'];baseline=ck['baseline']
        torch.set_rng_state(ck['rng'].cpu())
        if ck['cuda_rng'] is not None:torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all([x.cpu() for x in ck['cuda_rng']])
    else:
        prediction_path=out/'p0_prediction.npy' if not smoke and spec['mode']=='P1_FULL' else None
        baseline=evaluate(model,valid,1024,device,pred_path=prediction_path)
        if not smoke:
            source=json.loads((RUN/'pretrained'/f"{spec['family']}_r{spec['seed']}.metrics.json").read_text())['metrics']
            for key in ['mae','rmse']:assert np.isclose(baseline[key],source['per_farm_'+key][spec['station']],rtol=2e-5,atol=2e-7),(spec['id'],key,baseline[key],source['per_farm_'+key][spec['station']])
        best=baseline['mae'];save_best(0,baseline);atomic_json(out/'epoch0.json',baseline);epoch_started=time.monotonic();save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint())
    epochs=2 if smoke else spec['epochs'];start=time.monotonic();chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0;steps_done=0;last_save=time.monotonic()
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,lambda *_:atomic_json(out/'YIELD_REQUEST.json',dict(reason='signal')))
    try:
        while epoch<epochs and bad<spec['patience']:
            model.train();order=np.random.default_rng(spec['seed']*100000+spec['station']*100+epoch).permutation(train.n)
            while cursor<train.n:
                requested(out);end=min(cursor+512,train.n);ids=order[cursor:end];opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True);step_loss=0.
                for j in range(0,len(ids),micro):
                    ib=ids[j:j+micro];b,y,_,_=train.batch(ib,device);pred=model(b);loss=(pred-y).abs().mean()
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss):raise FloatingPointError('nonfinite loss')
                    (loss*len(ib)/len(ids)).backward();step_loss+=float(loss.detach())*len(ib)
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),1.,error_if_nonfinite=True);opt.step();loss_sum+=step_loss;seen+=len(ids);cursor=end;steps_done+=1;chunk_steps+=1
                if chunk_steps>=10 or cursor==train.n:
                    elapsed=time.monotonic()-chunk_start;active_seconds+=elapsed
                    atomic_json(out/'progress.json',dict(timestamp=now(),pid=os.getpid(),host=socket.gethostname(),epoch=epoch+1,step=(cursor+511)//512,total_steps=(train.n+511)//512,completed_steps=steps_done,chunk_steps=chunk_steps,seconds_per_step=elapsed/chunk_steps,loss=loss_sum/max(seen,1),best_mae=best,microbatch=micro,gpu_peak_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/2**20 if device.startswith('cuda') else 0,active_seconds=active_seconds))
                    chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0

        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        if isinstance(exc,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError):
            micro//=2

            atomic_json(ap,dict(microbatch=micro,effective_batch=512))
        else:save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint())

    return 0

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_site_finetune_20261114_v1/code/z1.py
