"""Frozen strictly causal rolling correction; no fitting on evaluation labels."""
import numpy as np

GROUPS=[('0_1h',0,4),('1_3h',4,12),('3_6h',12,24)]
BINS=np.array([.1,.3,.7])

def correction(origin,pred,y,days=30,exclude_hours=0,binned=True):
    origin=np.asarray(origin,dtype=np.int64)
    assert np.all(np.diff(origin)>0) and pred.shape==y.shape==(len(origin),24)
    assert np.isfinite(pred).all() and np.isfinite(y).all()
    bins=np.digitize(pred,BINS);residual=y.astype('f8')-pred
    out=np.zeros(pred.shape,'f8');fallback=np.zeros(3,'i8')
    lo=np.searchsorted(origin,origin-days*86400,side='left')
    for gi,(_,a,b) in enumerate(GROUPS):
        sums=np.zeros((len(origin),4));counts=np.zeros((len(origin),4),'i8')
        for k in range(a,b):
            # Contributions are from past ORIGINS within D days whose TARGET
            # has been observed through t (zero observation delay). This remains correct across gaps.
            hi=np.searchsorted(origin,origin-int(exclude_hours*3600)-(k+1)*900,side='right')
            hi=np.maximum(hi,lo)
            for g in range(4):
                ok=bins[:,k]==g
                cs=np.r_[0.,np.cumsum(np.where(ok,residual[:,k],0.))]
                cn=np.r_[0,np.cumsum(ok)]
                sums[:,g]+=cs[hi]-cs[lo];counts[:,g]+=cn[hi]-cn[lo]
        su=sums.sum(1);ct=counts.sum(1)
        uniform=np.divide(su,ct,out=np.zeros_like(su),where=ct>=24)
        for k in range(a,b):
            c=counts[np.arange(len(origin)),bins[:,k]]
            v=sums[np.arange(len(origin)),bins[:,k]]
            out[:,k]=np.where(c>=24,v/np.maximum(1,c),uniform) if binned else uniform
            fallback[0]+=np.sum(c<24);fallback[1]+=np.sum((c<24)&(ct>=24));fallback[2]+=np.sum(ct<24)
    return out,dict(bin_under24=int(fallback[0]),uniform_fallback=int(fallback[1]),zero_fallback=int(fallback[2]),total=int(pred.size))

def metrics(pred,y,high):
    err=pred.astype('f8')-y
    result=dict(mae=float(abs(err).mean()),rmse=float(np.sqrt((err**2).mean())),
                lead_mae=abs(err).mean(0).tolist(),lead_mse=(err**2).mean(0).tolist(),
                high_power_rmse=float(np.sqrt((err[high]**2).mean())) if np.any(high) else None,
                high_power_count=int(np.sum(high)),count=len(y))
    result['horizons']={name:dict(mae=float(abs(err[:,a:b]).mean()),rmse=float(np.sqrt((err[:,a:b]**2).mean()))) for name,a,b in GROUPS}
    return result

def analyze_predictions(origin,pred,y,high):
    result={'raw':metrics(pred,y,high)}
    for name,days,gap,binned in [('bin30',30,0,True),('bin7',7,0,True),('bin14',14,0,True),('bin30_exclude24h',30,24,True),('uniform30',30,0,False)]:
        c,f=correction(origin,pred,y,days,gap,binned)
        result[name]=metrics(pred+c,y,high)
        result[name]['fallback']=f
    return result

def self_test():
    # Explicit brute-force test includes exact time/bin boundaries, a large gap,
    # overlapping horizons, the 24-count fallback and the 24-hour exclusion.
    rng=np.random.default_rng(4)
    origin=np.r_[np.arange(45)*900,np.arange(100,135)*900,np.arange(300,340)*900]
    pred=rng.choice([-.1,.1,.2,.3,.6,.7,.9],size=(len(origin),24));y=rng.normal(.4,.2,pred.shape)
    for days,gap in [(1,0),(1,24),(30,0),(30,24)]:
        actual,_=correction(origin,pred,y,days,gap)
        expected=np.zeros_like(actual);bins=np.digitize(pred,BINS)
        for i,t in enumerate(origin):
            for _,a,b in GROUPS:
                target=origin[:,None]+np.arange(a+1,b+1)[None]*900
                ok=(origin[:,None]>=t-days*86400)&(target<=t-gap*3600)
                for k in range(a,b):
                    chosen=ok&(bins[:,a:b]==bins[i,k])
                    if chosen.sum()<24:chosen=ok
                    if chosen.sum()>=24:expected[i,k]=(y[:,a:b]-pred[:,a:b])[chosen].mean()
        assert np.allclose(actual,expected,atol=1e-12)
    p2=pred.copy();y2=y.copy();p2[50:]+=100;y2[50:]-=100
    a,_=correction(origin,pred,y);b,_=correction(origin,p2,y2)
    assert np.array_equal(a[:50],b[:50]),'future labels/predictions affected earlier outputs'
    return True
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/data.py

"""Same frozen samples as R0, with the eight available forecast nodes exposed."""
from base_data import SharedData as OriginalData
from settings import DATA
from physics import operating_state
import numpy as np
import torch


class SharedData(OriginalData):
    def __init__(self, split, spec, rows=None):
        super().__init__(split, spec, rows)
        self.wrong_state = spec.get('representation') == 'R4-wrongS'
        if self.wrong_state:
            self.state_donor = np.load(self.root / f'donor_r{spec["seed"]}.npy')
            self.donor_history = np.load(DATA/'train/h.npy', mmap_mode='r')
            self.donor_rings = np.load(DATA/'train/rings.npy', mmap_mode='r')

    def batch(self, indices, device='cpu'):
        inputs, y, local_si, ti = super().batch(indices, device)
        si = self.sites[local_si]
        cy = self.a['cycle'][ti]
        inputs['hourly'] = torch.as_tensor(np.array(self.a['nodes'][cy, si]), device=device)
        inputs['offset'] = torch.as_tensor(np.array(self.a['offset'][ti]), device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
        inputs['sample_key'] = torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(self.a['origin'][ti])//900*55+si,
                                             device=device, dtype=torch.long)
        h = np.array(self.a['h'][ti, si])
        rings = np.array(self.a['rings'][ti, si])
        if self.wrong_state:
            donor = self.state_donor[ti]
            h = np.array(self.donor_history[donor, si])
            rings = np.array(self.donor_rings[donor, si])
        state = operating_state(h, self.flat[si, None])
        state = (state-self.sm[si, 15:22])/self.ss[si, 15:22]
        rings = (rings-self.rm[si])/self.rs[si]
        # The unchanged 24-point history spans 23 intervals = 5.75 hours.
        c = np.concatenate([h[:, -1:], 1-h[:, -1:], (h[:, -1:]-h[:, -5:-4]),
                            (h[:, -1:]-h[:, :1])/5.75, state,
                            rings[:, [0, 2, 4, 6, 8, 10]]], axis=-1).astype('f4')
        inputs['state_c'] = torch.as_tensor(c, device=device)
        return inputs, y, local_si, ti
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/diagnostics.py

```
"""Descriptive pooled diagnostics; checkpoint selection still uses farm-macro MAE."""
import collections
import numpy as np


class Diagnostics:
    def __init__(self):
        self.bins = collections.defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(5, dtype='f8'))
        self.diversity = np.zeros(3, dtype='f8')
        self.median_error = np.zeros(3, dtype='f8')

    def update(self, b, y, p, samples=None):
        y=y.cpu().numpy().astype('f8');p=p.cpu().numpy().astype('f8')
        h=b['h'][:, -1:].cpu().numpy().astype('f8');err=p-y;dy=y-h;dp=p-h
        phase=(b['phase'].cpu().numpy()[:, None]+np.arange(1,25)[None])%4
        masks={f'clock_{int(q)*15:02d}':phase==q for q in range(4)}
        masks.update(high_up=(h>=.75)&(dy>0), high_down=(h>=.75)&(dy<0))
        for name,a,z in [('0_1h',0,4),('1_3h',4,12),('3_6h',12,24)]:
            mask=np.zeros_like(dy,dtype=bool);mask[:,a:z]=abs(dy[:,a:z])>.3
            masks['large_change_'+name]=mask
        for name,mask in masks.items():
            self.bins[name]+=np.array([mask.sum(),abs(err[mask]).sum(),(err[mask]**2).sum(),
                                      (dp*np.sign(dy))[mask].sum(),abs(dy[mask]).sum()])
        if samples is not None:
            draws=samples.cpu().numpy().astype('f8');std=draws.std(1)
            self.diversity+=np.array([std.size,std.sum(),(std>1e-6).sum()])
            median=np.median(draws,axis=1)-y
            self.median_error+=np.array([median.size,abs(median).sum(),(median**2).sum()])

    def result(self):
        bins={}
        for name,(n,ab,sq,cap,den) in self.bins.items():
            bins[name]=dict(count=int(n),mae=ab/n if n else None,mse=sq/n if n else None,
                            capture_ratio=cap/den if den else None,
                            abs_error_sum=ab,squared_error_sum=sq,capture_numerator=cap,capture_denominator=den)
        result=dict(aggregation='pooled diagnostic cells; not farm-macro selection metrics',bins=bins)
        n,sd,positive=self.diversity
        if n:
            count,ab,sq=self.median_error
            result.update(sample_std_mean=sd/n,nonzero_sample_std_fraction=positive/n,
                          sample_median_mae=ab/count,sample_median_rmse=float(np.sqrt(sq/count)))
        return result
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/model.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/model.py

SHA-256: 4068ca2dbdb561bceeb2c48147b046850356879717d17835d0480c860e39c15b

```
"""V2 changes only the weather-query path, plus the explicit R0+H control."""
from backbone import SharedModel as Backbone, configure, seed_all, zero_last
from representation import Window, Teacher, Flow, lerp, kernels, stateless_noise
import torch
from torch import nn


class WeatherTimeE2L(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d):
        super().__init__()
        self.weather = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(14, d), nn.Identity(), nn.Linear(d, d),
                                     nn.Identity(), nn.Linear(d, d))
        self.time = nn.Linear(4, d, bias=False)

    def forward(self, x):
        return self.weather(x[..., :14])+self.time(x[..., 14:])


class SharedModel(Backbone):
    def __init__(self, spec):
        super().__init__(spec)
        self.representation = spec.get('representation', 'R0')
        self.generative = self.representation.startswith('R4')
        # Preserve all existing backbone parameters and its initialization RNG.
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            # Seed CPU initialization only: torch.manual_seed also resets CUDA,
            # which fork_rng(devices=[]) intentionally does not snapshot.
            torch.default_generator.manual_seed(spec['seed']+20261116)
            if self.family == 'TF' and self.representation != 'TF_R3':
                self.original.w_in = WeatherTimeE2L(self.d)
            if self.representation == 'R0+H':
                old = self.head[0]
                new = nn.Linear(self.d+2, old.out_features)
                with torch.no_grad():
                    new.weight[:, :self.d].copy_(old.weight)
                    new.weight[:, self.d:].zero_()
                    new.bias.copy_(old.bias)
                self.head[0] = new
            if self.representation in ['R2', 'R3'] or self.generative:
                self.window = Window(self.d, self.representation not in ['R2', 'R4-noS'])
                self.residual_projection = nn.Linear(4, self.d, bias=False)
            if self.generative:
                self.teacher = Teacher(self.d)
                self.flow = Flow()
                # At initialization, stochastic models also exactly recover R1.
                nn.init.zeros_(self.residual_projection.weight)

    def latent(self, b):
        projection = self.weather_projection if self.family == 'W0' else self.original.w_in.weather
        z = projection(b['hourly'])
        return z, lerp(z, b['offset'])

    def decode(self, b, weather):
        h = b['h']; n = len(h)
        site = torch.cat([self.static[b['site']], self.embedding(b['site'])], -1)
        sv = self.site_projection(site)
        lead = torch.arange(1, 25, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype)[None].expand(n, -1)/24
        extra = sv[:, None].expand(-1, 24, -1)
        if self.has_region:
            extra = extra+self.sel(b['x'])+self.regional(b['region'])[:, None]+self.neighbor(b)
        if self.family == 'W0':
            time = torch.stack([lead, torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead/4), torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead/4)], -1)
            context = torch.cat([time, site[:, None].expand(-1, 24, -1),
                                 b['phase'][:, None, None].to(h).expand(-1, 24, 1)/3], -1)
            clock = torch.arange(-23, 1, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)/24
            memory = self.history_encoder(self.history_projection(torch.stack([h, clock], -1)))
            w = self.align_out(self.align_v(weather))
            q = self.context_projection(context)+w+self.condition_response(b, w, memory, lead)+extra
            hidden = self.decoder(q, memory)
            if self.representation == 'R0+H':
                level = torch.stack([h[:, -1], 1-h[:, -1]], -1)
                hidden = torch.cat([hidden, level[:, None].expand(-1, 24, -1)], -1)
            return h[:, -1:]+self.head(hidden).squeeze(-1)
        m = self.original
        clock = torch.linspace(-1, 0, 24, device=h.device, dtype=h.dtype).expand_as(h)
        memory = m.h_enc(m.pe(m.h_in(torch.stack([h, clock], -1))+sv[:, None]))
        tf = torch.stack([lead, torch.sin(2*torch.pi*lead), torch.cos(2*torch.pi*lead),
                          b['phase'][:, None].to(h).expand(-1, 24)/3], -1)
        q = m.pe(weather+m.w_in.time(tf)+extra)
        out = m.dec(q, memory)
        return h[:, -1:]+m.head(torch.cat([out, h[:, -1:, None].expand(-1, 24, 1)], -1)).squeeze(-1)

    def with_parameters(self, b, base, params):
        return self.decode(b, base+self.residual_projection(kernels(b['offset'])@params))

    def sampled_power(self, b, base, params):
        n, count = params.shape[:2]
        repeated = {k: v[:, None].expand(-1, count, *v.shape[1:]).reshape(n*count, *v.shape[1:])
                    for k, v in b.items()}
        power = self.with_parameters(repeated, base[:, None].expand(-1, count, -1, -1).reshape(n*count, 24, self.d),
                                     params.reshape(n*count, 7, 4))
        return power.reshape(n, count, 24)

    def forward(self, b):
        if self.representation in ['R0', 'TF_R3', 'TF_E2L']:
            return super().forward(b)
        if self.representation == 'R0+H':
            return self.decode(b, self.weather_projection(b['weather']))
        z, base = self.latent(b)
        if self.representation == 'R1':
            return self.decode(b, base)
        if not self.generative:
            return self.with_parameters(b, base, self.window(z, b['offset'], b['state_c']))
        return self.prediction_samples(b, z, base).mean(1)

    def prediction_samples(self, b, z=None, base=None):
        if z is None:
            z, base = self.latent(b)
        context = self.window.features(z, b['offset'], b['state_c'])
        noise = stateless_noise(b['sample_key'], 8, self.spec['seed'], z.dtype)
        params = self.flow.sample(noise, context)
        return self.sampled_power(b, base, params)

    def training_loss(self, b, y):
        mse_weight = self.spec.get('mse_weight', 0.0)
        def point(p):
            return (p-y).abs().mean()+mse_weight*(p-y).square().mean()
        if not self.generative:
            return point(self(b))
        z, base = self.latent(b)
        at_origin = lerp(z, b['offset'], z.new_zeros(1))
        target, kl = self.teacher(y-b['h'][:, -1:], base-at_origin, b['offset'])
        teacher_power = self.with_parameters(b, base, target)
        context = self.window.features(z, b['offset'], b['state_c'])
        noise = torch.randn_like(target)
        t = torch.rand(len(y), device=y.device, dtype=y.dtype)
        detached = target.detach()
        mixed = (1-t[:, None, None])*noise+t[:, None, None]*detached
        matching = (self.flow(mixed, t, context)-(detached-noise)).square().mean()
        student_noise = torch.randn(len(y), 4, 7, 4, device=y.device, dtype=y.dtype)
        student_params = self.flow.sample(student_noise, context)
        student_power = self.sampled_power(b, base, student_params).mean(1)
        return point(teacher_power)+point(student_power)+0.1*matching+0.001*kl
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/plan.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/plan.py

SHA-256: 2dc14a4467fdd01c0c9df06263480f46c04f87babf8e33922b083cfd756b1d87

```
"""Predeclare all conditional jobs; future activation never changes the science specs."""
from settings import *
import argparse, shutil

def main(freeze=False):
    if freeze:
        assert json.loads((RUN/'operations/CHECKS.json').read_text())['passed']
        names=[p for p in (RUN/'code').rglob('*.py')]
        names += [RUN/'manifest.json',RUN/'diagnostic_manifest.json',RUN/'PROTOCOL.json']
        names += [p for p in ASSETS.rglob('*') if p.is_file() and p.name!='selected_candidate.json']
        names += list((RUN/'reference_metrics').rglob('*.json'))
        values={str(p.relative_to(RUN)):sha(p) for p in sorted(names)}
        path=RUN/'SCIENCE_FREEZE.json'
        if path.exists():assert json.loads(path.read_text())['files']==values,'Refuse changing a frozen experiment'
        else:atomic_json(path,dict(timestamp=now(),files=values))
        return
    assert not (RUN/'SCIENCE_FREEZE.json').exists()
    fingerprint=json.loads((DATA/'READY.json').read_text())['preprocess_fingerprint']
    specs=[]
    stages=[('stage1',['R0+H','R1','R2','R3','TF_E2L'],None),
            ('stage2',['R4','R4-noS','R4-wrongS'],'operations/ENABLE_STAGE2.json'),
            ('cross_backbone',['TF_R4'],'operations/ENABLE_TF_R4.json'),
            ('loss_review',['LOSS_CANDIDATE','LOSS_TF'],'operations/ENABLE_LOSS.json')]
    for stage,groups,gate in stages:
        for group in groups:
            for seed in SEEDS:
                family='TF' if group in ['TF_E2L','TF_R4','LOSS_TF'] else 'W0'
                rep={'TF_R4':'R4','LOSS_CANDIDATE':'SELECTED','LOSS_TF':'TF_R3'}.get(group,group)
                specs.append(dict(id=f'{group.replace("+","plus").replace("-","_")}_r{seed}',
                    stage=stage,group=group,representation=rep,family=family,input='R3',condition='neutral',
                    weather_variant='E2L',seed=seed,config_from=family+'_R3',epochs=80,patience=10,
                    mse_weight=.2 if stage=='loss_review' else 0.,
                    dependencies=[f'assets/selected/{family}_R3.json']+([gate] if gate else []),
                    preprocess_fingerprint=fingerprint))
    atomic_json(RUN/'manifest.json',specs)
    diagnostic=[]
    for group,source_run,source_group in [('R0','daily_ablation_restart_20261114_v1','ENC_neutral_E2L'),
                                          ('TF_R3','daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1','TF_R3')]:
        for source in json.loads((RUN.parent/source_run/'manifest.json').read_text()):
            if source['group']!=source_group or source['seed'] not in SEEDS or source['stage']=='tune':continue
            rel=f'assets/reference_weights/{group}_r{source["seed"]}.pt'
            target=RUN/rel;target.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(RUN.parent/source_run/'jobs'/source['id']/'best.pt',target)
            metric=json.loads((RUN/'reference_metrics'/group/str(source['seed'])/'metrics.json').read_text())
            assert sha(target)==metric['checkpoint_sha256']
            spec=dict(source,id=f'REFERENCE_{group}_r{source["seed"]}',group='REFERENCE_'+group,
                      representation=group,stage='diagnostics',task='reference_evaluation',
                      checkpoint=rel,checkpoint_sha256=sha(target),dependencies=[],mse_weight=0.)
            diagnostic.append(spec)
    atomic_json(RUN/'diagnostic_manifest.json',diagnostic)
    # Eight jobs on four local GPUs; seventeen on eight V100s (one queued).
    assignments={}
    for stage,_,_ in stages:
        jobs=[s for s in specs if s['stage']==stage]
        for i,s in enumerate(jobs):assignments[s['id']]='local4090' if i%3==1 else 'v1006001'
    for i,s in enumerate(diagnostic):assignments[s['id']]='local4090' if i%3==1 else 'v1006001'
    atomic_json(RUN/'operations/ASSIGNMENTS.json',assignments)
    atomic_json(RUN/'operations/RESOURCES.json',dict(local4090=dict(gpus=[0,1,2,3],target_per_gpu=2),
                v1006001=dict(gpus=list(range(8)),target_per_gpu=2),minimum_free_mib=3072,
                memory_reserve_fraction=.15,max_workers_per_gpu=3,
                note='At least two processes on each used GPU when enough jobs and memory; third only with measured aggregate throughput gain.'))
    atomic_json(RUN/'PROTOCOL.json',dict(version=2,date='2026-11-16',data_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        task='single-farm 0-6h every 15min; shared training over 55 farms',train_year=2022,selection_year=2023,
        pangu_issue_utc=0,latency_hours=15,crop_leads=[9,45],regional_delay_minutes=0,
        effective_batch=512,optimizer='AdamW',lr=.0002,weight_decay=.0001,dropout=.05,
        checkpoint_selection='strict minimum 2023 farm-macro MAE; once each epoch; no RMSE tie break',
        generator=dict(width=32,layers=1,heads=2,dropout=0.,hourly_nodes=8,segments=7,parameters_per_segment=4,
                       kernel='normalized Gaussian centers at hourly-segment midpoints, sigma=0.75h',
                       state_dimension=17,history_span_hours=5.75),
        flow=dict(euler_steps=8,training_samples=4,evaluation_samples=8,posterior_prior_std=.1,
                  loss='teacher MAE + student-mean MAE + 0.1 flow MSE + 0.001 posterior KL',
                  posterior_target_detached_for_flow=True,evaluation_noise='stateless per origin/site/seed/sample',
                  inference_teacher=False,dispersion='reported; mean-of-4 MAE does not guarantee noncollapse'),
        stage1_runs=25,stage2_conditional_runs=15,cross_backbone_conditional_runs=5,loss_review_runs=10,
        main_gate=dict(mae_change_percent_max=-.1,rmse_change_percent_max=-.1,farm_wins_min=28,
                       both_metrics_seed_wins_min=4,high_power_rmse_change_percent_max=.1,
                       references=['R0','TF_R3','TF_E2L']),
        stage2_gate='R3 strictly improves macro MAE and RMSE versus R2',
        mechanisms=dict(R3=['R2','R0+H'],R4=['R3','R4-noS','R4-wrongS']),
        loss_candidate_rule='R4 if mechanism passes; else R3 if mechanism passes; else R1 if main gate passes; else R0',
        loss_adoption='candidate beats loss-matched TF in MAE/RMSE and itself not worse than its MAE-trained version',
        limitations=['R0 selected among seven encodings on 2023','2023 is not an independent test set',
                     '2024 previously inspected; later review is exploratory','R1 and R0 are affine-equivalent'],
        baseline_reuse='five existing R0 and five existing TF_R3; no changes to frozen original runs'))
    shutil.copyfile(RUN.parent/'daily_protocol_restart_20261114_v1/DATA_FREEZE.json',RUN/'DATA_FREEZE.json')
    print(json.dumps(dict(stage1=25,conditional=30,all_specs=len(specs))))

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--freeze',action='store_true');main(p.parse_args().freeze)

"""Re-evaluate frozen R0/TF weights for the newly required diagnostics; no training."""
from settings import *
from train import resolve,evaluate
from data import SharedData
from model import SharedModel,configure
import argparse,torch,traceback

def main(jid):
    configure()
    requested={s['id']:s for s in json.loads((RUN/'diagnostic_manifest.json').read_text())}[jid]
    spec=resolve(requested);out=JOBS/jid;out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    path=RUN/spec['checkpoint'];assert sha(path)==spec['checkpoint_sha256']
    ck=torch.load(path,map_location='cpu',weights_only=False)
    model=SharedModel(spec).cuda();model.load_state_dict(ck['model'])
    data=SharedData('val',spec);metrics=evaluate(model,data,1024,'cuda:0',diagnostics=True)
    group=spec['representation'];reference=json.loads((RUN/'reference_metrics'/group/str(spec['seed'])/'metrics.json').read_text())
    for key in ['mae','rmse','high_power_rmse']:assert abs(metrics[key]-reference['metrics'][key])<2e-7,(key,metrics[key],reference['metrics'][key])
    atomic_json(out/'metrics.json',dict(timestamp=now(),requested_spec=requested,spec=spec,smoke_only=False,
                evaluation_year=2023,checkpoint_epoch=ck['epoch'],checkpoint_sha256=sha(path),
                parameter_count=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()),metrics=metrics,no_training=True))
    atomic_json(out/'SUCCESS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,no_training=True))

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--job-id',required=True);args=p.parse_args()
    try:main(args.job_id)
    except Exception:
        atomic_json(JOBS/args.job_id/'FAILURE.json',dict(timestamp=now(),error=traceback.format_exc()));raise

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/representation.py

- project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/representation.py

SHA-256: 448ea4bba13f76c127f3f024ef2d97b69caed1dc5dac43d0f40360c4d3f7dfa2

```
"""Hourly-window residuals and a training-only posterior for conditional flow matching."""
import math
import torch
from torch import nn


def lerp(nodes, offset, queries=None):
    if queries is None:
        queries = torch.arange(1, 25, device=nodes.device, dtype=nodes.dtype)/4
    x = queries[None]-offset[:, :1]
    lo = x.floor().long().clamp(0, nodes.shape[1]-2)
    frac = (x-lo)[..., None]
    left = torch.gather(nodes, 1, lo[..., None].expand(-1, -1, nodes.shape[-1]))
    right = torch.gather(nodes, 1, (lo+1)[..., None].expand(-1, -1, nodes.shape[-1]))
    return left*(1-frac)+right*frac


def kernels(offset):
    query = torch.arange(1, 25, device=offset.device, dtype=offset.dtype)/4
    centers = offset[:, :-1]+0.5
    weights = torch.exp(-0.5*((query[None, :, None]-centers[:, None])/0.75)**2)
    return weights/weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)


def encoder(width=32):
    layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(width, 2, width*2, dropout=0.0,
                                      activation='gelu', batch_first=True, norm_first=True)
    return nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, 1, enable_nested_tensor=False)


class Window(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, d, state):
        super().__init__()
        self.use_state = state
        self.input = nn.Linear(2*d+1+(17 if state else 0), 32)
        self.encoder = encoder()
        self.output = nn.Linear(32, 4)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.output.weight); nn.init.zeros_(self.output.bias)

    def features(self, z, offset, c):
        dz = torch.cat([z[:, 1:]-z[:, :-1], torch.zeros_like(z[:, :1])], 1)
        items = [z, dz, offset[..., None]/6]
        if self.use_state:
            items.append(c[:, None].expand(-1, 8, -1))
        hidden = self.encoder(self.input(torch.cat(items, -1)))
        return (hidden[:, :-1]+hidden[:, 1:])/2

    def forward(self, z, offset, c):
        return self.output(self.features(z, offset, c))


class Teacher(nn.Module):
    """Posterior sees labels only in training_loss; forward inference never calls it."""
    def __init__(self, d):
        super().__init__()
        self.input = nn.Linear(d+2, 32)
        self.encoder = encoder()
        self.output = nn.Linear(32, 8)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.output.weight); nn.init.zeros_(self.output.bias)

    def forward(self, change, latent_change, offset):
        clock = torch.arange(1, 25, device=change.device, dtype=change.dtype)/24
        x = torch.cat([change[..., None], latent_change,
                       clock[None, :, None].expand(len(change), -1, -1)], -1)
        hidden = self.encoder(self.input(x))
        weights = kernels(offset).transpose(1, 2)
        weights = weights/weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
        mu, logvar = self.output(weights@hidden).chunk(2, -1)
        logvar = logvar.clamp(-6, 2)
        # Posterior prior N(0, 0.1^2). KL is applied to standardized parameters.
        sample = 0.1*(mu+torch.exp(0.5*logvar)*torch.randn_like(mu))
        kl = 0.5*(mu.square()+logvar.exp()-1-logvar).mean()
        return sample, kl


def stateless_noise(key, count, seed, dtype):
    """Batch/order invariant evaluation noise, with no change to the training RNG."""
    s = torch.arange(count, device=key.device)[None, :, None, None]
    j = torch.arange(28, device=key.device).reshape(1, 1, 7, 4)
    m = 2147483647
    x = (key[:, None, None, None]*104729+s*13007+j*7919+seed*15485863+1) % m
    x = ((x ^ (x >> 16))*73244475) % m
    x = ((x ^ (x >> 16))*73244475) % m
    x = x ^ (x >> 16)
    u1 = (x.to(torch.float64)+0.5)/m
    # XOR can produce x == m, making u1 slightly > 1 and sqrt(-log(u1)) NaN.
    # Repair only that endpoint; all originally finite evaluation noise is identical.
    u1 = u1.clamp_max((m-0.5)/m)
    x = ((x ^ (x >> 15))*73244475+17) % m
    x = x ^ (x >> 16)
    u2 = (x.to(torch.float64)+0.5)/m
    return (torch.sqrt(-2*torch.log(u1))*torch.cos(2*math.pi*u2)).to(dtype)


class Flow(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.input = nn.Linear(32+4+1, 32)
        self.encoder = encoder()
        self.output = nn.Linear(32, 4)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.output.weight); nn.init.zeros_(self.output.bias)

    def forward(self, x, t, context):
        clock = t.reshape(-1, 1, 1).expand(-1, 7, 1)
        return self.output(self.encoder(self.input(torch.cat([x, context, clock], -1))))

    def sample(self, noise, context, steps=8):
        n, count = noise.shape[:2]
        x = noise.reshape(n*count, 7, 4)
        context = context[:, None].expand(-1, count, -1, -1).reshape(n*count, 7, 32)
        for i in range(steps):
            t = x.new_full((len(x),), i/steps)
            x = x+self(x, t, context)/steps
        return x.reshape(n, count, 7, 4)
```

## SOURCE: project/runs/daily_state_representation_20261116_v2/code/train.py

from settings import *
from data import SharedData
from model import SharedModel,configure,seed_all
from diagnostics import Diagnostics
import torch,numpy as np,time,socket,argparse,traceback,signal

class YieldJob(Exception):pass
def save_torch(p,x):
    tmp=p.with_suffix('.tmp');torch.save(x,tmp);os.replace(tmp,p)
def requested(out):
    if (out/'YIELD_REQUEST.json').exists():raise YieldJob()
def resolve(spec):
    s=dict(spec)
    if s.get('representation')=='SELECTED':
        p=ASSETS/'selected_candidate.json';selected=json.loads(p.read_text())
        s['representation']=selected['representation'];s['candidate_selection_sha256']=sha(p)
    if 'config_from' in s:
        p=ASSETS/'selected'/f'{s["config_from"]}.json';sel=json.loads(p.read_text());s['config']=sel['config'];s['selection_sha256']=sha(p)
    return s

@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model,data,batch_size,device,out=None,pred_path=None,diagnostics=False):
    model.eval();nf=data.farms;ab=np.zeros((nf,24));sq=ab.copy();counts=np.zeros(nf);hi_ab=ab.copy();hi_sq=ab.copy();hi_count=np.zeros(nf)
    diagnostic=Diagnostics() if diagnostics else None
    pred=None
    if pred_path is not None:pred=np.lib.format.open_memmap(pred_path,mode='w+',dtype='f4',shape=(len(data.rows),nf,24))
    for st in range(0,data.n,batch_size):
        if out is not None:requested(out)
        inds=np.arange(st,min(st+batch_size,data.n));b,y,si,_=data.batch(inds,device)
        samples=model.prediction_samples(b) if model.generative else None
        p=samples.mean(1) if samples is not None else model(b)
        err=(p-y).cpu().numpy().astype('f8')
        if diagnostic is not None:diagnostic.update(b,y,p,samples)
        if not np.isfinite(err).all():raise FloatingPointError('nonfinite predictions')
        np.add.at(ab,si,abs(err));np.add.at(sq,si,err**2);np.add.at(counts,si,1)
        high=b['h'][:,-1].cpu().numpy()>=.75
        np.add.at(hi_ab,si[high],abs(err[high]));np.add.at(hi_sq,si[high],err[high]**2);np.add.at(hi_count,si[high],1)
        if pred is not None:pred.reshape(-1,24)[inds]=p.cpu().numpy()
    if pred is not None:pred.flush()
    lead_mae=ab/counts[:,None];lead_mse=sq/counts[:,None];mae=lead_mae.mean(1);rmse=np.sqrt(lead_mse.mean(1))
    high_rmse=np.sqrt(hi_sq.sum(1)/np.maximum(1,hi_count*24));valid=hi_count>0
    result=dict(mae=float(mae.mean()),rmse=float(rmse.mean()),per_farm_mae=mae.tolist(),per_farm_rmse=rmse.tolist(),lead_mae=lead_mae.tolist(),lead_rmse=np.sqrt(lead_mse).tolist(),
        high_power_rmse=float(high_rmse[valid].mean()) if valid.any() else None,high_power_per_farm_rmse=high_rmse.tolist(),high_power_counts=hi_count.astype(int).tolist(),counts=counts.astype(int).tolist(),
        horizons={label:dict(mae=float(lead_mae[:,a:b].mean()),rmse=float(np.sqrt(lead_mse[:,a:b].mean(1)).mean())) for label,a,b in [('0_1h',0,4),('1_3h',4,12),('3_6h',12,24)]})
    if diagnostic is not None:result['diagnostics']=diagnostic.result()
    return result

def run(requested_spec,smoke=False,device='cuda:0',max_batches=None):
    configure();spec=resolve(requested_spec);seed_all(spec['seed']);out=(RUN/'smoke_jobs' if smoke else JOBS)/spec['id'];out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    if not smoke and (out/'SUCCESS.json').exists():return
    atomic_json(out/'spec.json',spec)
    if spec['family']=='B0' and spec['fold']>=0:
        f=np.load(ASSETS/f'fold{spec["fold"]}.npz');train=SharedData('train',spec,f['train']);valid=SharedData('train',spec,f['valid'])
    else:train=SharedData('train',spec);valid=SharedData('val',spec)
    if smoke:
        train.rows=train.rows[:32];train.n=len(train.rows)*train.farms;valid.rows=valid.rows[:16];valid.n=len(valid.rows)*valid.farms
    model=SharedModel(spec).to(device);opt=torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(),lr=spec['config']['lr'],weight_decay=spec['config']['weight_decay'])
    epoch=0;cursor=0;history=[];best=float('inf');bad=0;loss_sum=0.;seen=0;active_seconds=0.;micro=128 if model.generative else 512
    ap=out/'adaptive_microbatch.json'
    if ap.exists():micro=int(json.loads(ap.read_text())['microbatch'])
    epoch_started=time.monotonic();epoch_elapsed_prior=0.
    def checkpoint():
        return dict(spec=spec,model=model.state_dict(),optimizer=opt.state_dict(),epoch=epoch,cursor=cursor,history=history,best=best,bad=bad,loss_sum=loss_sum,seen=seen,active_seconds=active_seconds,
            epoch_elapsed=epoch_elapsed_prior+time.monotonic()-epoch_started,rng=torch.get_rng_state(),cuda_rng=torch.cuda.get_rng_state_all() if device.startswith('cuda') else None)
    if (out/'latest.pt').exists():
        ck=torch.load(out/'latest.pt',map_location=device,weights_only=False);assert ck['spec']==spec;model.load_state_dict(ck['model']);opt.load_state_dict(ck['optimizer'])
        epoch=ck['epoch'];cursor=ck['cursor'];history=ck['history'];best=ck['best'];bad=ck['bad'];loss_sum=ck['loss_sum'];seen=ck['seen'];active_seconds=ck['active_seconds'];epoch_elapsed_prior=ck['epoch_elapsed']
        torch.set_rng_state(ck['rng'].cpu())
        if ck['cuda_rng'] is not None:torch.cuda.set_rng_state_all([x.cpu() for x in ck['cuda_rng']])
    epochs=1 if smoke else spec['epochs'];start=time.monotonic();chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0;steps_done=0;last_save=time.monotonic()
    if not (out/'latest.pt').exists():save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint())
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM,lambda *_:atomic_json(out/'YIELD_REQUEST.json',dict(reason='signal')))
    try:
        while epoch<epochs and bad<spec['patience']:
            model.train();order=np.random.default_rng(spec['seed']*100000+epoch).permutation(train.n)
            while cursor<train.n:
                requested(out);end=min(cursor+512,train.n);ids=order[cursor:end];opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True);step_loss=0.
                for j in range(0,len(ids),micro):
                    ib=ids[j:j+micro];b,y,_,_=train.batch(ib,device);loss=model.training_loss(b,y)
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss):raise FloatingPointError('nonfinite training loss')
                    (loss*len(ib)/len(ids)).backward();step_loss+=float(loss.detach())*len(ib)
                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(),1.0,error_if_nonfinite=True);opt.step();loss_sum+=step_loss;seen+=len(ids);cursor=end;steps_done+=1;chunk_steps+=1
                if chunk_steps>=25 or cursor==train.n:
                    elapsed=time.monotonic()-chunk_start;active_seconds+=elapsed
                    atomic_json(out/'progress.json',dict(timestamp=now(),pid=os.getpid(),host=socket.gethostname(),epoch=epoch+1,step=cursor//512,total_steps=(train.n+511)//512,completed_steps=steps_done,chunk_steps=chunk_steps,seconds_per_step=elapsed/chunk_steps,loss=loss_sum/max(seen,1),best_mae=None if best==float('inf') else best,microbatch=micro,gpu_peak_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated()/2**20 if device.startswith('cuda') else 0,active_seconds=active_seconds))
                    chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0
                if time.monotonic()-last_save>120:save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint());last_save=time.monotonic()
                if max_batches and steps_done>=max_batches:raise YieldJob()
            vm=evaluate(model,valid,max(micro,2*micro),device,out)
            improved=vm['mae']<best
            if improved:
                best=vm['mae'];bad=0;save_torch(out/'best.pt',dict(model=model.state_dict(),spec=spec,epoch=epoch+1,validation=vm,normalization_sha256=sha(ASSETS/'normalization.json'),cohort_sha256=sha(ASSETS/'COHORT.json')))
            else:bad+=1
            row=dict(timestamp=now(),epoch=epoch+1,seconds=epoch_elapsed_prior+time.monotonic()-epoch_started,train_objective=loss_sum/max(seen,1),validation_mae=vm['mae'],validation_rmse=vm['rmse'],best_mae=best)
            history.append(row);atomic_json(out/'history.json',history);print(json.dumps(row),flush=True)
            epoch+=1;cursor=0;loss_sum=0.;seen=0;epoch_elapsed_prior=0.;epoch_started=time.monotonic();save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint());last_save=time.monotonic();chunk_start=time.monotonic();chunk_steps=0
        ck=torch.load(out/'best.pt',map_location=device,weights_only=False);model.load_state_dict(ck['model'])
        evaluation=valid;predict_path=None
        if spec['family']=='B0' and not smoke:
            if spec['fold']>=0:evaluation=SharedData('train',spec,np.load(ASSETS/f'fold{spec["fold"]}.npz')['heldout'])
            predict_path=out/'reference_prediction.npy'
        final=evaluate(model,evaluation,2*micro,device,out,predict_path,diagnostics=True)
        result=dict(timestamp=now(),requested_spec=requested_spec,spec=spec,smoke_only=smoke,evaluation_year=2022 if spec['family']=='B0' and spec['fold']>=0 else 2023,validation_selection='2022 internal block' if spec['family']=='B0' and spec['fold']>=0 else '2023 macroMAE',checkpoint_epoch=ck['epoch'],checkpoint_sha256=sha(out/'best.pt'),elapsed_this_session_seconds=time.monotonic()-start,parameter_count=sum(x.numel() for x in model.parameters()),metrics=final)
        if predict_path is not None:result['reference_sha256']=sha(predict_path)
        atomic_json(out/'metrics.json',result);atomic_json(out/'SUCCESS.json',dict(timestamp=now(),passed=True,smoke_only=smoke));(out/'latest.pt').unlink(missing_ok=True)
    except (YieldJob,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError) as exc:
        opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        if isinstance(exc,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError):
            micro//=2
            if micro<32:raise
            atomic_json(ap,dict(microbatch=micro,effective_batch=512))
        else:save_torch(out/'latest.pt',checkpoint())
        # OOM rolls back to the last stable checkpoint, including optimizer/RNG;
        # never persist a possibly partial Adam update.
        atomic_json(out/'YIELDED.json',dict(timestamp=now(),reason='cuda_oom' if isinstance(exc,torch.cuda.OutOfMemoryError) else 'cooperative',epoch=epoch,cursor=cursor));return 75
    return 0

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--job-id',required=True);p.add_argument('--smoke',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--device',default='cuda:0');p.add_argument('--max-batches',type=int);a=p.parse_args()
    specs={s['id']:s for s in json.loads((RUN/'manifest.json').read_text())};spec=specs[a.job_id]
    try:rc=run(spec,a.smoke,a.device,a.max_batches)
    except Exception:
        out=(RUN/'smoke_jobs' if a.smoke else JOBS)/spec['id'];atomic_json(out/'FAILURE.json',dict(timestamp=now(),error=traceback.format_exc()));raise
    raise SystemExit(rc)
```
